
===== src/app/page.tsx =====
import { SchoolsMetricsSection } from "@/components/dashboard/schools-metrics";
import { UsersMetricsSection } from "@/components/dashboard/users-metrics";
import { AcademyMetricsSection } from "@/components/dashboard/academy-metrics";
import { AssessmentMetricsSection } from "@/components/dashboard/assessment-metrics";
import { DashboardView } from "@/components/dashboard/dashboard-view";
import { RoleDashboard } from "@/components/dashboard/role-dashboard";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/auth/session";

export const metadata = {
  title: "لوحة المعلومات",
};

/**
 * Role-aware dashboard (server component). The session user comes from
 * the DB — never from the client:
 *   SUPER_ADMIN                -> platform dashboard + schools + users + academy metrics
 *   DEVELOPMENT_OFFICE_MANAGER -> same shell + THEIR school's metrics
 *                                 (schoolId from the session only)
 *   deputy / supervisor / teacher -> scoped role dashboard
 *
 * Phase 1 mock sections keep their honest "بيانات تجريبية" labels.
 */
export default async function HomePage() {
  const user = await requireUser();

  if (user.role === "SUPER_ADMIN") {
    return (
      <DashboardView
        schoolsSlot={
          <div className="space-y-6">
            <SchoolsMetricsSection />
            <UsersMetricsSection />
            <AcademyMetricsSection />
            <AssessmentMetricsSection />
          </div>
        }
      />
    );
  }

  if (user.role === "DEVELOPMENT_OFFICE_MANAGER" && user.schoolId) {
    return (
      <DashboardView
        schoolsSlot={
          <div className="space-y-6">
            <SchoolsMetricsSection schoolId={user.schoolId} />
            <UsersMetricsSection schoolId={user.schoolId} />
            <AcademyMetricsSection schoolId={user.schoolId} />
            <AssessmentMetricsSection schoolId={user.schoolId} />
          </div>
        }
      />
    );
  }

  return <RoleDashboard user={user} />;
}

===== src/lib/types.ts =====
import type { LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Platform identity & multi-school readiness                          */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

/** Educational stages a school can serve (used by future school config). */
export type EducationalStage =
  | "المرحلة الابتدائية"
  | "المرحلة المتوسطة"
  | "المرحلة الثانوية";

/**
 * Identity of a single school. In later phases each school tenant will
 * provide its own identity instead of relying on the platform defaults.
 */
export interface SchoolIdentity {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  logoUrl?: string;
  branches: string[];
  stages: EducationalStage[];
}

/**
 * Branding shown in the application shell. The platform ships with
 * default branding; per-school overrides arrive with multi-tenancy.
 */
export interface PlatformBranding {
  productName: string;
  subtitle: string;
  shortName: string;
  logoUrl?: string;
  /** The school workspace currently active. Undefined until tenant config lands. */
  currentSchool?: SchoolIdentity;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Navigation                                                          */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

export interface NavItem {
  /** Stable identifier used for active-state tracking. */
  id: string;
  label: string;
  /** Final route once the module is implemented in a later phase. */
  href: string;
  /** Short contextual description shown on the module page header. */
  description: string;
  icon: LucideIcon;
  /** Features planned for the module — surfaced honestly on placeholder pages. */
  plannedFeatures: string[];
  /** Optional small badge (e.g. "قريباً") rendered next to the label. */
  badge?: string;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* User & notifications                                                */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

export interface CurrentUser {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  role: string;
  email: string;
  initials: string;
}

export interface AppNotification {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  time: string;
  read: boolean;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Dashboard                                                           */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

export type TrendDirection = "up" | "down" | "flat";

export interface StatTrend {
  value: number;
  direction: TrendDirection;
  label: string;
}

export type AccentColor = "primary" | "info" | "success" | "warning";

export interface OverviewStat {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  value: number;
  suffix?: string;
  icon: LucideIcon;
  accent: AccentColor;
  trend: StatTrend;
  /** Nav item id the card navigates to. */
  targetModuleId: string;
}

export interface TrainingProgressPoint {
  month: string;
  /** Completed training hours for the month. */
  completed: number;
  /** Target training hours for the month. */
  target: number;
}

export interface StageDistributionPoint {
  stage: EducationalStage;
  count: number;
}

export type ActivityType =
  | "completed"
  | "enrolled"
  | "certificate"
  | "assessment"
  | "followup";

export interface RecentActivity {
  id: string;
  type: ActivityType;
  title: string;
  actor: string;
  time: string;
}

export type TrainingEventType = "workshop" | "course" | "meeting";

export type EventStatus = "upcoming" | "ongoing" | "completed";

export interface UpcomingEvent {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  day: string;
  date: string;
  time: string;
  location: string;
  type: TrainingEventType;
  status: EventStatus;
  seatsLeft?: number;
}

export interface QuickAction {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  description: string;
  icon: LucideIcon;
  /** Nav item id the action navigates to. */
  targetModuleId: string;
}

===== src/config/branding.ts =====
import type { PlatformBranding } from "@/lib/types";

/**
 * Default platform branding.
 *
 * The platform is designed as a multi-school system: every school will
 * eventually carry its own name, logo, branches and educational stages.
 * The object below is the fallback identity used until per-school
 * configuration is introduced in a later phase. No component should
 * hardcode a school name — always read from this config (or, in the
 * future, from the active tenant context).
 */
export const defaultBranding: PlatformBranding = {
  productName: "منارة قرطبة",
  subtitle: "منصة التنمية المهنية للمعلمين",
  shortName: "منارة",
  logoUrl: undefined,
  currentSchool: undefined,
};

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\assessments\assessment-filters.tsx =====
import { Search } from "lucide-react";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import { ASSESSMENT_STATUS_LABELS, ASSESSMENT_STATUSES } from "@/config/assessment";

interface AssessmentFiltersProps {
  action: string;
  q?: string;
  status?: string;
}

/**
 * GET-form filters for the management directory — server-rendered,
 * no client JS: submitting navigates with the query string.
 */
export function AssessmentFilters({ action, q, status }: AssessmentFiltersProps) {
  return (
    <form
      action={action}
      method="get"
      className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-card p-4"
    >
      <div className="min-w-52 flex-1 space-y-1.5">
        <label
          htmlFor="assessments-q"
          className="block text-xs font-bold text-muted-foreground"
        >
          بحث بالعنوان
        </label>
        <Input
          id="assessments-q"
          name="q"
          defaultValue={q ?? ""}
          placeholder="اكتب جزءًا من عنوان الاختبار…"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="w-44 space-y-1.5">
        <label
          htmlFor="assessments-status"
          className="block text-xs font-bold text-muted-foreground"
        >
          الحالة
        </label>
        <Select name="status" defaultValue={status || "all"}>
          <SelectTrigger id="assessments-status">
            <SelectValue placeholder="كل الحالات" />
          </SelectTrigger>
          <SelectContent>
            <SelectItem value="all">كل الحالات</SelectItem>
            {ASSESSMENT_STATUSES.map((value) => (
              <SelectItem key={value} value={value}>
                {ASSESSMENT_STATUS_LABELS[value]}
              </SelectItem>
            ))}
          </SelectContent>
        </Select>
      </div>

      <Button type="submit" variant="outline">
        <Search className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
        تطبيق
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\assessments\assessment-form.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { Save } from "lucide-react";

import {
  createAssessmentAction,
  updateAssessmentAction,
  type AssessmentFormState,
} from "@/app/actions/assessment";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";

export interface CourseOption {
  id: string;
  titleAr: string;
  schoolId: string | null;
}

export interface SchoolOption {
  id: string;
  nameAr: string;
}

export interface AssessmentFormValues {
  titleAr: string;
  titleEn: string;
  instructionsAr: string;
  instructionsEn: string;
  courseId: string;
  passingScore: string;
  timeLimitMinutes: string;
  attemptLimit: string;
  availableFrom: string;
  availableUntil: string;
}

interface AssessmentFormProps {
  mode: "create" | "edit";
  assessmentId?: string;
  initialValues?: Partial<AssessmentFormValues>;
  courseOptions: CourseOption[];
  schoolOptions: SchoolOption[];
  isSuperAdmin: boolean;
  /** Tenancy (scope + school) is fixed after creation. */
  lockedTenancy?: { scope: "platform" | "school"; schoolName: string | null };
}

/** Convert an ISO/Date string to the datetime-local input format. */
function toDatetimeLocal(value: string | undefined): string {
  if (!value) return "";
  const date = new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "";
  const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getDate())}T${pad(date.getHours())}:${pad(date.getMinutes())}`;
}

const emptyValues: AssessmentFormValues = {
  titleAr: "",
  titleEn: "",
  instructionsAr: "",
  instructionsEn: "",
  courseId: "",
  passingScore: "",
  timeLimitMinutes: "",
  attemptLimit: "",
  availableFrom: "",
  availableUntil: "",
};

/**
 * Assessment settings form (create + edit). Tenancy fields are rendered
 * read-only in edit mode — the server ignores them there anyway.
 */
export function AssessmentForm({
  mode,
  assessmentId,
  initialValues,
  courseOptions,
  schoolOptions,
  isSuperAdmin,
  lockedTenancy,
}: AssessmentFormProps) {
  const [state, formAction] = React.useActionState<
    AssessmentFormState | null,
    FormData
  >(mode === "create" ? createAssessmentAction : updateAssessmentAction, null);

  const values = { ...emptyValues, ...initialValues };
  const [scope, setScope] = React.useState<"platform" | "school">(
    lockedTenancy?.scope ?? "school"
  );

  return (
    <form action={formAction} className="space-y-5" noValidate>
      {mode === "edit" && assessmentId && (
        <input type="hidden" name="assessmentId" value={assessmentId} />
      )}

      {state?.message && (
        <Alert
          variant={state.ok ? "default" : "destructive"}
          role={state.ok ? "status" : "alert"}
        >
          <AlertDescription>{state.message}</AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      )}

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:col-span-2">
          <Label htmlFor="titleAr">عنوان الاختبار (بالعربية) *</Label>
          <Input
            id="titleAr"
            name="titleAr"
            defaultValue={values.titleAr}
            required
            aria-invalid={Boolean(state?.fieldErrors?.titleAr)}
          />
          {state?.fieldErrors?.titleAr && (
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
              {state.fieldErrors.titleAr}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:col-span-2">
          <Label htmlFor="titleEn">العنوان بالإنجليزية (اختياري)</Label>
          <Input
            id="titleEn"
            name="titleEn"
            dir="ltr"
            defaultValue={values.titleEn}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:col-span-2">
          <Label htmlFor="instructionsAr">تعليمات الاختبار (بالعربية) *</Label>
          <Textarea
            id="instructionsAr"
            name="instructionsAr"
            rows={4}
            defaultValue={values.instructionsAr}
            required
            aria-invalid={Boolean(state?.fieldErrors?.instructionsAr)}
          />
          {state?.fieldErrors?.instructionsAr && (
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
              {state.fieldErrors.instructionsAr}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:col-span-2">
          <Label htmlFor="instructionsEn">التعليمات بالإنجليزية (اختياري)</Label>
          <Textarea
            id="instructionsEn"
            name="instructionsEn"
            dir="ltr"
            rows={3}
            defaultValue={values.instructionsEn}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Tenancy */}
      {mode === "create" ? (
        isSuperAdmin ? (
          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label>نطاق الاختبار *</Label>
              <Select
                name="scope"
                value={scope}
                onValueChange={(value) =>
                  setScope(value as "platform" | "school")
                }
              >
                <SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectValue />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="platform">منصة مشتركة (لكل المدارس)</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="school">مدرسة محددة</SelectItem>
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
            {scope === "school" && (
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label htmlFor="schoolId">المدرسة المالكة *</Label>
                <Select name="schoolId" defaultValue="none">
                  <SelectTrigger id="schoolId">
                    <SelectValue placeholder="اختر المدرسة" />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent>
                    <SelectItem value="none">اختر المدرسة</SelectItem>
                    {schoolOptions.map((school) => (
                      <SelectItem key={school.id} value={school.id}>
                        {school.nameAr}
                      </SelectItem>
                    ))}
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
                {state?.fieldErrors?.schoolId && (
                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
                    {state.fieldErrors.schoolId}
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <input type="hidden" name="scope" value="school" />
        )
      ) : (
        lockedTenancy && (
          <p className="rounded-lg border bg-muted/40 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            النطاق:{" "}
            {lockedTenancy.scope === "platform"
              ? "منصة مشتركة"
              : lockedTenancy.schoolName ?? "مدرسة"}{" "}
            — لا يمكن تغييره بعد الإنشاء.
          </p>
        )
      )}

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="courseId">الدورة المرتبطة (اختياري)</Label>
          <Select
            name="courseId"
            defaultValue={values.courseId || "none"}
          >
            <SelectTrigger id="courseId">
              <SelectValue placeholder="بدون ربط بدورة" />
            </SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>
              <SelectItem value="none">بدون ربط بدورة</SelectItem>
              {courseOptions.map((course) => (
                <SelectItem key={course.id} value={course.id}>
                  {course.titleAr}
                </SelectItem>
              ))}
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
            عند الربط: لا يستطيع المعلم بدء الاختبار إلا إذا كان مسجلًا في الدورة.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="passingScore">درجة النجاح (نقاط) *</Label>
          <Input
            id="passingScore"
            name="passingScore"
            type="number"
            min={1}
            dir="ltr"
            defaultValue={values.passingScore}
            required
            aria-invalid={Boolean(state?.fieldErrors?.passingScore)}
          />
          {state?.fieldErrors?.passingScore && (
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
              {state.fieldErrors.passingScore}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="timeLimitMinutes">مدة الاختبار بالدقائق (اختياري)</Label>
          <Input
            id="timeLimitMinutes"
            name="timeLimitMinutes"
            type="number"
            min={5}
            max={300}
            dir="ltr"
            defaultValue={values.timeLimitMinutes}
            placeholder="بدون تحديد = اختبار غير مؤقت"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="attemptLimit">حد المحاولات (اختياري)</Label>
          <Input
            id="attemptLimit"
            name="attemptLimit"
            type="number"
            min={1}
            max={10}
            dir="ltr"
            defaultValue={values.attemptLimit}
            placeholder="بدون تحديد = محاولات غير محدودة"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="availableFrom">بداية التوافر (اختياري)</Label>
          <Input
            id="availableFrom"
            name="availableFrom"
            type="datetime-local"
            dir="ltr"
            defaultValue={toDatetimeLocal(values.availableFrom)}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="availableUntil">نهاية التوافر (اختياري)</Label>
          <Input
            id="availableUntil"
            name="availableUntil"
            type="datetime-local"
            dir="ltr"
            defaultValue={toDatetimeLocal(values.availableUntil)}
          />
          {state?.fieldErrors?.availableUntil && (
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
              {state.fieldErrors.availableUntil}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex justify-end">
        <Button type="submit">
          <Save className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          {mode === "create" ? "إنشاء الاختبار (يبدأ كمسودة)" : "حفظ الإعدادات"}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\assessments\assessment-publish-controls.tsx =====
import { Archive, Rocket, Undo2 } from "lucide-react";

import {
  setAssessmentStatusAction,
} from "@/app/actions/assessment";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { assessmentStatusLabel } from "@/config/assessment";

interface AssessmentPublishControlsProps {
  assessmentId: string;
  status: string;
}

/**
 * Lifecycle controls for one assessment (DRAFT -> PUBLISHED ->
 * ARCHIVED). The publish guard (questions + passing score) lives in the
 * service layer; failures surface through the editor notice area.
 */
export function AssessmentPublishControls({
  assessmentId,
  status,
}: AssessmentPublishControlsProps) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
      {status === "DRAFT" && (
        <form action={setAssessmentStatusAction}>
          <input type="hidden" name="assessmentId" value={assessmentId} />
          <input type="hidden" name="status" value="PUBLISHED" />
          <Button type="submit" size="sm">
            <Rocket className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            نشر الاختبار
          </Button>
        </form>
      )}
      {status === "PUBLISHED" && (
        <>
          <form action={setAssessmentStatusAction}>
            <input type="hidden" name="assessmentId" value={assessmentId} />
            <input type="hidden" name="status" value="DRAFT" />
            <Button type="submit" size="sm" variant="outline">
              <Undo2 className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              إرجاع إلى مسودة
            </Button>
          </form>
          <form action={setAssessmentStatusAction}>
            <input type="hidden" name="assessmentId" value={assessmentId} />
            <input type="hidden" name="status" value="ARCHIVED" />
            <Button
              type="submit"
              size="sm"
              variant="outline"
              className="text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10"
            >
              <Archive className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              أرشفة
            </Button>
          </form>
        </>
      )}
      {status === "ARCHIVED" && (
        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-muted-foreground">
          الحالة الحالية: {assessmentStatusLabel(status)} — لا يمكن التعديل بعد الأرشفة.
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\assessments\attempt-review-form.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { ClipboardCheck } from "lucide-react";

import { reviewAttemptAction, type AssessmentFormState } from "@/app/actions/assessment";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";

export interface ReviewShortAnswer {
  questionId: string;
  questionText: string;
  points: number;
  answerText: string;
  modelAnswer: string | null;
}

interface AttemptReviewFormProps {
  attemptId: string;
  shortAnswers: ReviewShortAnswer[];
}

/**
 * Manual grading panel for a NEEDS_REVIEW attempt: shows the teacher's
 * short answers plus the optional model answer, collects per-question
 * points (0..question max) and submits the review — the service layer
 * finalizes the attempt atomically and keeps finalized rows immutable.
 */
export function AttemptReviewForm({
  attemptId,
  shortAnswers,
}: AttemptReviewFormProps) {
  const [state, formAction] = React.useActionState<
    AssessmentFormState | null,
    FormData
  >(reviewAttemptAction, null);

  const [scores, setScores] = React.useState<Record<string, string>>(() =>
    Object.fromEntries(shortAnswers.map((answer) => [answer.questionId, "0"]))
  );

  const scoresJson = JSON.stringify(
    shortAnswers.map((answer) => ({
      questionId: answer.questionId,
      points: Number(scores[answer.questionId] ?? "0"),
    }))
  );

  return (
    <form action={formAction} className="space-y-3" noValidate>
      <input type="hidden" name="attemptId" value={attemptId} />
      <input type="hidden" name="scoresJson" value={scoresJson} />

      {state?.message && (
        <Alert
          variant={state.ok ? "default" : "destructive"}
          role={state.ok ? "status" : "alert"}
        >
          <AlertDescription>{state.message}</AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      )}

      <ul className="space-y-3" role="list">
        {shortAnswers.map((answer, index) => (
          <li key={answer.questionId} className="rounded-lg border bg-background/60 p-3">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-foreground">
              {index + 1}. {answer.questionText}
            </p>
            <p className="mt-1.5 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-md bg-muted/50 px-3 py-2 text-xs leading-relaxed">
              {answer.answerText || <span className="text-muted-foreground">— بدون إجابة نصية —</span>}
            </p>
            {answer.modelAnswer && (
              <p className="mt-1.5 rounded-md bg-info-soft px-3 py-2 text-[11px] text-info">
                إرشاد المراجع: {answer.modelAnswer}
              </p>
            )}
            <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-2">
              <label
                htmlFor={`score-${attemptId}-${answer.questionId}`}
                className="text-[11px] font-bold text-muted-foreground"
              >
                الدرجة الممنوحة (0–{answer.points}):
              </label>
              <Input
                id={`score-${attemptId}-${answer.questionId}`}
                type="number"
                min={0}
                max={answer.points}
                dir="ltr"
                value={scores[answer.questionId] ?? "0"}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setScores((current) => ({
                    ...current,
                    [answer.questionId]: event.target.value,
                  }))
                }
                className="h-8 w-24"
              />
            </div>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>

      <div className="flex justify-end">
        <Button type="submit" size="sm">
          <ClipboardCheck className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          اعتماد المراجعة وحساب النتيجة
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\assessments\question-builder.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import {
  ArrowDown,
  ArrowUp,
  CheckCircle2,
  Circle,
  ListPlus,
  Pencil,
  Plus,
  Save,
  Trash2,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";

import {
  addQuestionAction,
  deleteQuestionAction,
  moveQuestionAction,
  updateQuestionAction,
  type AssessmentFormState,
} from "@/app/actions/assessment";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import {
  OPTIONS_MAX,
  QUESTION_TYPES,
  QUESTION_TYPE_LABELS,
  TRUE_FALSE_LABELS,
  questionTypeLabel,
  type QuestionType,
} from "@/config/assessment";
import type { ManageQuestionRow, QuestionOption } from "@/lib/assessment/service";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Question builder — add / edit / remove / reorder questions with      */
/* type-conditional controls. Correct-answer configuration is serialized */
/* to the server action; option ids are generated SERVER-SIDE only.     */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

interface QuestionBuilderProps {
  assessmentId: string;
  status: string;
  questions: ManageQuestionRow[];
}

export function QuestionBuilder({
  assessmentId,
  status,
  questions,
}: QuestionBuilderProps) {
  const editable = status !== "ARCHIVED";
  const [adding, setAdding] = React.useState(false);
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = React.useState<string | null>(null);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {editable && !adding && (
        <div>
          <Button type="button" onClick={() => setAdding(true)} size="sm">
            <Plus className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            إضافة سؤال
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}

      {editable && adding && (
        <div className="rounded-xl border bg-muted/20 p-4">
          <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold">سؤال جديد</h3>
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="ghost"
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => setAdding(false)}
            >
              <X className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              إلغاء
            </Button>
          </div>
          <QuestionForm
            mode="create"
            assessmentId={assessmentId}
            onDone={() => setAdding(false)}
          />
        </div>
      )}

      {questions.length === 0 && !adding ? (
        <p className="rounded-lg border border-dashed bg-muted/30 px-4 py-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          لا توجد أسئلة بعد — أضف السؤال الأول لبدء بناء الاختبار.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <ol className="space-y-3" role="list" aria-label="أسئلة الاختبار">
          {questions.map((question, index) => (
            <li key={question.id} className="rounded-xl border bg-card p-4">
              {editable && editingId === question.id ? (
                <div>
                  <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-bold">تعديل السؤال {index + 1}</h3>
                    <Button
                      type="button"
                      variant="ghost"
                      size="sm"
                      onClick={() => setEditingId(null)}
                    >
                      <X className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                      إلغاء
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                  <QuestionForm
                    mode="edit"
                    assessmentId={assessmentId}
                    question={question}
                    onDone={() => setEditingId(null)}
                  />
                </div>
              ) : (
                <QuestionCard
                  assessmentId={assessmentId}
                  question={question}
                  index={index}
                  total={questions.length}
                  editable={editable}
                  onEdit={() => setEditingId(question.id)}
                />
              )}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------ Card ------------------------------- */

function QuestionCard({
  assessmentId,
  question,
  index,
  total,
  editable,
  onEdit,
}: {
  assessmentId: string;
  question: ManageQuestionRow;
  index: number;
  total: number;
  editable: boolean;
  onEdit: () => void;
}) {
  const correctIds = correctOptionIds(question);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground">
            {index + 1}. {question.textAr}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
            <span className="rounded-full bg-muted px-2 py-0.5 font-bold">
              {questionTypeLabel(question.type)}
            </span>
            <span>{question.points} نقاط</span>
            {question.isRequired && <span>إجابته إلزامية</span>}
          </p>
        </div>
        {editable && (
          <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5">
            <form action={moveQuestionAction}>
              <input type="hidden" name="assessmentId" value={assessmentId} />
              <input type="hidden" name="questionId" value={question.id} />
              <input type="hidden" name="direction" value="up" />
              <Button
                type="submit"
                variant="outline"
                size="icon"
                className="size-8"
                disabled={index === 0}
                aria-label="نقل لأعلى"
              >
                <ArrowUp className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
              </Button>
            </form>
            <form action={moveQuestionAction}>
              <input type="hidden" name="assessmentId" value={assessmentId} />
              <input type="hidden" name="questionId" value={question.id} />
              <input type="hidden" name="direction" value="down" />
              <Button
                type="submit"
                variant="outline"
                size="icon"
                className="size-8"
                disabled={index === total - 1}
                aria-label="نقل لأسفل"
              >
                <ArrowDown className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
              </Button>
            </form>
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              size="sm"
              onClick={onEdit}
            >
              <Pencil className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
              تعديل
            </Button>
            <form action={deleteQuestionAction}>
              <input type="hidden" name="assessmentId" value={assessmentId} />
              <input type="hidden" name="questionId" value={question.id} />
              <Button
                type="submit"
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                className="text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10"
              >
                <Trash2 className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                حذف
              </Button>
            </form>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {question.type === "TRUE_FALSE" ? (
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          الإجابة الصحيحة:{" "}
          <span className="font-bold text-success">
            {question.answerKey && "value" in question.answerKey
              ? question.answerKey.value
                ? TRUE_FALSE_LABELS.true
                : TRUE_FALSE_LABELS.false
              : "—"}
          </span>
        </p>
      ) : question.type === "SHORT_ANSWER" ? (
        <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          <p className="font-bold text-info">تدرج يدويًا بعد التسليم</p>
          {question.answerKey && "modelAnswer" in question.answerKey && question.answerKey.modelAnswer && (
            <p className="mt-1">
              الإجابة النموذجية: {question.answerKey.modelAnswer}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <ul className="space-y-1.5" role="list">
          {question.options.map((option) => {
            const isCorrect = correctIds.includes(option.id);
            return (
              <li
                key={option.id}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs"
              >
                {isCorrect ? (
                  <CheckCircle2
                    className="size-4 shrink-0 text-success"
                    aria-hidden="true"
                  />
                ) : (
                  <Circle
                    className="size-4 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground/40"
                    aria-hidden="true"
                  />
                )}
                <span className={isCorrect ? "font-bold text-success" : ""}>
                  {option.textAr}
                </span>
                {isCorrect && (
                  <span className="rounded-full bg-success-soft px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-success">
                    الإجابة الصحيحة
                  </span>
                )}
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      )}

      {question.explanation && (
        <p className="rounded-lg bg-info-soft px-3 py-2 text-xs text-info">
          تغذية راجعة: {question.explanation}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function correctOptionIds(question: ManageQuestionRow): string[] {
  const key = question.answerKey;
  if (!key) return [];
  if ("optionId" in key) return [key.optionId];
  if ("optionIds" in key) return key.optionIds;
  return [];
}

/* ------------------------------ Form ------------------------------- */

interface QuestionFormProps {
  mode: "create" | "edit";
  assessmentId: string;
  question?: ManageQuestionRow;
  onDone: () => void;
}

function QuestionForm({ mode, assessmentId, question, onDone }: QuestionFormProps) {
  const [addState, addFormAction] = React.useActionState<
    AssessmentFormState | null,
    FormData
  >(addQuestionAction, null);
  const [editState, editFormAction] = React.useActionState<
    AssessmentFormState | null,
    FormData
  >(updateQuestionAction, null);
  const state = mode === "create" ? addState : editState;

  const [type, setType] = React.useState<QuestionType>(
    (question?.type as QuestionType) ?? "SINGLE_CHOICE"
  );
  const [optionTexts, setOptionTexts] = React.useState<string[]>(() => {
    if (question && question.options.length > 0) {
      return question.options.map((option) => option.textAr);
    }
    return ["", ""];
  });
  const [correctIndexes, setCorrectIndexes] = React.useState<number[]>(() =>
    question ? correctOptionIds(question).map((id) =>
      Math.max(0, question.options.findIndex((option) => option.id === id))
    ) : []
  );
  const [correctValue, setCorrectValue] = React.useState<string>(() => {
    if (question?.answerKey && "value" in question.answerKey) {
      return question.answerKey.value ? "true" : "false";
    }
    return "true";
  });

  const isChoice = type === "SINGLE_CHOICE" || type === "MULTIPLE_CHOICE";

  // Close on success (and reset for the next add).
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (state?.ok) {
      if (mode === "create") {
        setType("SINGLE_CHOICE");
        setOptionTexts(["", ""]);
        setCorrectIndexes([]);
        setCorrectValue("true");
      }
      onDone();
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [state?.ok]);

  function handleTypeChange(next: QuestionType) {
    setType(next);
    if (!question) {
      setOptionTexts(next === "SINGLE_CHOICE" || next === "MULTIPLE_CHOICE" ? ["", ""] : []);
      setCorrectIndexes([]);
    }
  }

  function toggleCorrectIndex(index: number, checked: boolean) {
    if (type === "SINGLE_CHOICE") {
      setCorrectIndexes([index]);
      return;
    }
    setCorrectIndexes((current) =>
      checked
        ? [...current, index].sort()
        : current.filter((value) => value !== index)
    );
  }

  return (
    <form
      action={mode === "create" ? addFormAction : editFormAction}
      className="space-y-4"
      noValidate
    >
      <input type="hidden" name="assessmentId" value={assessmentId} />
      {mode === "edit" && question && (
        <input type="hidden" name="questionId" value={question.id} />
      )}
      <input type="hidden" name="type" value={type} />
      <input
        type="hidden"
        name="optionsJson"
        value={JSON.stringify(
          optionTexts.map((textAr) => ({ textAr: textAr.trim() }))
        )}
      />
      <input
        type="hidden"
        name="correctOptionIndexesJson"
        value={JSON.stringify(correctIndexes)}
      />
      <input type="hidden" name="correctValue" value={correctValue} />

      {state?.message && (
        <Alert variant="destructive" role="alert">
          <AlertDescription>{state.message}</AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      )}

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label>نوع السؤال *</Label>
          <Select value={type} onValueChange={(value) => handleTypeChange(value as QuestionType)}>
            <SelectTrigger>
              <SelectValue />
            </SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>
              {QUESTION_TYPES.map((value) => (
                <SelectItem key={value} value={value}>
                  {QUESTION_TYPE_LABELS[value]}
                </SelectItem>
              ))}
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor={`points-${question?.id ?? "new"}`}>درجة السؤال *</Label>
          <Input
            id={`points-${question?.id ?? "new"}`}
            name="points"
            type="number"
            min={1}
            max={100}
            dir="ltr"
            defaultValue={question?.points ?? 1}
            required
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-end gap-2 pb-2">
          <input
            id={`required-${question?.id ?? "new"}`}
            name="isRequired"
            type="checkbox"
            defaultChecked={question?.isRequired ?? true}
            className="size-4 accent-[#1D5BD6]"
          />
          <Label htmlFor={`required-${question?.id ?? "new"}`} className="text-xs">
            إجابته إلزامية
          </Label>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-1.5">
        <Label htmlFor={`textAr-${question?.id ?? "new"}`}>نص السؤال (بالعربية) *</Label>
        <Textarea
          id={`textAr-${question?.id ?? "new"}`}
          name="textAr"
          rows={2}
          defaultValue={question?.textAr ?? ""}
          required
        />
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-1.5">
        <Label htmlFor={`textEn-${question?.id ?? "new"}`}>
          نص السؤال بالإنجليزية (اختياري)
        </Label>
        <Input
          id={`textEn-${question?.id ?? "new"}`}
          name="textEn"
          dir="ltr"
          defaultValue={question?.textEn ?? ""}
        />
      </div>

      {isChoice && (
        <div className="space-y-2 rounded-lg border bg-background/60 p-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <Label className="text-xs">
              الخيارات * — حدّد {type === "SINGLE_CHOICE" ? "الإجابة الصحيحة الواحدة" : "كل الإجابات الصحيحة"}
            </Label>
            {optionTexts.length < OPTIONS_MAX && (
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                onClick={() => setOptionTexts((current) => [...current, ""])}
              >
                <ListPlus className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                خيار إضافي
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
          {optionTexts.map((text, index) => (
            <div key={index} className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <input
                type={type === "SINGLE_CHOICE" ? "radio" : "checkbox"}
                name={type === "SINGLE_CHOICE" ? "correct-radio" : undefined}
                checked={correctIndexes.includes(index)}
                onChange={(event) => toggleCorrectIndex(index, event.target.checked)}
                aria-label={`الإجابة الصحيحة للخيار ${index + 1}`}
                className="size-4 shrink-0 accent-[#1D5BD6]"
              />
              <Input
                value={text}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setOptionTexts((current) =>
                    current.map((value, i) => (i === index ? event.target.value : value))
                  )
                }
                placeholder={`نص الخيار ${index + 1}`}
                required
                className="flex-1"
              />
              {optionTexts.length > 2 && (
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="ghost"
                  size="icon"
                  className="size-8 shrink-0"
                  aria-label={`حذف الخيار ${index + 1}`}
                  onClick={() => {
                    setOptionTexts((current) => current.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
                    setCorrectIndexes((current) =>
                      current
                        .filter((value) => value !== index)
                        .map((value) => (value > index ? value - 1 : value))
                    );
                  }}
                >
                  <X className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                </Button>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {type === "TRUE_FALSE" && (
        <div className="space-y-1.5 rounded-lg border bg-background/60 p-3">
          <Label className="text-xs">الإجابة الصحيحة *</Label>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            {(["true", "false"] as const).map((value) => (
              <label key={value} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm">
                <input
                  type="radio"
                  name="correct-value-radio"
                  checked={correctValue === value}
                  onChange={() => setCorrectValue(value)}
                  className="size-4 accent-[#1D5BD6]"
                />
                {TRUE_FALSE_LABELS[value]}
              </label>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {type === "SHORT_ANSWER" && (
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor={`model-${question?.id ?? "new"}`}>
            الإجابة النموذجية (مساعدة للمراجع — اختيارية)
          </Label>
          <Textarea
            id={`model-${question?.id ?? "new"}`}
            name="modelAnswer"
            rows={2}
            defaultValue={
              question?.answerKey && "modelAnswer" in question.answerKey
                ? question.answerKey.modelAnswer ?? ""
                : ""
            }
          />
          <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
            أسئلة الإجابة القصيرة تُدرّج يدويًا بعد التسليم — تظهر المحاولة بحالة &quot;بانتظار المراجعة&quot;.
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-1.5">
        <Label htmlFor={`explanation-${question?.id ?? "new"}`}>
          تغذية راجعة تظهر بعد التسليم (اختياري)
        </Label>
        <Textarea
          id={`explanation-${question?.id ?? "new"}`}
          name="explanation"
          rows={2}
          defaultValue={question?.explanation ?? ""}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="flex justify-end">
        <Button type="submit">
          <Save className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          {mode === "create" ? "إضافة السؤال" : "حفظ التعديلات"}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

/** Exported for the editor preview (maps option rows safely). */
export function questionOptionTexts(options: QuestionOption[]): string[] {
  return options.map((option) => option.textAr);
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\assessments\take-assessment.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import {
  AlertTriangle,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  Clock,
  ListChecks,
  Save,
  Send,
} from "lucide-react";

import {
  saveDraftAction,
  submitAttemptAction,
  type AssessmentFormState,
} from "@/app/actions/assessment";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { TRUE_FALSE_LABELS } from "@/config/assessment";
import type { TakeAttemptView } from "@/lib/assessment/service";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Teacher attempt UI: one question per screen, numbered navigation,    */
/* per-type inputs (RTL, keyboard accessible), debounced server-side    */
/* draft autosave, and a countdown for timed assessments.               */
/*                                                                      */
/* SECURITY: the deadline is enforced by the SERVER on submit — this    */
/* timer is a courtesy display only. No answer keys exist in this       */
/* component (they never cross the RSC boundary before submission).     */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

type AnswerEntry = { choice?: string[]; bool?: boolean; text?: string };

interface TakeAssessmentProps {
  view: TakeAttemptView;
}

export function TakeAssessment({ view }: TakeAssessmentProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { attempt, assessment, questions } = view;

  const [answers, setAnswers] = React.useState<Record<string, AnswerEntry>>(
    () => {
      const initial: Record<string, AnswerEntry> = {};
      for (const answer of view.savedAnswers) {
        initial[answer.questionId] = {
          choice: answer.choice,
          bool: answer.bool,
          text: answer.text,
        };
      }
      return initial;
    }
  );
  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = React.useState(0);
  const [showReview, setShowReview] = React.useState(false);
  const [draftSavedAt, setDraftSavedAt] = React.useState<string | null>(null);
  const [remaining, setRemaining] = React.useState<number | null>(null);
  const autoSubmittedRef = React.useRef(false);

  const [submitState, submitFormAction] = React.useActionState<
    AssessmentFormState | null,
    FormData
  >(submitAttemptAction, null);
  const [, saveFormAction] = React.useActionState<
    AssessmentFormState | null,
    FormData
  >(saveDraftAction, null);
  const [isSaving, startSaving] = React.useTransition();

  const answersJson = React.useMemo(
    () =>
      JSON.stringify(
        questions.map((question) => ({
          questionId: question.id,
          ...(answers[question.id] ?? {}),
        }))
      ),
    [answers, questions]
  );
  const answersJsonRef = React.useRef(answersJson);
  answersJsonRef.current = answersJson;

  /* Countdown (cosmetic — the server enforces the real deadline). */
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!attempt.deadlineAt) return;
    const deadline = new Date(attempt.deadlineAt).getTime();
    function tick() {
      const left = Math.max(0, Math.round((deadline - Date.now()) / 1000));
      setRemaining(left);
    }
    tick();
    const interval = setInterval(tick, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [attempt.deadlineAt]);

  /* Debounced server-side draft autosave. */
  const firstRenderRef = React.useRef(true);
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (firstRenderRef.current) {
      firstRenderRef.current = false;
      return;
    }
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.set("attemptId", attempt.id);
      formData.set("answersJson", answersJsonRef.current);
      startSaving(async () => {
        await saveFormAction(formData);
        setDraftSavedAt(
          new Intl.DateTimeFormat("ar", {
            hour: "2-digit",
            minute: "2-digit",
            second: "2-digit",
          }).format(new Date())
        );
      });
    }, 2500);
    return () => clearTimeout(timeout);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [answers]);

  /* Auto-submit exactly once when the countdown reaches zero. */
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (remaining === null || remaining > 0 || autoSubmittedRef.current) return;
    autoSubmittedRef.current = true;
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.set("attemptId", attempt.id);
    formData.set("answersJson", answersJsonRef.current);
    startSaving(async () => {
      await submitFormAction(formData);
    });
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [remaining]);

  const current = questions[currentIndex];
  const unansweredRequired = questions.filter((question) => {
    const answer = answers[question.id];
    if (!question.isRequired) return false;
    if (!answer) return true;
    if (question.type === "SINGLE_CHOICE") return !answer.choice?.length;
    if (question.type === "MULTIPLE_CHOICE") return !answer.choice?.length;
    if (question.type === "TRUE_FALSE") return answer.bool === undefined;
    return !(answer.text ?? "").trim();
  });

  function setAnswer(questionId: string, entry: AnswerEntry) {
    setAnswers((current) => ({ ...current, [questionId]: entry }));
  }

  const minutes = remaining === null ? null : Math.floor(remaining / 60);
  const seconds = remaining === null ? null : remaining % 60;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Header card: timer + progress + draft indicator */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-card px-4 py-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-foreground">
          <ListChecks className="size-4 text-brand-600" aria-hidden="true" />
          السؤال {currentIndex + 1} من {questions.length}
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          {isSaving ? (
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-muted-foreground">
              <Save className="size-3.5 animate-pulse" aria-hidden="true" />
              جارٍ حفظ المسودة…
            </span>
          ) : draftSavedAt ? (
            <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-success">
              حُفظت المسودة {draftSavedAt}
            </span>
          ) : null}
          {remaining !== null && (
            <span
              className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-extrabold tabular-nums ${
                remaining <= 60
                  ? "bg-error-soft text-error"
                  : "bg-brand-50 text-brand-700"
              }`}
              role="timer"
              aria-label="الوقت المتبقي"
            >
              <Clock className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              {minutes}:{String(seconds).padStart(2, "0")}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {submitState?.message && (
        <Alert variant="destructive" role="alert">
          <AlertTriangle className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          <AlertDescription>
            {submitState.message}
            {submitState.message.includes("وقت") && (
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="link"
                className="h-auto p-0 ps-2 text-xs"
                onClick={() => router.push(`/assessments/${assessment.id}`)}
              >
                العودة إلى الاختبار
              </Button>
            )}
          </AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      )}

      {/* Question navigation grid */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5" role="tablist" aria-label="التنقل بين الأسئلة">
        {questions.map((question, index) => {
          const answered =
            question.type === "TRUE_FALSE"
              ? answers[question.id]?.bool !== undefined
              : question.type === "SHORT_ANSWER"
                ? Boolean(answers[question.id]?.text?.trim())
                : Boolean(answers[question.id]?.choice?.length);
          return (
            <button
              key={question.id}
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={index === currentIndex}
              onClick={() => setCurrentIndex(index)}
              className={`size-9 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold tabular-nums transition-colors ${
                index === currentIndex
                  ? "bg-brand-600 text-white"
                  : answered
                    ? "bg-success-soft text-success"
                    : "border bg-card text-muted-foreground hover:bg-accent"
              }`}
            >
              {index + 1}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Current question */}
      {current && (
        <div className="rounded-xl border bg-card p-5" role="tabpanel">
          <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-[11px] font-bold text-muted-foreground">
            <span className="rounded-full bg-brand-50 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-brand-700">
              {current.points} نقاط
            </span>
            {current.isRequired && <span>إجابة إلزامية</span>}
          </div>
          <p className="text-base font-bold leading-relaxed text-foreground">
            {current.textAr}
          </p>
          {current.textEn && (
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground" dir="ltr">
              {current.textEn}
            </p>
          )}

          <div className="mt-5">
            <QuestionInput
              question={current}
              value={answers[current.id] ?? {}}
              onChange={(entry) => setAnswer(current.id, entry)}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Navigation + submit */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="outline"
          disabled={currentIndex === 0}
          onClick={() => setCurrentIndex((value) => Math.max(0, value - 1))}
        >
          <ChevronRight className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          السابق
        </Button>

        {currentIndex < questions.length - 1 ? (
          <Button
            type="button"
            onClick={() =>
              setCurrentIndex((value) => Math.min(questions.length - 1, value + 1))
            }
          >
            التالي
            <ChevronLeft className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          </Button>
        ) : (
          <Button type="button" onClick={() => setShowReview(true)}>
            <Send className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            مراجعة وتسليم
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Review-before-submit modal */}
      {showReview && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4"
          role="dialog"
          aria-modal="true"
          aria-label="مراجعة الإجابات قبل التسليم"
        >
          <div className="max-h-[85vh] w-full max-w-lg overflow-y-auto rounded-xl bg-card p-5 shadow-lg">
            <h2 className="text-base font-extrabold text-foreground">
              مراجعة نهائية قبل التسليم
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              بعد التسليم لا يمكن تعديل الإجابات أو إعادة هذه المحاولة.
            </p>

            {unansweredRequired.length > 0 && (
              <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-3">
                <AlertTriangle className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                <AlertDescription>
                  لديك {unansweredRequired.length}{" "}
                  {unansweredRequired.length === 1 ? "سؤال إلزامي" : "أسئلة إلزامية"}{" "}
                  بدون إجابة:{" "}
                  {unansweredRequired.map((question, index) => (
                    <button
                      key={question.id}
                      type="button"
                      className="font-bold underline"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setCurrentIndex(questions.indexOf(question));
                        setShowReview(false);
                      }}
                    >
                      رقم {questions.indexOf(question) + 1}
                      {index < unansweredRequired.length - 1 ? "، " : ""}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            )}

            {/* The real submission form — hidden payload updated at submit. */}
            <form action={submitFormAction} className="mt-4 space-y-3">
              <input type="hidden" name="attemptId" value={attempt.id} />
              <input
                type="hidden"
                name="answersJson"
                value={answersJson}
                ref={(node) => {
                  if (node) node.value = answersJsonRef.current;
                }}
              />
              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="outline"
                  onClick={() => setShowReview(false)}
                >
                  متابعة الحل
                </Button>
                <Button type="submit" disabled={isSaving}>
                  <Send className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                  {isSaving ? "جارٍ التسليم…" : "تسليم المحاولة نهائيًا"}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ------------------------- Per-type input -------------------------- */

function QuestionInput({
  question,
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  question: TakeAttemptView["questions"][number];
  value: AnswerEntry;
  onChange: (entry: AnswerEntry) => void;
}) {
  if (question.type === "SINGLE_CHOICE") {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-2" role="radiogroup" aria-label="خيارات السؤال">
        {question.options.map((option, index) => (
          <label
            key={option.id}
            className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm transition-colors hover:bg-accent has-[input:checked]:border-brand-600 has-[input:checked]:bg-brand-50"
          >
            <input
              type="radio"
              name={`q-${question.id}`}
              checked={value.choice?.[0] === option.id}
              onChange={() => onChange({ choice: [option.id] })}
              className="size-4 shrink-0 accent-[#1D5BD6]"
            />
            <span className="font-semibold">{option.textAr}</span>
            <span className="ms-auto text-[10px] text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true">
              {index + 1}
            </span>
          </label>
        ))}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (question.type === "MULTIPLE_CHOICE") {
    const selected = new Set(value.choice ?? []);
    function toggle(optionId: string, checked: boolean) {
      const next = new Set(selected);
      if (checked) next.add(optionId);
      else next.delete(optionId);
      onChange({ choice: [...next] });
    }
    return (
      <div className="space-y-2">
        {question.options.map((option) => (
          <label
            key={option.id}
            className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm transition-colors hover:bg-accent has-[input:checked]:border-brand-600 has-[input:checked]:bg-brand-50"
          >
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={selected.has(option.id)}
              onChange={(event) => toggle(option.id, event.target.checked)}
              className="size-4 shrink-0 accent-[#1D5BD6]"
            />
            <span className="font-semibold">{option.textAr}</span>
          </label>
        ))}
        <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
          اختر كل الإجابات الصحيحة — تُحتسب الدرجة عند تطابق الاختيارات تمامًا.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (question.type === "TRUE_FALSE") {
    return (
      <div className="flex gap-3" role="radiogroup" aria-label="صح أو خطأ">
        {(["true", "false"] as const).map((key) => {
          const boolValue = key === "true";
          return (
            <label
              key={key}
              className="flex flex-1 cursor-pointer items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg border px-3.5 py-3 text-sm font-bold transition-colors hover:bg-accent has-[input:checked]:border-brand-600 has-[input:checked]:bg-brand-50"
            >
              <input
                type="radio"
                name={`q-${question.id}`}
                checked={value.bool === boolValue}
                onChange={() => onChange({ bool: boolValue })}
                className="size-4 accent-[#1D5BD6]"
              />
              {TRUE_FALSE_LABELS[key]}
            </label>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    );
  }

  // SHORT_ANSWER
  return (
    <div className="space-y-1.5">
      <Label htmlFor={`answer-${question.id}`} className="text-xs">
        اكتب إجابتك — تُراجع يدويًا بعد التسليم
      </Label>
      <Textarea
        id={`answer-${question.id}`}
        rows={4}
        value={value.text ?? ""}
        onChange={(event) => onChange({ text: event.target.value })}
        maxLength={2000}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\auth\auth-card.tsx =====
import Link from "next/link";
import { GraduationCap, ShieldCheck } from "lucide-react";

import { defaultBranding } from "@/config/branding";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface AuthCardProps {
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  footer?: React.ReactNode;
  /** Narrower card for verification result screens. */
  compact?: boolean;
  className?: string;
}

/**
 * Shared card for the gate screens (login, registration, verification,
 * password recovery): brand lockup, RTL Arabic-first layout, blue
 * identity and consistent spacing with the rest of the platform.
 *
 * The backdrop is a quiet brand scene built purely from design tokens
 * (brand-blue washes + a faint dot grid fading at the edges) — no
 * imagery, no hard gradients, clipped by overflow-hidden so it can
 * never cause horizontal overflow on small screens.
 */
export function AuthCard({
  title,
  description,
  children,
  footer,
  compact,
  className,
}: AuthCardProps) {
  return (
    <div className="relative flex min-h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center overflow-hidden bg-background px-4 py-10">
      {/* Brand ambience (decorative only, hidden from AT) */}
      <div aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0">

        <div className="absolute -bottom-44 -left-36 h-96 w-[40rem] rounded-full bg-info-soft/80 blur-3xl" />
        <div className="absolute left-1/2 top-1/2 h-[30rem] w-[46rem] -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full bg-brand-50/70 blur-3xl" />
      </div>

      <div className={cn("relative w-full", compact ? "max-w-md" : "max-w-lg", className)}>
        {/* Secure-session badge */}
        <div className="mb-5 flex justify-center">
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-border/70 bg-card/80 py-1.5 ps-3.5 pe-4 text-xs font-semibold text-foreground shadow-sm backdrop-blur">
            <ShieldCheck className="size-3.5 text-success" aria-hidden="true" />

          </span>

          <div
            aria-hidden="true"

                <GraduationCap className="size-7" aria-hidden="true" />
              </span>
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <p className="text-sm font-bold text-brand-700">
                  {defaultBranding.productName}
                </p>
                <h1 className="text-2xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-foreground">
                  {title}
                </h1>
                {description && (
                  <p className="mx-auto max-w-sm text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
                    {description}
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {children}
          </div>
        </div>

        {footer && (
          <div className="mt-5 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">{footer}</div>
        )}

        <p className="mt-8 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground/70">
          <Link href="/" className="transition-colors hover:text-brand-600">
            {defaultBranding.productName}
          </Link>{" "}
          — بوابة الدخول الموحدة
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

import { BookOpen, CheckCircle2, GraduationCap, Users } from "lucide-react";

import { SectionCard } from "@/components/platform/section-card";
import { getAcademyMetrics } from "@/lib/training/service";

interface AcademyMetricsSectionProps {
  /** When set, renders the office manager's school-scoped metrics. */
  schoolId?: string | null;
}

/**
 * Real, persisted academy metrics for the dashboard. Reads straight
 * from the database on every request — no mock values.
 */
export async function AcademyMetricsSection({
  schoolId = null,
}: AcademyMetricsSectionProps) {
  const metrics = await getAcademyMetrics(schoolId);

  const items = [
    {
      id: "courses-total",
      label: "إجمالي الدورات",
      value: metrics.coursesTotal,
      icon: BookOpen,
      tileClass: "bg-brand-100 text-brand-700",
    },
    {
      id: "courses-published",
      label: "دورات منشورة",
      value: metrics.coursesPublished,
      icon: CheckCircle2,
      tileClass: "bg-success-soft text-success",
    },
    {
      id: "programs-total",
      label: "البرامج التدريبية",
      value: metrics.programsTotal,
      icon: GraduationCap,
      tileClass: "bg-info-soft text-info",
    },
    {
      id: "enrollments",
      label: "تسجيلات المعلمين",
      value: metrics.enrollmentsCount,
      icon: Users,
      tileClass: "bg-warning-soft text-warning",
    },
  ];

  return (
    <SectionCard
      title="الأكاديمية التدريبية"
      description="مؤشرات حقيقية محفوظة في قاعدة البيانات — تُحدَّث مع كل إضافة أو نشر."

      <div
        className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 xl:grid-cols-4"
        role="list"
        aria-label="مؤشرات الأكاديمية التدريبية"
      >
        {items.map((item) => {
          const Icon = item.icon;
          return (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              role="listitem"
              className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-background/60 p-3.5"
            >
              <span
                className={`flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg ${item.tileClass}`}
                aria-hidden="true"
              >
                <Icon className="size-5" strokeWidth={1.9} />
              </span>
              <span className="min-w-0 leading-tight">
                <span className="block text-xl font-extrabold tabular-nums text-foreground">
                  {item.value}
                </span>
                <span className="block truncate text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
                  {item.label}
                </span>
              </span>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </SectionCard>
  );
}

import { CheckCircle2, ClipboardCheck, FileQuestion, Percent } from "lucide-react";

import { SectionCard } from "@/components/platform/section-card";
import { getAssessmentMetrics } from "@/lib/assessment/service";

interface AssessmentMetricsSectionProps {
  /** When set, renders the office manager's school-scoped metrics. */
  schoolId?: string | null;
}

/**
 * Real, persisted assessment metrics for the dashboard. Empty states
 * are honest: null averages/rates render as "—" instead of misleading
 * zeros when no graded attempts exist yet.
 */
export async function AssessmentMetricsSection({
  schoolId = null,
}: AssessmentMetricsSectionProps) {
  const metrics = await getAssessmentMetrics(schoolId);

  const items = [
    {
      id: "assessments-published",
      label: "اختبارات منشورة",
      value: metrics.assessmentsPublished,
      icon: ClipboardCheck,
      tileClass: "bg-brand-100 text-brand-700",
    },
    {
      id: "attempts-finalized",
      label: "محاولات مُقيَّمة",
      value: metrics.attemptsFinalized,
      icon: FileQuestion,
      tileClass: "bg-info-soft text-info",
    },
    {
      id: "avg-percentage",
      label: "متوسط النتائج",
      value:
        metrics.avgPercentage === null ? "—" : `${metrics.avgPercentage}%`,
      icon: Percent,
      tileClass: "bg-success-soft text-success",
    },
    {
      id: "needs-review",
      label: "بانتظار المراجعة",
      value: metrics.needsReviewCount,
      icon: CheckCircle2,
      tileClass: "bg-warning-soft text-warning",
    },
  ];

  return (
    <SectionCard
      title="الاختبارات وتقييم الأثر"
      description="مؤشرات حقيقية محفوظة في قاعدة البيانات — محاولات مكتملة ومراجعات معلقة ومعدلات النجاح."
    >
      <div
        className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 xl:grid-cols-4"
        role="list"
        aria-label="مؤشرات الاختبارات"
      >
        {items.map((item) => {
          const Icon = item.icon;
          return (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              role="listitem"
              className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-background/60 p-3.5"

              <span
                className={`flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg ${item.tileClass}`}
                aria-hidden="true"
              >
                <Icon className="size-5" strokeWidth={1.9} />
              </span>
              <span className="min-w-0 leading-tight">
                <span className="block text-xl font-extrabold tabular-nums text-foreground">
                  {item.value}
                </span>
                <span className="block truncate text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
                  {item.label}
                </span>

            </div>
          );

"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { CalendarDays } from "lucide-react";

import { PageHeader } from "@/components/layout/page-header";
import { QuickActions } from "@/components/dashboard/quick-actions";
import { RecentActivities } from "@/components/dashboard/recent-activities";
import { StageDistributionChart } from "@/components/dashboard/stage-distribution-chart";
import { TrainingProgressChart } from "@/components/dashboard/training-progress-chart";
import { UpcomingEvents } from "@/components/dashboard/upcoming-events";
import { StatCard } from "@/components/platform/stat-card";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/platform/status-badge";
import { getNavItemId } from "@/config/navigation";
import { mockOverviewStats } from "@/lib/mock-data";
import type { NavItem } from "@/lib/types";

interface DashboardViewProps {
  /**
   * Server-rendered slot for the real (persisted) schools & branches
   * metrics section — passed from the server page so the dashboard
   * client bundle never touches the database directly.
   */
  schoolsSlot?: React.ReactNode;
}

const todayLabel = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("ar-EG", {
  weekday: "long",
  day: "numeric",
  month: "long",
  year: "numeric",
}).format(new Date());

/**
 * The dashboard: overview statistics, training progress visualization,
 * recent activities, upcoming events and quick actions.
 *
 * Phase 2: navigation goes through real routes via the router, and the
 * schools metrics section renders data straight from the database.
 */
export function DashboardView({ schoolsSlot }: DashboardViewProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const homeModule = getNavItemId("home") as NavItem;

  function handleModuleNavigation(moduleId: string) {
    router.push(getNavItemId(moduleId)?.href ?? "/");
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHeader
        module={homeModule}
        title="لوحة المعلومات"
        description="تابع حالة التنمية المهنية في مساحة عمل مدرستك: المعلمون، البرامج التدريبية، والمتابعات التربوية."
        meta={
          <>
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-card px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground ring-1 ring-border">
              <CalendarDays className="size-3.5 text-brand-600" aria-hidden="true" />
              {todayLabel}
            </span>
            <StatusBadge tone="info" withDot={false}>
              بيانات تجريبية للمرحلة الأولى
            </StatusBadge>
          </>
        }
      />

      {/* Overview statistics */}
      <div
        className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4"
        role="list"
        aria-label="إحصائيات عامة"
      >
        {mockOverviewStats.map((stat) => (
          <StatCard
            key={stat.id}
            stat={stat}
            onClick={() => handleModuleNavigation(stat.targetModuleId)}
          />
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Real schools & branches metrics (persisted data) */}
      {schoolsSlot && <div className="mt-6">{schoolsSlot}</div>}

      {/* Progress visualization */}
      <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 xl:grid-cols-3">
        <div className="xl:col-span-2">
          <TrainingProgressChart />
        </div>
        <StageDistributionChart />
      </div>

      {/* Activities & events */}
      <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 xl:grid-cols-5">
        <div className="xl:col-span-3">
          <RecentActivities />
        </div>
        <div className="xl:col-span-2">
          <UpcomingEvents />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Quick actions */}
      <div className="mt-6">
        <QuickActions onNavigate={handleModuleNavigation} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
