"use client";

import { ChevronLeft } from "lucide-react";

import { mockQuickActions } from "@/lib/mock-data";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { QuickAction } from "@/lib/types";

interface QuickActionsProps {
  onNavigate: (moduleId: string) => void;
}

/**
 * Quick professional-development actions. Each action navigates to its
 * related module — real navigation behavior, no fake flows.
 */
export function QuickActions({ onNavigate }: QuickActionsProps) {
  function handleAction(action: QuickAction) {
    onNavigate(action.targetModuleId);
  }

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="quick-actions-title">
      <div className="mb-3 flex items-baseline justify-between">
        <h2 id="quick-actions-title" className="text-base font-bold text-foreground">
          إجراءات سريعة
        </h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">الانتقال المباشر لأقسام العمل</p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        {mockQuickActions.map((action) => {
          const Icon = action.icon;
          return (
            <button
              key={action.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleAction(action)}
              className={cn(
                "group flex items-center gap-3.5 rounded-xl border bg-card p-4 text-start shadow-xs transition-all",
                "hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-brand-200 hover:shadow-md",
                "focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2"
              )}
            >
              <span
                className="flex size-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-brand-100 text-brand-700 transition-colors group-hover:bg-brand-600 group-hover:text-white"
                aria-hidden="true"
              >
                <Icon className="size-5" strokeWidth={1.9} />
              </span>
              <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <span className="block text-sm font-bold text-foreground">{action.label}</span>
                <span className="block truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  {action.description}
                </span>
              </span>
              <ChevronLeft
                className="size-4 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground/50 transition-transform group-hover:-translate-x-0.5 group-hover:text-brand-600"
                aria-hidden="true"
              />
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\dashboard\recent-activities.tsx =====
"use client";

import { activityIcons, mockRecentActivities } from "@/lib/mock-data";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { ActivityType } from "@/lib/types";

const activityTileStyles: Record<ActivityType, string> = {
  completed: "bg-success-soft text-success",
  enrolled: "bg-info-soft text-info",
  certificate: "bg-brand-100 text-brand-700",
  assessment: "bg-warning-soft text-warning",
  followup: "bg-muted text-muted-foreground",
};

/**
 * Recent professional-development activity feed, rendered as a
 * responsive list (timeline-style on desktop).
 */
export function RecentActivities() {
  return (
    <section
      aria-labelledby="recent-activities-title"
      className="flex flex-col rounded-xl border bg-card shadow-xs"
    >
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 pt-5 pb-4 sm:px-6">
        <div>
          <h2 id="recent-activities-title" className="text-base font-bold text-foreground">
            النشاطات الأخيرة
          </h2>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            آخر التحديثات في أنشطة التنمية المهنية
          </p>
        </div>
        <span className="rounded-full bg-muted px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold text-muted-foreground">
          {mockRecentActivities.length} نشاطات
        </span>
      </div>

      <ul className="scrollbar-thin max-h-96 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-5 pb-5 sm:px-6" role="list">
        {mockRecentActivities.map((activity, index) => {
          const Icon = activityIcons[activity.type];
          const isLast = index === mockRecentActivities.length - 1;
          return (
            <li key={activity.id} className="relative flex gap-3.5 pb-5 last:pb-0">
              {/* Timeline connector */}
              {!isLast && (
                <span
                  className="absolute top-11 bottom-0 start-[19px] w-px bg-border"
                  aria-hidden="true"
                />
              )}
              <span
                className={cn(
                  "z-10 flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full ring-4 ring-card",
                  activityTileStyles[activity.type]
                )}
                aria-hidden="true"
              >
                <Icon className="size-4.5" strokeWidth={1.9} />
              </span>
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 pt-1">
                <p className="text-sm font-semibold leading-snug text-foreground">
                  {activity.title}
                </p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  <span className="font-semibold text-foreground/70">{activity.actor}</span>
                  <span className="mx-1.5" aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                  {activity.time}
                </p>
              </div>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\dashboard\role-dashboard.tsx =====
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  Eye,
  GraduationCap,
  KeyRound,
  Landmark,
  Link2,
  MapPin,
  ShieldCheck,
  UserRound,
  UserCheck,
} from "lucide-react";

import Link from "next/link";

import { SectionCard } from "@/components/platform/section-card";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/platform/status-badge";
import { branchStageLabel } from "@/lib/validation/schools";
import { roleLabel } from "@/lib/auth/roles";
import type { SafeUser } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import {
  listDeputyGrants,
  listSupervisorAssignments,
} from "@/lib/users/service";
import {
  getOwnProfileView,
  getSupervisorWorkspace,
} from "@/lib/users/directory";

/**
 * Role-aware dashboard for the non-administrative roles. Each role sees
 * ONLY the data its server-side boundary allows:
 *   - supervisor: branches assigned to them + teachers inside them;
 *   - deputy: explicitly granted schools/branches, read-only notice;
 *   - teacher: own real profile (school/branch/supervisor) + honest
 *     placeholders for the future professional modules.
 */
export async function RoleDashboard({ user }: { user: SafeUser }) {
  if (user.role === "EDUCATIONAL_SUPERVISOR") {
    const [workspace, assignments] = await Promise.all([
      getSupervisorWorkspace(user.id),
      listSupervisorAssignments(user.id),
    ]);
    const teachers = workspace?.teachers ?? [];

    return (
      <div className="space-y-6">
        <WelcomeHeader user={user} />
        <SectionCard
          title="الفروع المسندة إليك"
          description="يمكنك متابعة معلمي هذه الفروع فقط — الإسناد يتم من إدارة مكتب التطوير في مدرستك."
          action={
            <Link
              href="/supervision"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-brand-600 transition-colors hover:bg-brand-50 hover:text-brand-700"
            >
              مساحة المشرف الكاملة
              <ArrowLeft className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
            </Link>
          }
        >
          {assignments.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              لا توجد فروع مسندة إليك بعد — تواصل مع إدارة مكتب التطوير.
            </p>
          ) : (
            <ul className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2" role="list">
              {assignments.map((assignment) => (
                <li
                  key={assignment.id}
                  className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-background/60 p-4"
                >
                  <span className="flex size-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-brand-100 text-brand-700">
                    <MapPin className="size-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  </span>
                  <span className="min-w-0">
                    <span className="block truncate text-sm font-bold">
                      {assignment.branch.nameAr}
                    </span>
                    <span className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {branchStageLabel(assignment.branch.stage)}
                    </span>
                  </span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </SectionCard>

        <SectionCard
          title={`المعلمون داخل نطاقك (${teachers.length})`}
          description="إسناد مباشر أو عبر تغطية الفروع — محصور دائمًا داخل مدرستك وبفروقك المصرّح بها."
        >
          {teachers.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              لا يوجد معلمون في نطاقك حتى الآن.
            </p>
          ) : (
            <ul className="divide-y rounded-xl border" role="list">
              {teachers.map((teacher) => (
                <li
                  key={teacher.id}
                  className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 p-3.5"
                >
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="truncate text-sm font-bold">{teacher.name}</p>
                    <p className="truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground" dir="ltr">
                      {teacher.email}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {teacher.branchName ?? "بدون فرع"}
                    </span>
                    <StatusBadge
                      tone={teacher.source === "explicit" ? "info" : "neutral"}
                      withDot={false}
                    >
                      {teacher.source === "explicit" ? "إسناد مباشر" : "عبر الفرع"}
                    </StatusBadge>
                  </div>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </SectionCard>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (user.role === "EDUCATIONAL_DEPUTY") {
    const grants = await listDeputyGrants(user.id);

    return (
      <div className="space-y-6">
        <WelcomeHeader user={user} />
        <SectionCard
          title="صلاحيات الاطلاع الممنوحة لك"
          description="وصول قراءة فقط على المدارس والفروع الممنوحة صراحة — لا تملك صلاحيات إنشاء أو تعديل أو حذف."
          action={
            <Link
              href="/deputy-overview"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-brand-600 transition-colors hover:bg-brand-50 hover:text-brand-700"
            >
              <Eye className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
              نطاق اطلاعي الكامل
            </Link>
          }
        >
          {grants.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              لا توجد صلاحيات اطلاع ممنوحة لك بعد — تواصل مع إدارة مكتب التطوير.
            </p>
          ) : (
            <ul className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2" role="list">
              {grants.map((grant) => (
                <li
                  key={grant.id}
                  className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-background/60 p-4"
                >
                  <span className="flex size-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-info-soft text-info">
                    <Landmark className="size-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  </span>
                  <span className="min-w-0">
                    <span className="block truncate text-sm font-bold">
                      {grant.school.nameAr}
                    </span>
                    <span className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {grant.branch
                        ? `فرع: ${grant.branch.nameAr}`
                        : "اطلاع على المدرسة كاملة"}
                    </span>
                  </span>
                  <span className="ms-auto shrink-0">
                    <StatusBadge tone="info">قراءة فقط</StatusBadge>
                  </span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </SectionCard>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // TEACHER — real own profile data (school, branch, supervisor) via the
  // session-bound reader; no other user's data is reachable here.
  const profile = await getOwnProfileView(user.id);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <WelcomeHeader user={user} />
      <SectionCard
        title="ملفي الشخصي"
        description="بيانات حسابك الحقيقية — وحدات التدريب والتقييم والشهادات الخاصة بك ستتوفر في مراحل لاحقة."
        action={
          <Link
            href="/my-profile"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-brand-600 transition-colors hover:bg-brand-50 hover:text-brand-700"
          >
            ملفي الكامل
            <ArrowLeft className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
          </Link>
        }
      >
        <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <ProfileRow icon={UserRound} label="الاسم" value={user.name} />
          <ProfileRow icon={KeyRound} label="البريد الإلكتروني" value={user.email} ltr />
          <ProfileRow
            icon={Landmark}
            label="المدرسة"
            value={profile?.school?.nameAr ?? "— غير محددة"}
          />
          <ProfileRow
            icon={MapPin}
            label="الفرع"
            value={profile?.branch?.nameAr ?? "— بدون فرع"}
          />
          {profile?.supervisor && (
            <ProfileRow
              icon={UserCheck}
              label="المشرف التربوي"
              value={profile.supervisor.name}
            />
          )}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-background/60 p-3.5">
            <span className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-success-soft text-success">
              <ShieldCheck className="size-5" aria-hidden="true" />
            </span>
            <span className="min-w-0 leading-tight">
              <span className="block text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
                حالة التوثيق
              </span>
              <span className="mt-1 block">
                <StatusBadge tone={user.emailVerifiedAt ? "success" : "warning"}>
                  {user.emailVerifiedAt ? "بريد موثّق" : "بانتظار التأكيد"}
                </StatusBadge>
              </span>
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </SectionCard>

      <SectionCard
        title="رحلتي المهنية"
        description="قيد التطوير — ستشمل برامجك التدريبية واختباراتك وشهاداتك في مراحل قادمة."
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-muted/40 p-4">
          <span className="flex size-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-brand-100 text-brand-700">
            <GraduationCap className="size-5" aria-hidden="true" />
          </span>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            لا توجد وحدات مهنية متاحة بعد — سيصلك إشعار عند إطلاقها.
          </p>
        </div>
      </SectionCard>
    </div>
  );
}

function WelcomeHeader({ user }: { user: SafeUser }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-card p-5 shadow-xs">
      <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">أهلًا بك في منارة قرطبة</p>
      <p className="mt-0.5 text-lg font-extrabold">
        {user.name}{" "}
        <span className="ms-1 rounded-full bg-brand-50 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-bold text-brand-700">
          {roleLabel(user.role)}
        </span>
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

function ProfileRow({
  icon: Icon,
  label,
  value,
  ltr,
}: {
  icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
  label: string;
  value: string;
  ltr?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-background/60 p-3.5">
      <span className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-brand-100 text-brand-700">
        <Icon className="size-5" aria-hidden="true" />
      </span>
      <span className="min-w-0 leading-tight">
        <span className="block text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">{label}</span>
        <span className="mt-0.5 block truncate text-sm font-bold" dir={ltr ? "ltr" : undefined}>
          {value}
        </span>
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";
import { Building2, ChevronLeft, Landmark, ToggleLeft, Users } from "lucide-react";

import { SectionCard } from "@/components/platform/section-card";
import { getSchoolMetricsForSchool, getSchoolsMetrics } from "@/lib/schools/service";

/**
 * Real, persisted schools & branches metrics for the dashboard.
 * Server component — reads straight from the database on every request
 * (Next revalidates these paths after every school mutation).
 *
 * Two scopes, enforced server-side by the caller:
 *   - no schoolId -> platform-wide (SUPER_ADMIN dashboard)
 *   - schoolId    -> ONE school's branches + teachers (office manager;
 *                    the schoolId comes from the session, never client)
 *
 * Mock Phase-1 sections keep their honest "بيانات تجريبية" label; this
 * section is labelled as real persisted data.
 */
interface SchoolsMetricsSectionProps {
  /** When set, renders school-scoped metrics for the office manager. */
  schoolId?: string;
}

export async function SchoolsMetricsSection({ schoolId }: SchoolsMetricsSectionProps) {
  if (schoolId) {
    const scoped = await getSchoolMetricsForSchool(schoolId);
    if (!scoped) {
      return (
        <SectionCard title="بيانات المدرسة" description="لم يتم العثور على بيانات المدرسة.">
          <p className="py-4 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            تعذر تحميل مؤشرات مدرستك — تواصل مع مدير المنصة.
          </p>
        </SectionCard>
      );
    }

    const scopedItems = [
      {
        id: "total-branches",
        label: "إجمالي الفروع",
        value: scoped.totalBranches,
        icon: Landmark,
        tileClass: "bg-brand-100 text-brand-700",
      },
      {
        id: "active-branches",
        label: "فروع نشطة",
        value: scoped.activeBranches,
        icon: ToggleLeft,
        tileClass: "bg-success-soft text-success",
      },
      {
        id: "inactive-branches",
        label: "فروع موقوفة",
        value: scoped.inactiveBranches,
        icon: Building2,
        tileClass: "bg-warning-soft text-warning",
      },
      {
        id: "teachers",
        label: "المعلمون المسجلون",
        value: scoped.teachersCount,
        icon: Users,
        tileClass: "bg-info-soft text-info",
      },
    ];

    return (
      <SectionCard
        title={`مدرستك — ${scoped.schoolName}`}
        description="مؤشرات حقيقية لمدرستك من قاعدة البيانات — تتحدث مع كل إضافة أو تعديل."
      >
        <div
          className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 xl:grid-cols-4"
          role="list"
          aria-label="مؤشرات مدرستك"
        >
          {scopedItems.map((item) => {
            const Icon = item.icon;
            return (
              <div
                key={item.id}
                role="listitem"
                className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-background/60 p-3.5"
              >
                <span
                  className={`flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg ${item.tileClass}`}
                  aria-hidden="true"
                >
                  <Icon className="size-5" strokeWidth={1.9} />
                </span>
                <span className="min-w-0 leading-tight">
                  <span className="block text-xl font-extrabold tabular-nums text-foreground">
                    {item.value}
                  </span>
                  <span className="block truncate text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
                    {item.label}
                  </span>
                </span>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </SectionCard>
    );
  }

  const metrics = await getSchoolsMetrics();

  const items = [
    {
      id: "total-schools",
      label: "إجمالي المدارس",
      value: metrics.totalSchools,
      icon: Landmark,
      tileClass: "bg-brand-100 text-brand-700",
    },
    {
      id: "active-schools",
      label: "مدارس نشطة",
      value: metrics.activeSchools,
      icon: ToggleLeft,
      tileClass: "bg-success-soft text-success",
    },
    {
      id: "inactive-schools",
      label: "مدارس موقوفة",
      value: metrics.inactiveSchools,
      icon: Building2,
      tileClass: "bg-warning-soft text-warning",
    },
    {
      id: "total-branches",
      label: "إجمالي الفروع",
      value: metrics.totalBranches,
      icon: Users,
      tileClass: "bg-info-soft text-info",
    },
  ];

  return (
    <SectionCard
      title="المدارس والفروع"
      description="مؤشرات حقيقية محفوظة في قاعدة البيانات — تُحدَّث مع كل إضافة أو تعديل."
      action={
        <Link
          href="/schools"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-brand-600 transition-colors hover:bg-brand-50 hover:text-brand-700"
        >
          إدارة المدارس
          <ChevronLeft className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
        </Link>
      }
    >
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 xl:grid-cols-4" role="list" aria-label="مؤشرات المدارس والفروع">
        {items.map((item) => {
          const Icon = item.icon;
          return (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              role="listitem"
              className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-background/60 p-3.5"
            >
              <span
                className={`flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg ${item.tileClass}`}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\dashboard\stage-distribution-chart.tsx =====
"use client";

import { Cell, Pie, PieChart, ResponsiveContainer, Tooltip } from "recharts";

import { SectionCard } from "@/components/platform/section-card";
import { mockTeachersByStage } from "@/lib/mock-data";
import type { StageDistributionPoint } from "@/lib/types";

const chartColors = ["var(--chart-1)", "var(--chart-2)", "var(--chart-3)"];

function DonutTooltip({
  active,
  payload,
}: {
  active?: boolean;
  payload?: { name?: string | number; value?: number | string }[];
}) {
  if (!active || !payload?.length) return null;
  const entry = payload[0];
  return (
    <div dir="rtl" className="rounded-lg border bg-popover px-3 py-2 shadow-lg">
      <p className="text-xs font-bold text-foreground">{entry.name}</p>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        <span className="font-bold text-foreground tabular-nums">{entry.value}</span> معلم
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * Teacher distribution across educational stages — donut chart fed
 * directly from the centralized mock dataset.
 */
export function StageDistributionChart() {
  const total = mockTeachersByStage.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.count, 0);

  return (
    <SectionCard
      title="توزيع المعلمين على المراحل"
      description="عدد المعلمين في كل مرحلة دراسية"
    >
      <div className="relative mx-auto h-52 w-full max-w-60">
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
          <PieChart>
            <Tooltip content={<DonutTooltip />} />
            <Pie
              data={mockTeachersByStage as StageDistributionPoint[]}
              dataKey="count"
              nameKey="stage"
              innerRadius="68%"
              outerRadius="94%"
              paddingAngle={3}
              strokeWidth={0}
              cornerRadius={6}
            >
              {mockTeachersByStage.map((entry, index) => (
                <Cell key={entry.stage} fill={chartColors[index % chartColors.length]} />
              ))}
            </Pie>
          </PieChart>
        </ResponsiveContainer>
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
          <p className="text-3xl font-extrabold tabular-nums text-foreground">{total}</p>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground">إجمالي المعلمين</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <ul className="mt-4 space-y-2.5" role="list" aria-label="تفاصيل توزيع المعلمين">
        {mockTeachersByStage.map((entry, index) => {
          const percent = Math.round((entry.count / total) * 100);
          return (
            <li key={entry.stage} className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-sm">
              <span
                className="size-2.5 shrink-0 rounded-full"
                style={{ backgroundColor: chartColors[index % chartColors.length] }}
                aria-hidden="true"
              />
              <span className="flex-1 font-semibold text-foreground/85">{entry.stage}</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground tabular-nums">{percent}%</span>
              <span className="w-9 text-end text-sm font-bold tabular-nums text-foreground">
                {entry.count}
              </span>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
    </SectionCard>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\dashboard\training-progress-chart.tsx =====
"use client";

import {
  Bar,
  CartesianGrid,
  ComposedChart,
  Line,
  ResponsiveContainer,
  Tooltip,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
} from "recharts";

import { SectionCard } from "@/components/platform/section-card";
import { mockTrainingProgress } from "@/lib/mock-data";

interface TooltipPayloadItem {
  name?: string | number;
  value?: number | string;
  color?: string;
}

function ChartTooltip({
  active,
  payload,
  label,
}: {
  active?: boolean;
  payload?: TooltipPayloadItem[];
  label?: string | number;
}) {
  if (!active || !payload?.length) return null;
  return (
    <div dir="rtl" className="rounded-lg border bg-popover px-3 py-2 shadow-lg">
      <p className="mb-1 text-xs font-bold text-foreground">{label}</p>
      {payload.map((entry) => (
        <p key={entry.name} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          <span
            className="size-2 rounded-full"
            style={{ backgroundColor: entry.color }}
            aria-hidden="true"
          />
          {entry.name === "completed" ? "ساعات منجزة" : "الهدف"}:{" "}
          <span className="font-bold text-foreground tabular-nums">{entry.value}</span> ساعة
        </p>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * Monthly training progress: completed hours (bars) against the
 * monthly target (line). Rendered RTL: months flow right-to-left and
 * the value axis sits on the right. Data comes exclusively from the
 * centralized mock dataset.
 */
export function TrainingProgressChart() {
  const totalCompleted = mockTrainingProgress.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.completed, 0);
  const totalTarget = mockTrainingProgress.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.target, 0);
  const progressPercent = Math.round((totalCompleted / totalTarget) * 100);

  return (
    <SectionCard
      title="تقدم التدريب الشهري"
      description="الساعات التدريبية المنجزة مقابل الهدف الشهري"
      action={
        <span className="rounded-full bg-brand-50 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-brand-700 ring-1 ring-brand-100">
          الإنجاز الكلي {progressPercent}%
        </span>
      }
    >
      <div dir="ltr" className="h-72 w-full">
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
          <ComposedChart data={mockTrainingProgress} margin={{ top: 8, right: 8, left: 8, bottom: 0 }}>
            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" vertical={false} stroke="var(--border)" />
            <XAxis
              dataKey="month"
              reversed
              tick={{ fill: "var(--muted-foreground)", fontSize: 12, fontFamily: "var(--font-cairo)" }}
              axisLine={{ stroke: "var(--border)" }}
              tickLine={false}
              dy={8}
            />
            <YAxis
              orientation="right"
              tick={{ fill: "var(--muted-foreground)", fontSize: 11 }}
              axisLine={false}
              tickLine={false}
              width={36}
            />
            <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} cursor={{ fill: "var(--accent)", opacity: 0.5 }} />
            <Bar
              dataKey="completed"
              name="completed"
              fill="var(--chart-1)"
              radius={[6, 6, 0, 0]}
              maxBarSize={36}
            />
            <Line
              type="monotone"
              dataKey="target"
              name="target"
              stroke="var(--chart-4)"
              strokeWidth={2}
              strokeDasharray="6 4"
              dot={{ r: 3, fill: "var(--chart-4)", strokeWidth: 0 }}
            />
          </ComposedChart>
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 border-t pt-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span className="size-2.5 rounded-sm bg-[var(--chart-1)]" aria-hidden="true" />
          ساعات منجزة
        </span>
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span className="h-0 w-4 border-t-2 border-dashed border-[var(--chart-4)]" aria-hidden="true" />
          الهدف الشهري
        </span>
      </div>
    </SectionCard>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\dashboard\upcoming-events.tsx =====
"use client";

import { CalendarDays, Clock, MapPin, Presentation, Users, Video } from "lucide-react";

import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/platform/status-badge";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/platform/empty-state";
import { mockUpcomingEvents } from "@/lib/mock-data";
import type { TrainingEventType } from "@/lib/types";

const eventTypeMeta: Record<TrainingEventType, { label: string; icon: typeof Presentation }> = {
  workshop: { label: "ورشة عمل", icon: Presentation },
  course: { label: "دورة", icon: Video },
  meeting: { label: "اجتماع", icon: Users },
};

/**
 * Upcoming training events list. Event data comes from the centralized
 * mock dataset; when no events exist the shared EmptyState is shown.
 */
export function UpcomingEvents() {
  const events = mockUpcomingEvents;

  return (
    <section
      aria-labelledby="upcoming-events-title"
      className="flex flex-col rounded-xl border bg-card shadow-xs"
    >
      <div className="px-5 pt-5 pb-4 sm:px-6">
        <h2 id="upcoming-events-title" className="text-base font-bold text-foreground">
          الفعاليات التدريبية القادمة
        </h2>
        <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          ورش ودورات واجتماعات مجدولة خلال الأسبوعين القادمين
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 px-5 pb-5 sm:px-6">
        {events.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState
            icon={CalendarDays}
            title="لا توجد فعاليات مجدولة"
            description="ستظهر هنا الورش والدورات التدريبية القادمة بمجرد جدولتها."
          />
        ) : (
          <ul className="space-y-3" role="list">
            {events.map((event) => {
              const TypeIcon = eventTypeMeta[event.type].icon;
              return (
                <li
                  key={event.id}
                  className="group flex gap-3.5 rounded-lg border bg-card p-3.5 transition-colors hover:border-brand-200 hover:bg-brand-50/40"
                >
                  {/* Date tile */}
                  <div className="flex size-14 shrink-0 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-b from-brand-600 to-brand-800 text-white shadow-sm">
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-white/80">{event.day}</span>
                    <span className="text-sm font-extrabold leading-tight">{event.date.split(" ")[0]}</span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-white/80">{event.date.split(" ")[1]}</span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-muted px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-muted-foreground">
                        <TypeIcon className="size-3" aria-hidden="true" />
                        {eventTypeMeta[event.type].label}
                      </span>
                      <StatusBadge tone="info">قادم</StatusBadge>
                    </div>
                    <p className="mt-1.5 truncate text-sm font-bold text-foreground">
                      {event.title}
                    </p>
                    <div className="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Clock className="size-3" aria-hidden="true" />
                        {event.time}
                      </span>
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                        <MapPin className="size-3" aria-hidden="true" />
                        {event.location}
                      </span>
                      {typeof event.seatsLeft === "number" && (
                        <span className="font-semibold text-warning">
                          {event.seatsLeft} مقعد متبقي
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\dashboard\users-metrics.tsx =====
import Link from "next/link";
import { ChevronLeft, Eye, ShieldCheck, UserCheck, UserCog, Users } from "lucide-react";

import { SectionCard } from "@/components/platform/section-card";
import { getPlatformUsersMetrics, getSchoolUsersMetrics } from "@/lib/users/directory";

/**
 * Real, persisted USER metrics for the dashboard (Phase 4):
 *   - no schoolId -> platform-wide (SUPER_ADMIN);
 *   - schoolId    -> one school (office manager; the id comes from the
 *     session, never the client). A school-scoped viewer never sees
 *     another school's counts.
 */
interface UsersMetricsSectionProps {
  schoolId?: string;
}

export async function UsersMetricsSection({ schoolId }: UsersMetricsSectionProps) {
  const metrics = schoolId
    ? await getSchoolUsersMetrics(schoolId)
    : await getPlatformUsersMetrics();

  const items = schoolId
    ? [
        {
          id: "school-users",
          label: "مستخدمو المدرسة",
          value: metrics.totalUsers,
          icon: Users,
          tileClass: "bg-brand-100 text-brand-700",
        },
        {
          id: "school-teachers",
          label: "المعلمون",
          value: metrics.teachersCount,
          icon: UserCheck,
          tileClass: "bg-info-soft text-info",
        },
        {
          id: "school-supervisors",
          label: "المشرفون التربويون",
          value: metrics.supervisorsCount,
          icon: UserCog,
          tileClass: "bg-success-soft text-success",
        },
        {
          id: "school-active",
          label: "حسابات نشطة",
          value: metrics.activeUsers,
          icon: ShieldCheck,
          tileClass: "bg-warning-soft text-warning",
        },
      ]
    : [
        {
          id: "platform-users",
          label: "إجمالي المستخدمين",
          value: metrics.totalUsers,
          icon: Users,
          tileClass: "bg-brand-100 text-brand-700",
        },
        {
          id: "platform-teachers",
          label: "المعلمون",
          value: metrics.teachersCount,
          icon: UserCheck,
          tileClass: "bg-info-soft text-info",
        },
        {
          id: "platform-managers",
          label: "مديرو مكاتب التطوير",
          value: metrics.managersCount,
          icon: UserCog,
          tileClass: "bg-success-soft text-success",
        },
        {
          id: "platform-active",
          label: "حسابات نشطة",
          value: metrics.activeUsers,
          icon: ShieldCheck,
          tileClass: "bg-warning-soft text-warning",
        },
      ];

  return (
    <SectionCard
      title={schoolId ? "مستخدمو مدرستك" : "مستخدمو المنصة"}
      description="مؤشرات حقيقية للحسابات من قاعدة البيانات — تُحدَّث مع كل إضافة أو تعديل."
      action={
        <Link
          href="/users"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-brand-600 transition-colors hover:bg-brand-50 hover:text-brand-700"
        >
          دليل المستخدمين
          <ChevronLeft className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
        </Link>
      }
    >
      <div
        className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 xl:grid-cols-4"
        role="list"
        aria-label="مؤشرات المستخدمين"
      >
        {items.map((item) => {
          const Icon = item.icon;
          return (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              role="listitem"
              className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-background/60 p-3.5"
            >
              <span
                className={`flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg ${item.tileClass}`}
                aria-hidden="true"
              >
                <Icon className="size-5" strokeWidth={1.9} />
              </span>
              <span className="min-w-0 leading-tight">
                <span className="block text-xl font-extrabold tabular-nums text-foreground">
                  {item.value}
                </span>
                <span className="block truncate text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
                  {item.label}
                </span>
              </span>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
      {schoolId && metrics.deputiesCount > 0 && (
        <p className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          <Eye className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
          ويشمل {metrics.deputiesCount} وكيلًا تعليميًا بصلاحيات اطلاع.
        </p>
      )}
    </SectionCard>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\layout\app-shell.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";

import { MobileNav } from "@/components/layout/mobile-nav";
import { Sidebar } from "@/components/layout/sidebar";
import { Topbar } from "@/components/layout/topbar";
import { defaultBranding } from "@/config/branding";
import { navItems } from "@/config/navigation";
import type { CurrentUser, NavItem } from "@/lib/types";

interface AppShellProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  /** Session identity — null for unauthenticated visitors. */
  user: CurrentUser | null;
  /** Module ids allowed for this role, computed on the SERVER. The
   * full items (with icons) are resolved here on the client because
   * icon components cannot cross the RSC serialization boundary. */
  allowedModuleIds: string[];
}

const PUBLIC_PREFIXES = [
  "/login",
  "/register",
  "/verify-email",
  "/forgot-password",
  "/reset-password",
  "/forbidden",
];

/**
 * Application shell: RTL sidebar (desktop), sticky topbar with
 * search/notifications/user menu (with real logout), mobile navigation
 * drawer and a sticky footer.
 *
 * Gate screens (login, registration, verification, recovery) render in
 * a minimal bare layout — this is presentation only; every security
 * decision is enforced on the server (middleware + per-page guards).
 */
export function AppShell({ children, user, allowedModuleIds }: AppShellProps) {
  const [mobileNavOpen, setMobileNavOpen] = React.useState(false);
  const pathname = usePathname();

  const items: NavItem[] = React.useMemo(
    () => navItems.filter((item) => allowedModuleIds.includes(item.id)),
    [allowedModuleIds]
  );

  const isPublicRoute =
    !user || PUBLIC_PREFIXES.some((p) => pathname === p || pathname.startsWith(`${p}/`));

  if (isPublicRoute) {
    return <div className="min-h-screen">{children}</div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen">
      <Sidebar branding={defaultBranding} items={items} />

      <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col">
        <Topbar
          user={user}
          items={items}
          onOpenMobileNav={() => setMobileNavOpen(true)}
        />

        <main className="flex-1 px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 sm:py-8 lg:px-8">
          <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl">{children}</div>
        </main>

        {/* Sticky footer: mt-auto keeps it at the bottom on short pages */}
        <footer className="mt-auto border-t border-border/70 bg-card/70 px-4 py-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
          <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-7xl flex-col items-center justify-between gap-1.5 text-center sm:flex-row sm:text-start">
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              {defaultBranding.productName} — {defaultBranding.subtitle}
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground/70">
              بيئة تطوير — المرحلة الثالثة © 2025
            </p>
          </div>
        </footer>
      </div>

      <MobileNav
        open={mobileNavOpen}
        onOpenChange={setMobileNavOpen}
        branding={defaultBranding}
        user={user}
        items={items}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\layout\brand-logo.tsx =====
import { GraduationCap } from "lucide-react";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { PlatformBranding } from "@/lib/types";

interface BrandLogoProps {
  branding: PlatformBranding;
  /** "sidebar" renders on the navy sidebar, "light" on light surfaces. */
  variant?: "sidebar" | "light";
  withSubtitle?: boolean;
  className?: string;
}

/**
 * Platform wordmark. Uses the configured branding (never a hardcoded
 * school name) so a future tenant can swap name/logo seamlessly.
 */
export function BrandLogo({
  branding,
  variant = "sidebar",
  withSubtitle = true,
  className,
}: BrandLogoProps) {
  const light = variant === "light";

  return (
    <div className={cn("flex items-center gap-3", className)}>
      <div
        className={cn(
          "flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl shadow-sm",
          light
            ? "bg-gradient-to-br from-brand-600 to-brand-800 text-white"
            : "bg-white/10 text-white ring-1 ring-white/15"
        )}
        aria-hidden="true"
      >
        <GraduationCap className="size-5.5" strokeWidth={1.8} />
      </div>
      <div className="min-w-0 text-start leading-tight">
        <p
          className={cn(
            "truncate text-base font-extrabold tracking-tight",
            light ? "text-foreground" : "text-white"
          )}
        >
          {branding.productName}
        </p>
        {withSubtitle && (
          <p
            className={cn(
              "truncate text-xs font-medium",
              light ? "text-muted-foreground" : "text-sidebar-foreground/70"
            )}
          >
            {branding.subtitle}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\layout\mobile-nav.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { Landmark } from "lucide-react";

import { Avatar, AvatarFallback } from "@/components/ui/avatar";
import { BrandLogo } from "@/components/layout/brand-logo";
import {
  Sheet,
  SheetContent,
  SheetDescription,
  SheetHeader,
  SheetTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/sheet";
import { activeNavItemId } from "@/config/navigation";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { CurrentUser, NavItem, PlatformBranding } from "@/lib/types";

interface MobileNavProps {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  branding: PlatformBranding;
  user: CurrentUser;
  items: NavItem[];
}

/**
 * Visual grouping — kept in sync with the desktop sidebar (presentation
 * only; never adds, removes or re-orders modules). Unknown ids fall back
 * to an unlabeled trailing group.
 */
const NAV_GROUPS: Array<{ label: string; members: string[] }> = [
  {
    label: "القائمة الرئيسية",
    members: ["home", "academy", "schools", "users", "supervision", "deputy-overview"],
  },
  {
    label: "التدريب والتقييم",
    members: [
      "programs",
      "course-management",
      "assessments",
      "assignments",
      "certificates",
      "reports",
    ],
  },
  {
    label: "حسابي",
    members: ["my-profile", "settings"],
  },
];

/** Group the role-filtered items, preserving config order inside each group. */
function groupNavItems(
  items: NavItem[]
): Array<{ label: string; items: NavItem[] }> {
  const known = new Set(NAV_GROUPS.flatMap((group) => group.members));
  const groups = NAV_GROUPS.map((group) => ({
    label: group.label,
    items: items.filter((item) => group.members.includes(item.id)),
  }));
  const leftovers = items.filter((item) => !known.has(item.id));
  if (leftovers.length > 0) groups.push({ label: "", items: leftovers });
  return groups.filter((group) => group.items.length > 0);
}

/**
 * Mobile navigation drawer. Slides from the inline-start edge (right
 * side in RTL) and mirrors the desktop sidebar navigation, including
 * its visual grouping.
 *
 * Active state uses the longest-href match — identical behavior to the
 * desktop sidebar. The drawer closes itself when a link is clicked and
 * the active state follows the current URL.
 */
export function MobileNav({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  branding,
  user,
  items,
}: MobileNavProps) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const activeId = activeNavItemId(pathname, items);
  const groups = groupNavItems(items);

  return (
    <Sheet open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <SheetContent
        side="right"
        className="flex w-80 flex-col bg-sidebar p-0 text-sidebar-foreground [&>button]:text-white/70"
      >
        <SheetHeader className="border-b border-sidebar-border/70 px-5 py-4 text-start">
          <SheetTitle asChild>
            <div>
              <BrandLogo branding={branding} variant="sidebar" />
            </div>
          </SheetTitle>
          <SheetDescription className="sr-only">
            قائمة التنقل الرئيسية
          </SheetDescription>
        </SheetHeader>

        <nav
          aria-label="التنقل الرئيسي"
          className="scrollbar-thin flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 pb-4 pt-2"
        >
          {groups.map((group, groupIndex) => (
            <div
              key={group.label || `extra-${groupIndex}`}
              className={cn(groupIndex > 0 && "mt-5")}
            >
              {group.label && (
                <p className="px-3 pb-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-sidebar-foreground/40">
                  {group.label}
                </p>
              )}
              <ul className="space-y-0.5" role="list">
                {group.items.map((item) => {
                  const active = item.id === activeId;
                  const Icon = item.icon;
                  return (
                    <li key={item.id}>
                      <Link
                        href={item.href}
                        onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}
                        aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
                        className={cn(
                          "flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors",
                          "focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-sidebar-ring",
                          active
                            ? "bg-gradient-to-l from-brand-600 to-brand-500 text-white shadow-md shadow-brand-900/30"
                            : "text-sidebar-foreground/75 hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-sidebar-accent-foreground"
                        )}
                      >
                        <Icon
                          className={cn(
                            "size-4.5 shrink-0",
                            active
                              ? "text-white"
                              : "text-sidebar-foreground/55 group-hover:text-sidebar-accent-foreground"
                          )}
                          strokeWidth={active ? 2.2 : 1.8}
                          aria-hidden="true"
                        />
                        <span className="flex-1 text-start">{item.label}</span>
                        {item.badge && !active && (
                          <span className="rounded-full bg-white/8 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-sidebar-foreground/70 ring-1 ring-white/10">
                            {item.badge}
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </Link>
                    </li>
                  );
                })}
              </ul>
            </div>
          ))}
        </nav>

        <div className="shrink-0 border-t border-sidebar-border/70 p-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.06] p-3 ring-1 ring-white/10">
            <Avatar className="size-9">
              <AvatarFallback className="bg-brand-600 text-xs font-bold text-white">
                {user.initials}
              </AvatarFallback>
            </Avatar>
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 leading-tight">
              <p className="truncate text-sm font-bold text-sidebar-foreground">
                {user.name}
              </p>
              <p className="truncate text-[11px] text-sidebar-foreground/60">
                {user.role}
              </p>
            </div>
            <Landmark className="size-4 shrink-0 text-sidebar-foreground/40" aria-hidden="true" />
          </div>
        </div>
      </SheetContent>
    </Sheet>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\layout\page-header.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { ChevronLeft } from "lucide-react";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { NavItem } from "@/lib/types";

export interface BreadcrumbItem {
  label: string;
  /** When omitted the crumb renders as the current page (no link). */
  href?: string;
}

interface PageHeaderProps {
  /** Active module for the default trail (legacy Phase-1 usage). */
  module?: NavItem;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  /** Optional contextual chips/meta rendered under the description. */
  meta?: React.ReactNode;
  actions?: React.ReactNode;
  /**
   * Phase 2: explicit breadcrumb trail rendered after the "الرئيسية"
   * root, which always links to "/". The last crumb is the current page.
   */
  breadcrumbs?: BreadcrumbItem[];
  /** Legacy home-crumb click handler (Phase 1 dashboard). */
  onNavigateHome?: () => void;
}

const crumbLinkClass =
  "rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-semibold transition-colors hover:text-brand-700 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring";

/**
 * Consistent page header: breadcrumb trail, page title, contextual
 * description and an optional actions slot.
 */
export function PageHeader({
  module,
  title,
  description,
  meta,
  actions,
  breadcrumbs,
  onNavigateHome,
}: PageHeaderProps) {
  const legacyIsHome = module?.id === "home";

  return (
    <div className="mb-6">
      {/* Breadcrumb */}
      <nav aria-label="مسار التنقل" className="mb-2">
        <ol className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground" role="list">
          {/* Root crumb */}
          <li>
            {breadcrumbs || !onNavigateHome ? (
              <Link href="/" className={cn(crumbLinkClass, legacyIsHome && "text-foreground")} aria-current={legacyIsHome ? "page" : undefined}>
                الرئيسية
              </Link>
            ) : (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onNavigateHome}
                className={cn(crumbLinkClass, legacyIsHome && "text-foreground")}
                aria-current={legacyIsHome ? "page" : undefined}
              >
                الرئيسية
              </button>
            )}
          </li>

          {/* Legacy module crumb (Phase 1 usage) */}
          {!breadcrumbs && module && !legacyIsHome && (
            <>
              <li aria-hidden="true">
                <ChevronLeft className="size-3.5 text-muted-foreground/60" />
              </li>
              <li>
                <span className="rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-semibold text-foreground" aria-current="page">
                  {module.label}
                </span>
              </li>
            </>
          )}

          {/* Explicit Phase-2 trail */}
          {breadcrumbs?.map((crumb, index) => {
            const isLast = index === breadcrumbs.length - 1;
            return (
              <li key={`${crumb.label}-${index}`} className="flex items-center gap-1">
                <ChevronLeft className="size-3.5 text-muted-foreground/60" aria-hidden="true" />
                {crumb.href && !isLast ? (
                  <Link href={crumb.href} className={crumbLinkClass}>
                    {crumb.label}
                  </Link>
                ) : (
                  <span
                    className="rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-semibold text-foreground"
                    aria-current={isLast ? "page" : undefined}
                  >
                    {crumb.label}
                  </span>
                )}
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ol>
      </nav>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <h1 className="truncate text-2xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-foreground">
            {title}
          </h1>
          {description && (
            <p className="mt-1.5 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
              {description}
            </p>
          )}
          {meta && <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">{meta}</div>}
        </div>
        {actions && <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2">{actions}</div>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\layout\sidebar.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { Landmark } from "lucide-react";

import { BrandLogo } from "@/components/layout/brand-logo";
import { activeNavItemId } from "@/config/navigation";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { NavItem, PlatformBranding } from "@/lib/types";

interface SidebarProps {
  branding: PlatformBranding;
  items: NavItem[];
  className?: string;
}

/**
 * Visual grouping of the sidebar — presentation only. It never adds,
 * removes or re-orders modules: items still arrive role-filtered from
 * the server (navIdsForRole) in their config order, and any id that is
 * not listed below falls back to an unlabeled trailing group, so a
 * future module can never disappear from the UI by accident.
 */
const NAV_GROUPS: Array<{ label: string; members: string[] }> = [
  {
    label: "القائمة الرئيسية",
    members: ["home", "academy", "schools", "users", "supervision", "deputy-overview"],
  },
  {
    label: "التدريب والتقييم",
    members: [
      "programs",
      "course-management",
      "assessments",
      "assignments",
      "certificates",
      "reports",
    ],
  },
  {
    label: "حسابي",
    members: ["my-profile", "settings"],
  },
];

/** Group the role-filtered items, preserving config order inside each group. */
function groupNavItems(
  items: NavItem[]
): Array<{ label: string; items: NavItem[] }> {
  const known = new Set(NAV_GROUPS.flatMap((group) => group.members));
  const groups = NAV_GROUPS.map((group) => ({
    label: group.label,
    items: items.filter((item) => group.members.includes(item.id)),
  }));
  const leftovers = items.filter((item) => !known.has(item.id));
  if (leftovers.length > 0) groups.push({ label: "", items: leftovers });
  return groups.filter((group) => group.items.length > 0);
}

/**
 * Desktop application sidebar — premium navy rail on the inline-start
 * side, which in the RTL layout is the right edge of the screen.
 * Hidden on mobile (the mobile navigation Sheet takes over below lg).
 *
 * Active state uses the longest-href match, so nested routes highlight
 * exactly one item (e.g. /academy/manage/12 highlights "إدارة الدورات"
 * without also highlighting "الأكاديمية التدريبية").
 */
export function Sidebar({ branding, items, className }: SidebarProps) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const activeId = activeNavItemId(pathname, items);
  const groups = groupNavItems(items);

  return (
    <aside
      className={cn(
        "hidden lg:flex lg:w-72 lg:shrink-0 lg:flex-col",
        "sticky top-0 h-screen bg-sidebar text-sidebar-foreground",
        "border-e border-sidebar-border/70",
        className
      )}
    >
      {/* Brand */}
      <div className="flex h-16 shrink-0 items-center border-b border-sidebar-border/70 px-5">
        <BrandLogo branding={branding} variant="sidebar" />
      </div>

      {/* Navigation */}
      <nav
        aria-label="التنقل الرئيسي"
        className="scrollbar-thin flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 pb-4 pt-2"
      >
        {groups.map((group, groupIndex) => (
          <div
            key={group.label || `extra-${groupIndex}`}
            className={cn(groupIndex > 0 && "mt-5")}
          >
            {group.label && (
              <p className="px-3 pb-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-sidebar-foreground/40">
                {group.label}
              </p>
            )}
            <ul className="space-y-0.5" role="list">
              {group.items.map((item) => {
                const active = item.id === activeId;
                const Icon = item.icon;
                return (
                  <li key={item.id}>
                    <Link
                      href={item.href}
                      aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
                      className={cn(
                        "group flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors",
                        "focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-sidebar-ring",
                        active
                          ? "bg-gradient-to-l from-brand-600 to-brand-500 text-white shadow-md shadow-brand-900/30"
                          : "text-sidebar-foreground/75 hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-sidebar-accent-foreground"
                      )}
                    >
                      <Icon
                        className={cn(
                          "size-4.5 shrink-0",
                          active
                            ? "text-white"
                            : "text-sidebar-foreground/55 group-hover:text-sidebar-accent-foreground"
                        )}
                        strokeWidth={active ? 2.2 : 1.8}
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      />
                      <span className="flex-1 text-start">{item.label}</span>
                      {item.badge && !active && (
                        <span className="rounded-full bg-white/8 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-sidebar-foreground/70 ring-1 ring-white/10">
                          {item.badge}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </Link>
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
          </div>
        ))}
      </nav>

      {/* School workspace card — placeholder until tenant config arrives */}
      <div className="shrink-0 border-t border-sidebar-border/70 p-4">
        <div className="rounded-xl bg-white/[0.06] p-3.5 ring-1 ring-white/10">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-white/10">
              <Landmark className="size-4 text-brand-100" aria-hidden="true" />
            </div>
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 leading-tight">
              <p className="truncate text-xs font-bold text-sidebar-foreground">
                {branding.currentSchool?.name ?? "مساحة عمل المدرسة"}
              </p>
              <p className="truncate text-[11px] text-sidebar-foreground/60">
                تخصيص هوية المدرسة — قريباً
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-3 text-center text-[11px] text-sidebar-foreground/40">
          {branding.productName} · الإصدار 0.2.0
        </p>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\layout\topbar.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import {
  Bell,
  CheckCheck,
  ChevronDown,
  LogOut,
  Menu,
  Search,
  Settings,
  UserRound,
} from "lucide-react";

import { Avatar, AvatarFallback } from "@/components/ui/avatar";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuLabel,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import {
  Popover,
  PopoverAnchor,
  PopoverContent,
  PopoverTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/popover";
import { Separator } from "@/components/ui/separator";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { logoutAction } from "@/app/actions/auth";
import { mockNotifications } from "@/lib/mock-data";
import type {
  AppNotification,
  CurrentUser,
  NavItem,
} from "@/lib/types";

interface TopbarProps {
  user: CurrentUser;
  items: NavItem[];
  onOpenMobileNav: () => void;
}

/**
 * Top application bar: clean white surface with the module search,
 * notifications, and the user identity + menu. Every visible control
 * performs a real action (navigation, local state change, or an
 * explicit "coming in a later phase" notice) — no dead buttons.
 *
 * The user-menu "الإعدادات" entry renders only for roles whose
 * role-filtered `items` actually include the settings module — the
 * sidebar and the user menu can never disagree again.
 */
export function Topbar({
  user,
  items,
  onOpenMobileNav,
}: TopbarProps) {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [query, setQuery] = React.useState("");
  const [searchOpen, setSearchOpen] = React.useState(false);
  const [notificationList, setNotificationList] =
    React.useState<AppNotification[]>(mockNotifications);
  const unreadCount = notificationList.filter((n) => !n.read).length;
  const canSeeSettings = items.some((item) => item.id === "settings");

  const normalizedQuery = query.trim();
  const searchResults =
    normalizedQuery.length > 0
      ? items.filter(
          (item) =>
            item.label.includes(normalizedQuery) ||
            item.description.includes(normalizedQuery)
        )
      : items;

  function handleNavigate(href: string) {
    setSearchOpen(false);
    setQuery("");
    router.push(href);
  }

  function markAllRead() {
    setNotificationList((prev) => prev.map((n) => ({ ...n, read: true })));
  }

  const laterPhaseNotice = (feature: string) =>
    toast({
      title: `${feature} — قيد التطوير`,
      description: "هذه الميزة جزء من مرحلة لاحقة من خطة التطوير.",
    });

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 flex h-16 items-center gap-2 border-b border-border/70 bg-card/90 px-4 backdrop-blur-md sm:gap-3 sm:px-6">
      {/* Mobile menu */}
      <Button
        variant="ghost"
        size="icon"
        className="lg:hidden"
        onClick={onOpenMobileNav}
        aria-label="فتح قائمة التنقل"
      >
        <Menu className="size-5" />
      </Button>

      {/* Module search */}
      <Popover open={searchOpen} onOpenChange={setSearchOpen}>
        <PopoverAnchor asChild>
          <div className="relative hidden flex-1 md:block md:max-w-md">
            <Search
              className="pointer-events-none absolute start-3.5 top-1/2 size-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground"
              aria-hidden="true"
            />
            <Input
              value={query}
              onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
              onFocus={() => setSearchOpen(true)}
              placeholder="ابحث عن صفحة أو ميزة…"
              className="h-10 rounded-xl border-transparent bg-muted/60 ps-10 text-sm transition-colors hover:bg-muted/80 focus-visible:bg-card"
              aria-label="البحث في المنصة"
            />
          </div>
        </PopoverAnchor>
        <PopoverContent
          align="start"
          className="w-[min(26rem,92vw)] rounded-xl p-2"
          onOpenAutoFocus={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
        >
          <ul className="scrollbar-thin max-h-72 overflow-y-auto" role="listbox">
            {searchResults.length === 0 ? (
              <li className="px-3 py-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                لا توجد نتائج مطابقة لـ «{normalizedQuery}»
              </li>
            ) : (
              searchResults.map((item) => {
                const Icon = item.icon;
                return (
                  <li key={item.id}>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      role="option"
                      aria-selected="false"
                      onClick={() => handleNavigate(item.href)}
                      className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 text-start transition-colors hover:bg-accent focus-visible:bg-accent focus-visible:outline-none"
                    >
                      <span className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-brand-50 text-brand-700">
                        <Icon className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                      </span>
                      <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <span className="block truncate text-sm font-semibold">
                          {item.label}
                        </span>
                        <span className="block truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          {item.description}
                        </span>
                      </span>
                      {item.badge && (
                        <span className="rounded-full bg-muted px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-muted-foreground">
                          {item.badge}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </button>
                  </li>
                );
              })
            )}
          </ul>
        </PopoverContent>
      </Popover>

      {/* Flank (start side): balances the end flank so the search + notifications group stays centered (RTL-safe). */}
      <div className="flex-1" aria-hidden="true" />

      {/* Notifications */}
      <Popover>
        <PopoverTrigger asChild>
          <Button
            variant="ghost"
            size="icon"
            className="relative rounded-xl"
            aria-label={`الإشعارات${unreadCount > 0 ? ` (${unreadCount} غير مقروءة)` : ""}`}
          >
            <Bell className="size-5" />
            {unreadCount > 0 && (
              <span
                className="absolute end-1.5 top-1.5 flex size-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-error text-[10px] font-bold text-white"
                aria-hidden="true"
              >
                {unreadCount}
              </span>
            )}
          </Button>
        </PopoverTrigger>
        <PopoverContent align="end" className="w-[min(22rem,92vw)] rounded-xl p-0">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b px-4 py-3">
            <p className="text-sm font-bold">الإشعارات</p>
            {unreadCount > 0 && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={markAllRead}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-brand-600 hover:text-brand-700"
              >
                <CheckCheck className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                تحديد الكل كمقروء
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
          <ul className="scrollbar-thin max-h-80 overflow-y-auto" role="list">
            {notificationList.length === 0 ? (
              <li className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                لا توجد إشعارات حالياً
              </li>
            ) : (
              notificationList.map((n) => (
                <li
                  key={n.id}
                  className={cn(
                    "border-b px-4 py-3 last:border-b-0",
                    !n.read && "bg-brand-50/70"
                  )}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
                    <span
                      className={cn(
                        "mt-1.5 size-2 shrink-0 rounded-full",
                        n.read ? "bg-muted" : "bg-brand-600"
                      )}
                      aria-hidden="true"
                    />
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <p className="text-sm font-semibold leading-snug">{n.title}</p>
                      {n.description && (
                        <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
                          {n.description}
                        </p>
                      )}
                      <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground/70">{n.time}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </li>
              ))
            )}
          </ul>
          <div className="border-t px-4 py-2.5 text-center">
            <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              بيانات إشعارات تجريبية — الإشعارات الفعلية في مرحلة لاحقة
            </span>
          </div>
        </PopoverContent>
      </Popover>

      <Separator orientation="vertical" className="hidden h-8 sm:block" />

      {/* User identity + menu */}
      <DropdownMenu>
        <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-xl p-1.5 transition-colors hover:bg-accent focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring"
            aria-label="قائمة المستخدم"
          >
            <Avatar className="size-9">
              <AvatarFallback className="bg-gradient-to-br from-brand-600 to-brand-800 text-xs font-bold text-white">
                {user.initials}
              </AvatarFallback>
            </Avatar>
            <span className="hidden min-w-0 text-start leading-tight sm:block">
              <span className="block truncate text-sm font-bold">{user.name}</span>
              <span className="block truncate text-[11px] font-medium text-muted-foreground">
                {user.role}
              </span>
            </span>
            <ChevronDown className="hidden size-4 text-muted-foreground sm:block" aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </DropdownMenuTrigger>
        <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="w-64 rounded-xl">
          <DropdownMenuLabel className="px-3 py-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <Avatar className="size-10">
                <AvatarFallback className="bg-gradient-to-br from-brand-600 to-brand-800 text-xs font-bold text-white">
                  {user.initials}
                </AvatarFallback>
              </Avatar>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <p className="truncate text-sm font-bold">{user.name}</p>
                <p className="truncate text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">
                  {user.email}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </DropdownMenuLabel>
          <DropdownMenuSeparator />
          <DropdownMenuItem asChild>
            <Link href="/my-profile" className="cursor-pointer">
              <UserRound className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              الملف الشخصي
            </Link>
          </DropdownMenuItem>
          {canSeeSettings && (
            <DropdownMenuItem onSelect={() => handleNavigate("/settings")}>
              <Settings className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              الإعدادات
            </DropdownMenuItem>
          )}
          <DropdownMenuSeparator />
          {/* Real logout: the server action clears the session cookie. */}
          <form action={logoutAction}>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="flex w-full cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-sm px-2 py-1.5 text-sm text-error outline-none transition-colors hover:bg-error-soft focus:bg-error-soft"
            >
              <LogOut className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              تسجيل الخروج
            </button>
          </form>
        </DropdownMenuContent>
      </DropdownMenu>
    </header>
  );
}

import type { LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface EmptyStateProps {
  icon: LucideIcon;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  /** Optional call-to-action rendered under the description. */
  action?: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}

/** Consistent empty-state block for lists and future module views. */
export function EmptyState({
  icon: Icon,
  title,
  description,
  action,
  className,
}: EmptyStateProps) {
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-dashed bg-muted/40 px-6 py-10 text-center",
        className
      )}
    >
      <div
        className="mb-3 flex size-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-brand-100 text-brand-700"
        aria-hidden="true"
      >
        <Icon className="size-6" strokeWidth={1.8} />
      </div>

        <p className="mt-1 max-w-sm text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
          {description}
        </p>
      )}
      {action && <div className="mt-4">{action}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\platform\module-placeholder-route.tsx =====
"use client";

import { ModulePlaceholder } from "@/components/platform/module-placeholder";
import { getNavItemId } from "@/config/navigation";

interface ModulePlaceholderRouteProps {
  moduleId: string;
}

/**
 * Client bridge for placeholder module routes. Server pages resolve the
 * serializable moduleId; the icon-bearing NavItem is resolved here on
 * the client (icons are component references and cannot cross the
 * server/client serialization boundary).
 */
export function ModulePlaceholderRoute({ moduleId }: ModulePlaceholderRouteProps) {
  const navItem = getNavItemId(moduleId);
  if (!navItem) return null;
  return <ModulePlaceholder module={navItem} />;
}

          </p>

          {module.plannedFeatures.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-6 w-full max-w-md rounded-lg border bg-muted/40 p-4 text-start">
