import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Separator } from "@/components/ui/separator";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface SectionCardProps {
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  /** Optional control rendered at the end of the header row. */
  action?: React.ReactNode;
  /** Optional footer rendered after a separator at the card bottom. */
  footer?: React.ReactNode;
  contentClassName?: string;
  className?: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

/**
 * Shared dashboard panel: card with a consistent header (title,
 * optional description and action) and an optional footer.
 */
export function SectionCard({
  title,
  description,
  action,
  footer,
  contentClassName,
  className,
  children,
}: SectionCardProps) {
  return (
    <Card
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-col gap-0 rounded-xl border bg-card py-0 shadow-xs",
        className
      )}
    >
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 px-5 pt-5 pb-4 sm:px-6">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-foreground">{title}</h2>
          {description && (
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{description}</p>
          )}
        </div>
        {action && <div className="shrink-0">{action}</div>}
      </div>
      <CardContent className={cn("px-5 pb-5 sm:px-6", contentClassName)}>
        {children}
      </CardContent>
      {footer && (
        <>
          <Separator />
          <div className="px-5 py-3 sm:px-6">{footer}</div>
        </>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\platform\stat-card.tsx =====
import { TrendingDown, TrendingUp } from "lucide-react";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { AccentColor, OverviewStat } from "@/lib/types";

interface StatCardProps {
  stat: OverviewStat;
  onClick?: () => void;
  className?: string;
}

const accentStyles: Record<AccentColor, { tile: string; trend: string }> = {
  primary: {
    tile: "bg-brand-100 text-brand-700",
    trend: "text-success",
  },
  info: {
    tile: "bg-info-soft text-info",
    trend: "text-success",
  },
  success: {
    tile: "bg-success-soft text-success",
    trend: "text-success",
  },
  warning: {
    tile: "bg-warning-soft text-warning",
    trend: "text-warning",
  },
};

/**
 * Dashboard overview statistic card. Clicking navigates to the related
 * module, so the card is a real control — not a dead tile.
 */
export function StatCard({ stat, onClick, className }: StatCardProps) {
  const Icon = stat.icon;
  const styles = accentStyles[stat.accent];
  const TrendIcon = stat.trend.direction === "down" ? TrendingDown : TrendingUp;

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      aria-label={`${stat.label}: ${stat.value}${stat.suffix ?? ""} — انتقل إلى القسم المرتبط`}
      className={cn(
        "group flex flex-col gap-4 rounded-xl border bg-card p-5 text-start shadow-xs transition-all",
        "hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-brand-200 hover:shadow-md",
        "focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2",
        className
      )}
    >
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
        <div
          className={cn(
            "flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-lg",
            styles.tile
          )}
          aria-hidden="true"
        >
          <Icon className="size-5.5" strokeWidth={1.9} />
        </div>
        <span
          className={cn(
            "inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold",
            stat.trend.direction === "down"
              ? "bg-warning-soft text-warning"
              : "bg-success-soft text-success"
          )}
        >
          <TrendIcon className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
          {stat.trend.value}%
        </span>
      </div>

      <div>
        <p className="text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-foreground tabular-nums">
          {stat.value.toLocaleString("en-US")}
          {stat.suffix && (
            <span className="ms-1 text-base font-bold text-muted-foreground">
              {stat.suffix}
            </span>
          )}
        </p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-foreground/80">{stat.label}</p>
        <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{stat.trend.label}</p>
      </div>
    </button>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\platform\status-badge.tsx =====
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export type StatusTone = "success" | "info" | "warning" | "error" | "neutral";

interface StatusBadgeProps extends React.ComponentProps<"span"> {
  tone: StatusTone;
  /** Optional leading dot rendered before the label. */
  withDot?: boolean;
}

/**
 * Semantic status badge built on the platform's status tokens
 * (success / info / warning / error / neutral) instead of scattered
 * hardcoded color classes.
 */
export function StatusBadge({
  tone,
  withDot = true,
  className,
  children,
  ...props
}: StatusBadgeProps) {
  return (
    <span
      className={cn(
        "inline-flex w-fit items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold whitespace-nowrap",
        tone === "success" && "bg-success-soft text-success",
        tone === "info" && "bg-info-soft text-info",
        tone === "warning" && "bg-warning-soft text-warning",
        tone === "error" && "bg-error-soft text-error",
        tone === "neutral" && "bg-muted text-muted-foreground",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {withDot && (
        <span
          aria-hidden="true"
          className={cn(
            "size-1.5 rounded-full",
            tone === "success" && "bg-success",
            tone === "info" && "bg-info",
            tone === "warning" && "bg-warning",
            tone === "error" && "bg-error",
            tone === "neutral" && "bg-muted-foreground"
          )}
        />
      )}
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\platform\submit-button.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useFormStatus } from "react-dom";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface SubmitButtonProps extends React.ComponentProps<"button"> {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

/**
 * Shared form-submit button with a pending state (react-dom
 * useFormStatus) — used by server-action forms across the platform.
 */
export function SubmitButton({ children, className, disabled, ...props }: SubmitButtonProps) {
  const { pending } = useFormStatus();
  return (
    <button
      type="submit"
      disabled={pending || disabled}
      className={cn(
        "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-bold transition-colors disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {pending ? "جارٍ التنفيذ…" : children}
    </button>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\schools\branches-manager.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import {
  ChevronRight,
  Loader2,
  Pencil,
  Plus,
  School as SchoolIcon,
} from "lucide-react";

import {
  createBranchAction,
  setBranchActiveAction,
  updateBranchAction,
} from "@/app/actions/schools";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from "@/components/ui/table";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/platform/status-badge";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/platform/empty-state";
import { SectionCard } from "@/components/platform/section-card";
import { branchStageLabel, BRANCH_STAGES } from "@/lib/validation/schools";
import type { BranchListItem } from "@/lib/schools/dto";

interface BranchesManagerProps {
  schoolId: string;
  branches: BranchListItem[];
}

interface BranchDialogState {
  open: boolean;
  editing: BranchListItem | null;
}

/** Draft form values for the branch dialog (kept in local state). */
interface BranchFormValues {
  nameAr: string;
  nameEn: string;
  stage: string;
  isActive: boolean;
}

const emptyForm: BranchFormValues = {
  nameAr: "",
  nameEn: "",
  stage: "primary",
  isActive: true,
};

/**
 * Branch management inside the school details view: list, create, edit,
 * change stage and activate/deactivate. Every mutation runs through the
 * server actions, which re-validate the branch↔school relationship on
 * the server — a branch can never be attached to a different school.
 */
export function BranchesManager({ schoolId, branches }: BranchesManagerProps) {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [dialog, setDialog] = React.useState<BranchDialogState>({
    open: false,
    editing: null,
  });
  const [form, setForm] = React.useState<BranchFormValues>(emptyForm);
  const [formError, setFormError] = React.useState<string | null>(null);
  const [fieldErrors, setFieldErrors] = React.useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [saving, setSaving] = React.useState(false);
  const [togglingId, setTogglingId] = React.useState<string | null>(null);

  function openCreate() {
    setForm(emptyForm);
    setFormError(null);
    setFieldErrors({});
    setDialog({ open: true, editing: null });
  }

  function openEdit(branch: BranchListItem) {
    setForm({
      nameAr: branch.nameAr,
      nameEn: branch.nameEn ?? "",
      stage: branch.stage,
      isActive: branch.isActive,
    });
    setFormError(null);
    setFieldErrors({});
    setDialog({ open: true, editing: branch });
  }

  function closeDialog() {
    setDialog({ open: false, editing: null });
  }

  async function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true);
    setFormError(null);
    setFieldErrors({});

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.set("nameAr", form.nameAr);
    formData.set("nameEn", form.nameEn);
    formData.set("stage", form.stage);
    if (!dialog.editing) formData.set("isActive", form.isActive ? "on" : "off");
    if (dialog.editing) formData.set("branchId", dialog.editing.id);

    const result = dialog.editing
      ? await updateBranchAction(schoolId, formData)
      : await createBranchAction(schoolId, formData);

    setSaving(false);

    if (result?.ok) {
      toast({
        title: dialog.editing ? "تم تحديث الفرع" : "تم إضافة الفرع",
        description: `«${form.nameAr}» — ${branchStageLabel(form.stage)}`,
      });
      closeDialog();
    } else {
      setFormError(result?.message ?? "حدث خطأ غير متوقع — أعد المحاولة");
      if (result?.fieldErrors) setFieldErrors(result.fieldErrors);
    }
  }

  function handleToggle(branch: BranchListItem) {
    setTogglingId(branch.id);
    const next = !branch.isActive;
    // Optimistic-free approach: wait for the server result, then let
    // revalidation refresh the row state.
    (async () => {
      const result = await setBranchActiveAction(schoolId, branch.id, next);
      setTogglingId(null);
      if (result?.ok) {
        toast({
          title: next ? "تم تفعيل الفرع" : "تم إيقاف الفرع",
          description: `«${branch.nameAr}»`,
        });
      } else {
        toast({
          title: "تعذر تغيير حالة الفرع",
          description: result?.message ?? "حدث خطأ غير متوقع — أعد المحاولة",
          variant: "destructive",
        });
      }
    })();
  }

  return (
    <SectionCard
      title="فروع المدرسة"
      description="الفروع التعليمية التابعة لهذه المدرسة ومراحلها — أسماء الفروع فريدة داخل المدرسة."
      action={
        <Button size="sm" onClick={openCreate}>
          <Plus className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          إضافة فرع
        </Button>
      }
    >
      {branches.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={SchoolIcon}
          title="لا توجد فروع بعد"
          description="أضف أول فرع لهذه المدرسة وحدّد مرحلته التعليمية: ابتدائية أو إعدادية أو ثانوية."
          action={
            <Button size="sm" onClick={openCreate}>
              <Plus className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              إضافة فرع
            </Button>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* Desktop table */}
          <div className="hidden rounded-xl border md:block">
            <Table>
              <TableHeader>
                <TableRow className="hover:bg-transparent">
                  <TableHead className="py-3 ps-4">الفرع</TableHead>
                  <TableHead className="py-3">المرحلة التعليمية</TableHead>
                  <TableHead className="py-3">الحالة</TableHead>
                  <TableHead className="py-3">أُضيف في</TableHead>
                  <TableHead className="py-3 pe-4 text-end">إجراءات</TableHead>
                </TableRow>
              </TableHeader>
              <TableBody>
                {branches.map((branch) => (
                  <TableRow key={branch.id}>
                    <TableCell className="py-3 ps-4">
                      <p className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground">{branch.nameAr}</p>
                      {branch.nameEn && (
                        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground" dir="ltr">
                          {branch.nameEn}
                        </p>
                      )}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="py-3">
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-brand-50 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-brand-700">
                        {branchStageLabel(branch.stage)}
                      </span>
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="py-3">
                      {branch.isActive ? (
                        <StatusBadge tone="success">نشط</StatusBadge>
                      ) : (
                        <StatusBadge tone="neutral">موقوف</StatusBadge>
                      )}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="py-3">
                      <span className="text-sm tabular-nums text-muted-foreground">
                        {branch.createdAtLabel}
                      </span>
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="py-3 pe-4">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
                        <Switch
                          checked={branch.isActive}
                          onCheckedChange={() => handleToggle(branch)}
                          disabled={togglingId === branch.id}
                          aria-label={
                            branch.isActive
                              ? `إيقاف فرع ${branch.nameAr}`
                              : `تفعيل فرع ${branch.nameAr}`
                          }
                        />
                        <Button
                          variant="outline"
                          size="sm"
                          onClick={() => openEdit(branch)}
                        >
                          <Pencil className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                          تعديل
                        </Button>
                      </div>
                    </TableCell>
                  </TableRow>
                ))}
              </TableBody>
            </Table>
          </div>

          {/* Mobile cards */}
          <ul className="space-y-3 md:hidden" role="list" aria-label="قائمة الفروع">
            {branches.map((branch) => (
              <li key={branch.id} className="rounded-xl border p-4">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                  <div className="min-w-0 leading-tight">
                    <p className="truncate text-sm font-bold text-foreground">
                      {branch.nameAr}
                    </p>
                    {branch.nameEn && (
                      <p className="truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground" dir="ltr">
                        {branch.nameEn}
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  {branch.isActive ? (
                    <StatusBadge tone="success">نشط</StatusBadge>
                  ) : (
                    <StatusBadge tone="neutral">موقوف</StatusBadge>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-between border-t pt-3">
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-brand-50 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-brand-700">
                    {branchStageLabel(branch.stage)}
                  </span>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Switch
                      checked={branch.isActive}
                      onCheckedChange={() => handleToggle(branch)}
                      disabled={togglingId === branch.id}
                      aria-label={branch.isActive ? `إيقاف فرع ${branch.nameAr}` : `تفعيل فرع ${branch.nameAr}`}
                    />
                    <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => openEdit(branch)}>
                      <Pencil className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                      تعديل
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </>
      )}

      {/* Create / edit dialog */}
      <Dialog open={dialog.open} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && closeDialog()}>
        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg" dir="rtl">
          <DialogHeader className="text-start">
            <DialogTitle>
              {dialog.editing ? `تعديل فرع «${dialog.editing.nameAr}»` : "إضافة فرع جديد"}
            </DialogTitle>
            <DialogDescription>
              الفروع تابعة دائماً للمدرسة الحالية — لا يمكن نقل فرع إلى مدرسة أخرى من هذه الواجهة.
            </DialogDescription>
          </DialogHeader>

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
            {formError && (
              <p
                className="rounded-lg border border-error/30 bg-error-soft px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-error"
                role="alert"
              >
                {formError}
              </p>
            )}

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="branch-nameAr">
                اسم الفرع بالعربية <span className="text-error">*</span>
              </Label>
              <Input
                id="branch-nameAr"
                value={form.nameAr}
                onChange={(event) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, nameAr: event.target.value }))}
                placeholder="مثال: فرع المنطقة الأولى"
                required
                aria-invalid={Boolean(fieldErrors.nameAr)}
              />
              {fieldErrors.nameAr && (
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-error" role="alert">
                  {fieldErrors.nameAr}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label htmlFor="branch-nameEn">
                  الاسم بالإنجليزية
                  <span className="ms-1 text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">(اختياري)</span>
                </Label>
                <Input
                  id="branch-nameEn"
                  value={form.nameEn}
                  onChange={(event) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, nameEn: event.target.value }))}
                  placeholder="First District Branch"
                  dir="ltr"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label htmlFor="branch-stage">المرحلة التعليمية</Label>
                <Select
                  value={form.stage}
                  onValueChange={(value) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, stage: value }))}
                >
                  <SelectTrigger id="branch-stage" aria-label="المرحلة التعليمية">
                    <SelectValue placeholder="اختر المرحلة" />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent>
                    {BRANCH_STAGES.map((stage) => (
                      <SelectItem key={stage} value={stage}>
                        {branchStageLabel(stage)}
                      </SelectItem>
                    ))}
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </div>
            </div>

            {!dialog.editing && (
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border bg-muted/40 p-3.5">
                <div className="space-y-0.5">
                  <Label htmlFor="branch-active" className="text-sm font-bold">
                    تفعيل الفرع بعد الإضافة
                  </Label>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    الفروع الموقوفة تظهر في القائمة لكنها غير جاهزة للتشغيل.
                  </p>
                </div>
                <Switch
                  id="branch-active"
                  checked={form.isActive}
                  onCheckedChange={(checked) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, isActive: checked }))}
                  aria-label="تفعيل الفرع بعد الإضافة"
                />
              </div>
            )}

            <DialogFooter className="gap-2 sm:justify-start">
              <Button type="submit" disabled={saving}>
                {saving && <Loader2 className="size-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />}
                {dialog.editing ? "حفظ التعديلات" : "إضافة الفرع"}
              </Button>
              <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={closeDialog}>
                إلغاء
              </Button>
            </DialogFooter>
          </form>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>

      {/* Back to directory */}
      <p className="mt-4 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        <Link
          href="/schools"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-semibold text-brand-600 transition-colors hover:text-brand-700"
        >
          <ChevronRight className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
          العودة إلى دليل المدارس
        </Link>
      </p>
    </SectionCard>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\schools\school-form.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useFormStatus } from "react-dom";
import { useActionState } from "react";
import { AlertCircle, CheckCircle2, ImageIcon, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";

import { createSchoolAction, updateSchoolAction } from "@/app/actions/schools";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { slugify } from "@/lib/validation/schools";
import { schoolInitials, type SchoolListItem } from "@/lib/schools/dto";

interface SchoolFormProps {
  mode: "create" | "edit";
  initial?: SchoolListItem;
}

const PLATFORM_DEFAULT_COLOR = "#1D5BD6";

function SubmitButton({ mode }: { mode: SchoolFormProps["mode"] }) {
  const { pending } = useFormStatus();
  return (
    <Button type="submit" disabled={pending}>
      {pending && <Loader2 className="size-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />}
      {mode === "create" ? "إنشاء المدرسة" : "حفظ التعديلات"}
    </Button>
  );
}

function FieldError({ message }: { message?: string }) {
  if (!message) return null;
  return (
    <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-error" role="alert">
      {message}
    </p>
  );
}

/**
 * Create/edit school form. All validation runs again on the server
 * (server action + Zod); field errors below mirror the server result.
 */
export function SchoolForm({ mode, initial }: SchoolFormProps) {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const action = mode === "create" ? createSchoolAction : updateSchoolAction;
  const [state, formAction] = useActionState(action, null);

  const [nameEn, setNameEn] = React.useState(initial?.nameEn ?? "");
  const [slug, setSlug] = React.useState(initial?.slug ?? "");
  const [slugEdited, setSlugEdited] = React.useState(mode === "edit");
  const [logoUrl, setLogoUrl] = React.useState(initial?.logoUrl ?? "");
  const [logoBroken, setLogoBroken] = React.useState(false);
  const [brandColor, setBrandColor] = React.useState(
    initial?.brandColor ?? PLATFORM_DEFAULT_COLOR
  );
  const [isActive, setIsActive] = React.useState(initial?.isActive ?? true);

  // Edit mode: surface the success feedback (create mode redirects).
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (state?.ok) {
      toast({
        title: "تم حفظ التعديلات",
        description: "تم تحديث بيانات المدرسة بنجاح.",
      });
    }
  }, [state, toast]);

  const fieldErrors = state && "fieldErrors" in state ? state.fieldErrors : undefined;
  const formMessage = state && "message" in state && !state.ok ? state.message : undefined;

  return (
    <form action={formAction} className="space-y-6">
      {mode === "edit" && <input type="hidden" name="id" value={initial?.id} />}

      {formMessage && (
        <Alert variant="destructive">
          <AlertCircle className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          <AlertDescription>{formMessage}</AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      )}

      {/* Names */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="nameAr">
            اسم المدرسة بالعربية <span className="text-error">*</span>
          </Label>
          <Input
            id="nameAr"
            name="nameAr"
            defaultValue={initial?.nameAr}
            placeholder="مثال: مدرسة قرطبة النموذجية"
            required
            aria-invalid={Boolean(fieldErrors?.nameAr)}
            className={cn(fieldErrors?.nameAr && "border-error focus-visible:ring-error/30")}
          />
          <FieldError message={fieldErrors?.nameAr} />
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="nameEn">
            اسم المدرسة بالإنجليزية
            <span className="ms-1 text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">(اختياري)</span>
          </Label>
          <Input
            id="nameEn"
            name="nameEn"
            value={nameEn}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setNameEn(event.target.value);
              if (!slugEdited) setSlug(slugify(event.target.value));
            }}
            placeholder="Qurtuba Model School"
            dir="ltr"
            aria-invalid={Boolean(fieldErrors?.nameEn)}
            className={cn(fieldErrors?.nameEn && "border-error focus-visible:ring-error/30")}
          />
          <FieldError message={fieldErrors?.nameEn} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Slug */}
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="slug">
          الرابط المختصر (Slug)
          <span className="ms-1 text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">
            — يُولَّد تلقائياً من الاسم الإنجليزي ويمكن تعديله
          </span>
        </Label>
        <Input
          id="slug"
          name="slug"
          value={slug}
          onChange={(event) => {
            setSlugEdited(true);
            setSlug(event.target.value);
          }}
          placeholder="qurtuba-school"
          dir="ltr"
          aria-invalid={Boolean(fieldErrors?.slug)}
          className={cn(
            "font-mono text-sm",
            fieldErrors?.slug && "border-error focus-visible:ring-error/30"
          )}
        />
        <FieldError message={fieldErrors?.slug} />
      </div>

      {/* Logo + brand color */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="logoUrl">
            رابط شعار المدرسة
            <span className="ms-1 text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">(اختياري)</span>
          </Label>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            {/* Preview + fallback */}
            {logoUrl && !logoBroken ? (
              <img
                src={logoUrl}
                alt="معاينة الشعار"
                className="size-12 shrink-0 rounded-xl border bg-card object-contain p-1"
                onError={() => setLogoBroken(true)}
              />
            ) : (
              <span
                className="flex size-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-white"
                style={{ backgroundColor: brandColor }}
                aria-hidden="true"
              >
                {initial?.nameAr ? (
                  schoolInitials(initial.nameAr)
                ) : (
                  <ImageIcon className="size-5" />
                )}
              </span>
            )}
            <Input
              id="logoUrl"
              name="logoUrl"
              value={logoUrl}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setLogoUrl(event.target.value);
                setLogoBroken(false);
              }}
              placeholder="https://example.com/logo.png"
              dir="ltr"
              aria-invalid={Boolean(fieldErrors?.logoUrl)}
              className={cn(
                "flex-1 text-sm",
                fieldErrors?.logoUrl && "border-error focus-visible:ring-error/30"
              )}
            />
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            الصق رابط صورة الشعار (PNG/SVG). رفع الملفات مباشرة متاح في مرحلة لاحقة عند ربط نظام تخزين الملفات.
          </p>
          <FieldError message={fieldErrors?.logoUrl} />
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="brandColor">لون الهوية الأساسي</Label>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <input
              type="color"
              value={/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(brandColor) ? brandColor : PLATFORM_DEFAULT_COLOR}
              onChange={(event) => setBrandColor(event.target.value)}
              className="size-10 shrink-0 cursor-pointer rounded-lg border bg-card p-1"
              aria-label="اختيار لون الهوية"
            />
            <Input
              id="brandColor"
              name="brandColor"
              value={brandColor}
              onChange={(event) => setBrandColor(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="#1D5BD6"
              dir="ltr"
              aria-invalid={Boolean(fieldErrors?.brandColor)}
              className={cn(
                "font-mono text-sm",
                fieldErrors?.brandColor && "border-error focus-visible:ring-error/30"
              )}
            />
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            الافتراضي أزرق المنصة ({PLATFORM_DEFAULT_COLOR}). يُستخدم للشعار البديل وعناصر هوية المدرسة.
          </p>
          <FieldError message={fieldErrors?.brandColor} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Initial status (create mode only; editing uses the status toggle) */}
      {mode === "create" && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border bg-card p-4">
          <div className="space-y-0.5">
            <Label htmlFor="isActive" className="text-sm font-bold">
              تفعيل المدرسة بعد الإنشاء
            </Label>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              المدارس الموقوفة تظهر في الدليل لكنها غير جاهزة للتشغيل.
            </p>
          </div>
          {/* Hidden input feeds the server action (Radix Switch renders a button, not a checkbox) */}
          <input type="hidden" name="isActive" value={isActive ? "on" : "off"} />
          <Switch
            id="isActive"
            checked={isActive}
            onCheckedChange={setIsActive}
            aria-label="تفعيل المدرسة بعد الإنشاء"
          />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Actions */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-t pt-5">
        <SubmitButton mode={mode} />
        <Button variant="outline" asChild>
          <Link href={mode === "edit" ? `/schools/${initial?.id}` : "/schools"}>إلغاء</Link>
        </Button>
        {state?.ok && (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-success">
            <CheckCircle2 className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            تم الحفظ بنجاح
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\schools\school-status-toggle.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { Loader2, Power } from "lucide-react";

import { setSchoolActiveAction } from "@/app/actions/schools";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";

interface SchoolStatusToggleProps {
  schoolId: string;
  schoolName: string;
  isActive: boolean;
}

/**
 * Activate/deactivate a school. Permanent deletion is deliberately not
 * offered — schools may own branches (and future teacher/program
 * records), so deactivation preserves history and referential integrity.
 */
export function SchoolStatusToggle({
  schoolId,
  schoolName,
  isActive,
}: SchoolStatusToggleProps) {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [pending, startTransition] = React.useTransition();

  function handleToggle() {
    const next = !isActive;
    startTransition(async () => {
      const result = await setSchoolActiveAction(schoolId, next);
      if (result?.ok) {
        toast({
          title: next ? "تم تفعيل المدرسة" : "تم إيقاف المدرسة",
          description: next
            ? `«${schoolName}» نشطة الآن ويمكن التشغيل عليها.`
            : `«${schoolName}» موقوفة مؤقتاً — لم يتم حذف أي بيانات.`,
        });
      } else {
        toast({
          title: "تعذر تغيير الحالة",
          description: result?.message ?? "حدث خطأ غير متوقع — أعد المحاولة.",
          variant: "destructive",
        });
      }
    });
  }

  return (
    <Button
      variant={isActive ? "outline" : "default"}
      onClick={handleToggle}
      disabled={pending}
      className={isActive ? "border-warning/40 text-warning hover:bg-warning-soft hover:text-warning" : ""}
    >
      {pending ? (
        <Loader2 className="size-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
      ) : (
        <Power className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
      )}
      {isActive ? "إيقاف المدرسة" : "إعادة تفعيل المدرسة"}
    </Button>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\schools\schools-directory.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ChevronLeft, Plus, Search, School as SchoolIcon } from "lucide-react";

import { EmptyState } from "@/components/platform/empty-state";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/platform/status-badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from "@/components/ui/table";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { schoolInitials, type SchoolListItem } from "@/lib/schools/dto";

type StatusFilter = "all" | "active" | "inactive";

const statusFilters: { value: StatusFilter; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "all", label: "الكل" },
  { value: "active", label: "النشطة" },
  { value: "inactive", label: "الموقوفة" },
];

/** Fallback avatar: initials on a brand-colored tile. */
function SchoolAvatar({ school }: { school: SchoolListItem }) {
  return school.logoUrl ? (
    <img
      src={school.logoUrl}
      alt={`شعار ${school.nameAr}`}
      className="size-10 shrink-0 rounded-full border bg-card object-cover"
      onError={(event) => {
        event.currentTarget.style.display = "none";
      }}
    />
  ) : (
    <span
      className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs font-bold text-white"
      style={{ backgroundColor: school.brandColor }}
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      {schoolInitials(school.nameAr)}
    </span>
  );
}

/**
 * Schools directory: real-time client-side search (Arabic/English/slug)
 * and status filtering over the persisted list. Desktop renders a
 * table; small screens render stacked cards.
 */
export function SchoolsDirectory({ schools }: { schools: SchoolListItem[] }) {
  const [query, setQuery] = React.useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = React.useState<StatusFilter>("all");

  const normalizedQuery = query.trim();
  const filtered = React.useMemo(() => {
    const q = normalizedQuery.toLowerCase();
    return schools.filter((school) => {
      const matchesStatus =
        status === "all" ||
        (status === "active" && school.isActive) ||
        (status === "inactive" && !school.isActive);
      if (!matchesStatus) return false;
      if (q.length === 0) return true;
      return (
        school.nameAr.includes(normalizedQuery) ||
        (school.nameEn?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ?? false) ||
        school.slug.includes(q)
      );
    });
  }, [schools, normalizedQuery, status]);

  return (
    <div>
      {/* Toolbar */}
      <div className="mb-4 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
        <div className="relative flex-1">
          <Search
            className="pointer-events-none absolute start-3 top-1/2 size-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground"
            aria-hidden="true"
          />
          <Input
            value={query}
            onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
            placeholder="ابحث باسم المدرسة أو الرابط المختصر…"
            className="ps-9"
            aria-label="البحث في المدارس"
          />
        </div>
        <div
          className="flex w-fit items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border bg-card p-1"
          role="group"
          aria-label="تصفية حسب الحالة"
        >
          {statusFilters.map((filter) => (
            <button
              key={filter.value}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setStatus(filter.value)}
              aria-pressed={status === filter.value}
              className={cn(
                "rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold transition-colors",
                "focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring",
                status === filter.value
                  ? "bg-brand-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                  : "text-muted-foreground hover:bg-accent hover:text-foreground"
              )}
            >
              {filter.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Content */}
      {schools.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border bg-card shadow-xs">
          <EmptyState
            icon={SchoolIcon}
            title="لا توجد مدارس مسجلة بعد"
            description="ابدأ بإضافة أول مدرسة لتبني عليها الفروع والمراحل التعليمية وباقي وحدات المنصة."
            action={
              <Button asChild>
                <Link href="/schools/new">
                  <Plus className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                  إضافة أول مدرسة
                </Link>
              </Button>
            }
          />
        </div>
      ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border bg-card shadow-xs">
          <EmptyState
            icon={Search}
            title="لا توجد نتائج مطابقة"
            description="جرّب تعديل كلمة البحث أو تغيير فلتر الحالة."
            action={
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                onClick={() => {
                  setQuery("");
                  setStatus("all");
                }}
              >
                إعادة ضبط البحث
              </Button>
            }
          />
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* Desktop table */}
          <div className="hidden rounded-xl border bg-card shadow-xs md:block">
            <Table>
              <TableHeader>
                <TableRow className="hover:bg-transparent">
                  <TableHead className="py-3.5 ps-5">المدرسة</TableHead>
                  <TableHead className="py-3.5">الرابط المختصر</TableHead>
                  <TableHead className="py-3.5 text-center">الفروع</TableHead>
                  <TableHead className="py-3.5">الحالة</TableHead>
                  <TableHead className="py-3.5">تاريخ الإضافة</TableHead>
                  <TableHead className="py-3.5 pe-5">
                    <span className="sr-only">فتح التفاصيل</span>
                  </TableHead>
                </TableRow>
              </TableHeader>
              <TableBody>
                {filtered.map((school) => (
                  <TableRow key={school.id}>
                    <TableCell className="py-3.5 ps-5">
                      <Link
                        href={`/schools/${school.id}`}
                        className="group flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring"
                      >
                        <SchoolAvatar school={school} />
                        <span className="min-w-0 leading-tight">
                          <span className="block truncate text-sm font-bold text-foreground transition-colors group-hover:text-brand-700">
                            {school.nameAr}
                          </span>
                          {school.nameEn && (
                            <span className="block truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground" dir="ltr">
                              {school.nameEn}
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </span>
                      </Link>
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="py-3.5">
                      <code
                        className="rounded-md bg-muted px-2 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground"
                        dir="ltr"
                      >
                        {school.slug}
                      </code>
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="py-3.5 text-center">
                      <span className="text-sm font-bold tabular-nums text-foreground">
                        {school.branchesCount}
                      </span>
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="py-3.5">
                      {school.isActive ? (
                        <StatusBadge tone="success">نشطة</StatusBadge>
                      ) : (
                        <StatusBadge tone="neutral">موقوفة</StatusBadge>
                      )}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="py-3.5">
                      <span className="text-sm tabular-nums text-muted-foreground">
                        {school.createdAtLabel}
                      </span>
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="py-3.5 pe-5 text-end">
                      <Link
                        href={`/schools/${school.id}`}
                        aria-label={`فتح تفاصيل ${school.nameAr}`}
                        className="inline-flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-accent hover:text-brand-700 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring"
                      >
                        <ChevronLeft className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                      </Link>
                    </TableCell>
                  </TableRow>
                ))}
              </TableBody>
            </Table>
          </div>

          {/* Mobile cards */}
          <ul className="space-y-3 md:hidden" role="list" aria-label="قائمة المدارس">
            {filtered.map((school) => (
              <li key={school.id}>
                <Link
                  href={`/schools/${school.id}`}
                  className="block rounded-xl border bg-card p-4 shadow-xs outline-none transition-colors hover:border-brand-200 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <SchoolAvatar school={school} />
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 leading-tight">
                      <p className="truncate text-sm font-bold text-foreground">
                        {school.nameAr}
                      </p>
                      {school.nameEn && (
                        <p className="truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground" dir="ltr">
                          {school.nameEn}
                        </p>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    {school.isActive ? (
                      <StatusBadge tone="success">نشطة</StatusBadge>
                    ) : (
                      <StatusBadge tone="neutral">موقوفة</StatusBadge>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-between border-t pt-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      الفروع:
                      <span className="font-bold tabular-nums text-foreground">
                        {school.branchesCount}
                      </span>
                    </span>
                    <code className="rounded-md bg-muted px-2 py-0.5 font-semibold" dir="ltr">
                      {school.slug}
                    </code>
                  </div>
                </Link>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </>
      )}

      {/* Count summary */}
      {schools.length > 0 && (
        <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          عرض {filtered.length} من {schools.length} مدرسة
          {status !== "all" && " (وفلتر الحالة)"}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { Search, X } from "lucide-react";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import {
  CONTENT_STATUSES,
  CONTENT_STATUS_LABELS,
  TRAINING_CATEGORIES,
  TRAINING_CATEGORY_LABELS,
} from "@/config/training";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Shared GET-form filters for the academy catalogs. Deliberately a     */
/* server component (native form + native selects): filtering works    */
/* without client JS and stays stateless/URL-driven like the users     */
/* directory.                                                          */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

interface CatalogFiltersProps {
  /** Route the GET form submits to. */
  action: string;
  q?: string;
  category?: string;
  programId?: string;
  programs?: { id: string; nameAr: string; platformWide?: boolean }[];
  status?: string;
  showStatus?: boolean;
  showMine?: boolean;
  mine?: boolean;
}

const selectClass =
  "h-9 w-full appearance-none rounded-lg border border-input bg-background px-3 text-sm shadow-xs outline-none transition-colors focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/30";

export function CatalogFilters({
  action,
  q = "",
  category = "",
  programId = "",
  programs,
  status = "",
  showStatus = false,
  showMine = false,
  mine = false,
}: CatalogFiltersProps) {
  return (
    <form
      action={action}
      method="get"
      className="rounded-xl border bg-card p-4 shadow-xs"
    >
      <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor={`${action}-q`}>البحث بالعنوان</Label>
          <div className="relative">
            <Search
              className="pointer-events-none absolute end-3 top-1/2 size-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground"
              aria-hidden="true"
            />
            <Input
              id={`${action}-q`}
              name="q"
              defaultValue={q}
              placeholder="ابحث بعنوان الدورة…"
              className="pe-9"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor={`${action}-category`}>التصنيف</Label>
          <select
            id={`${action}-category`}
            name="category"
            defaultValue={category}
            className={selectClass}
          >
            <option value="">كل التصنيفات</option>
            {TRAINING_CATEGORIES.map((key) => (
              <option key={key} value={key}>
                {TRAINING_CATEGORY_LABELS[key]}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        {programs && programs.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor={`${action}-program`}>البرنامج</Label>
            <select
              id={`${action}-program`}
              name="programId"
              defaultValue={programId}
              className={selectClass}
            >
              <option value="">كل البرامج</option>
              {programs.map((program) => (
                <option key={program.id} value={program.id}>
                  {program.nameAr}
                  {program.platformWide ? " — مشترك" : ""}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <span />
        )}

        {showStatus ? (
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor={`${action}-status`}>حالة النشر</Label>
            <select
              id={`${action}-status`}
              name="status"
              defaultValue={status}
              className={selectClass}
            >
              <option value="">كل الحالات</option>
              {CONTENT_STATUSES.map((key) => (
                <option key={key} value={key}>
                  {CONTENT_STATUS_LABELS[key]}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
        ) : showMine ? (
          <div className="flex items-end">
            <label className="flex h-9 items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                name="mine"
                value="1"
                defaultChecked={mine}
                className="size-4 rounded border-input accent-brand-600"
              />
              دوراتي المسجّل بها فقط
            </label>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <span />
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-2">
        <Button type="submit" size="sm">
          <Search className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          تطبيق الفلاتر
        </Button>
        <a
          href={action}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-accent"
        >
          <X className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
          مسح
        </a>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
