"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import {
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  Download,
  FileText,
  Link2,
  Pencil,
  Plus,
  Trash2,
  Video,
  type LucideIcon,
} from "lucide-react";

import {
  createLessonAction,
  createSectionAction,
  deleteLessonAction,
  deleteSectionAction,
  moveLessonAction,
  moveSectionAction,
  updateLessonAction,
  updateSectionAction,
  uploadLessonFileAction,
  type TrainingFormState,
} from "@/app/actions/training";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/platform/empty-state";
import { SectionCard } from "@/components/platform/section-card";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/platform/status-badge";
import { LESSON_TYPES, LESSON_TYPE_LABELS } from "@/config/training";
import type { CourseManageDetail } from "@/lib/training/service";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Course content builder — sections & lessons with real server-side    */
/* persistence: create/edit/delete, swap-based reordering, per-type     */
/* lesson fields and real file uploads through the storage adapter.     */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

type SectionRow = CourseManageDetail["sections"][number];
type LessonRow = SectionRow["lessons"][number];

interface ContentBuilderProps {
  courseId: string;
  courseStatus: string;
  sections: SectionRow[];
}

const LESSON_ICONS: Record<string, LucideIcon> = {
  TEXT: FileText,
  VIDEO: Video,
  EXTERNAL_VIDEO: Link2,
  RESOURCE: Download,
};

/* --------------------------- Section form -------------------------- */

function SectionForm({
  courseId,
  section,
  onDone,
}: {
  courseId: string;
  section?: SectionRow;
  onDone: () => void;
}) {
  const [state, formAction, isPending] = React.useActionState<
    TrainingFormState | null,
    FormData
  >(section ? updateSectionAction : createSectionAction, null);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (state?.ok) onDone();
  }, [state, onDone]);

  return (
    <form action={formAction} className="space-y-3" noValidate>
      <input type="hidden" name="courseId" value={courseId} />
      {section && (
        <input type="hidden" name="sectionId" value={section.id} />
      )}

      {state?.message && !state.ok && (
        <Alert variant="destructive" role="alert">
          <AlertDescription>{state.message}</AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      )}

      <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor={`section-title-${section?.id ?? "new"}`}>
            عنوان القسم *
          </Label>
          <Input
            id={`section-title-${section?.id ?? "new"}`}
            name="titleAr"
            required
            defaultValue={section?.titleAr}
            placeholder="مثال: الوحدة الأولى — المداخل النظرية"
          />
          {state?.fieldErrors?.titleAr && (
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
              {state.fieldErrors.titleAr}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor={`section-desc-${section?.id ?? "new"}`}>
            وصف مختصر
          </Label>
          <Input
            id={`section-desc-${section?.id ?? "new"}`}
            name="description"
            defaultValue={section?.description ?? ""}
            placeholder="اختياري"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <Button type="submit" size="sm" disabled={isPending}>
          <Plus className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          {isPending ? "جارٍ الحفظ…" : section ? "حفظ القسم" : "إضافة القسم"}
        </Button>
        {section && (
          <Button type="button" size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={onDone}>
            إلغاء
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

/* --------------------------- Move / delete ------------------------- */

function SectionMove({
  courseId,
  sectionId,
  direction,
  disabled,
}: {
  courseId: string;
  sectionId: string;
  direction: "up" | "down";
  disabled: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <form action={moveSectionAction}>
      <input type="hidden" name="courseId" value={courseId} />
      <input type="hidden" name="sectionId" value={sectionId} />
      <input type="hidden" name="direction" value={direction} />
      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={disabled}
        aria-label={direction === "up" ? "تحريك القسم لأعلى" : "تحريك القسم لأسفل"}
        className="inline-flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-accent disabled:opacity-40"
      >
        {direction === "up" ? (
          <ChevronUp className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
        ) : (
          <ChevronDown className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
        )}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}

function LessonMove({
  courseId,
  lessonId,
  direction,
  disabled,
}: {
  courseId: string;
  lessonId: string;
  direction: "up" | "down";
  disabled: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <form action={moveLessonAction}>
      <input type="hidden" name="courseId" value={courseId} />
      <input type="hidden" name="lessonId" value={lessonId} />
      <input type="hidden" name="direction" value={direction} />
      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={disabled}
        aria-label={direction === "up" ? "تحريك الدرس لأعلى" : "تحريك الدرس لأسفل"}
        className="inline-flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-accent disabled:opacity-40"
      >
        {direction === "up" ? (
          <ChevronUp className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
        ) : (
          <ChevronDown className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
        )}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}

/* --------------------------- Lesson dialog ------------------------- */

function LessonDialog({
  courseId,
  sectionId,
  sectionTitle,
  lesson,
  onClose,
}: {
  courseId: string;
  sectionId: string;
  sectionTitle: string;
  lesson?: LessonRow;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const [type, setType] = React.useState<string>(lesson?.type ?? "TEXT");
  const [attachment, setAttachment] = React.useState<{
    id?: string;
    name?: string;
  }>(
    lesson?.attachmentId
      ? { id: lesson.attachmentId, name: lesson.attachmentName ?? undefined }
      : {}
  );

  const [state, formAction, isPending] = React.useActionState<
    TrainingFormState | null,
    FormData
  >(lesson ? updateLessonAction : createLessonAction, null);

  const [uploadState, uploadFormAction] = React.useActionState<
    TrainingFormState | null,
    FormData
  >(uploadLessonFileAction, null);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (uploadState?.ok && uploadState.attachmentId) {
      setAttachment({
        id: uploadState.attachmentId,
        name: uploadState.attachmentName,
      });
    }
  }, [uploadState]);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (state?.ok) onClose();
  }, [state, onClose]);

  const needsAttachment = type === "VIDEO" || type === "RESOURCE";

  return (
    <Dialog open onOpenChange={(open) => !open && onClose()}>
      <DialogContent className="max-h-[85vh] overflow-y-auto sm:max-w-xl">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>
            {lesson ? "تعديل الدرس" : "إضافة درس جديد"}
          </DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>
            القسم: {sectionTitle} — اختر نوع الدرس وستظهر حقوله المطلوبة.
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>

        {state?.message && !state.ok && (
          <Alert variant="destructive" role="alert">
            <AlertDescription>{state.message}</AlertDescription>
          </Alert>
        )}

        {/* Upload form — a sibling form so the lesson form stays valid. */}
        {needsAttachment && (
          <div className="rounded-xl border bg-muted/30 p-3">
            <form action={uploadFormAction} className="space-y-2">
              <input type="hidden" name="courseId" value={courseId} />
              <input type="hidden" name="lessonId" value={lesson?.id ?? ""} />
              <input type="hidden" name="kind" value={type} />
              <Label htmlFor="lesson-file" className="text-xs">
                {type === "VIDEO"
                  ? "ملف الفيديو (MP4 أو WebM — حتى 200 ميغابايت)"
                  : "ملف المورد (PDF، Word، صور، فيديو — حتى 25 ميغابايت)"}
              </Label>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <Input
                  id="lesson-file"
                  name="file"
                  type="file"
                  className="w-64"
                  required
                />
                <Button type="submit" size="sm" variant="outline">
                  {uploadState?.ok ? "تم الرفع — استبدال" : "رفع الملف"}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </form>
            {attachment.id ? (
              <p className="mt-2 text-xs font-semibold text-success">
                الملف الحالي: {attachment.name ?? attachment.id}
              </p>
            ) : (
              <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                لم يُرفع ملف بعد — يلزم رفع الملف قبل الحفظ.
              </p>
            )}
            {uploadState?.message && !uploadState.ok && (
              <p className="mt-2 text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
                {uploadState.message}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        <form action={formAction} className="space-y-3" noValidate>
          <input type="hidden" name="courseId" value={courseId} />
          <input type="hidden" name="sectionId" value={sectionId} />
          {lesson && <input type="hidden" name="lessonId" value={lesson.id} />}
          {needsAttachment && (
            <input type="hidden" name="attachmentId" value={attachment.id ?? ""} />
          )}

          <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="lesson-title">عنوان الدرس *</Label>
              <Input
                id="lesson-title"
                name="titleAr"
                required
                defaultValue={lesson?.titleAr}
                placeholder="مثال: مقدمة في التعلم النشط"
              />
              {state?.fieldErrors?.titleAr && (
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
                  {state.fieldErrors.titleAr}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="lesson-type">نوع الدرس *</Label>
              <Select value={type} onValueChange={setType}>
                <SelectTrigger id="lesson-type" className="w-full">
                  <SelectValue />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  {LESSON_TYPES.map((key) => (
                    <SelectItem key={key} value={key}>
                      {LESSON_TYPE_LABELS[key]}
                    </SelectItem>
                  ))}
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
              {/* Keep the selected type inside the submitted form. */}
              <input type="hidden" name="type" value={type} />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="lesson-desc">وصف مختصر</Label>
            <Input
              id="lesson-desc"
              name="description"
              defaultValue={lesson?.description ?? ""}
              placeholder="اختياري"
            />
          </div>

          {type === "TEXT" && (
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="lesson-content">محتوى الدرس النصي *</Label>
              <Textarea
                id="lesson-content"
                name="content"
                rows={8}
                defaultValue={lesson?.content ?? ""}
                placeholder="اكتب محتوى الدرس كنص عادي — يُعرض بأمان دون أي HTML."
              />
              {state?.fieldErrors?.content && (
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
                  {state.fieldErrors.content}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {type === "EXTERNAL_VIDEO" && (
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="lesson-video-url">رابط الفيديو الخارجي *</Label>
              <Input
                id="lesson-video-url"
                name="videoUrl"
                dir="ltr"
                defaultValue={lesson?.videoUrl ?? ""}
                placeholder="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=…"
              />
              <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
                يُسمح فقط بروابط YouTube أو Vimeo عبر https — لا يُرفع أي
                ملف هنا.
              </p>
              {state?.fieldErrors?.videoUrl && (
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
                  {state.fieldErrors.videoUrl}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              name="isPublished"
              defaultChecked={lesson?.isPublished ?? false}
              className="size-4 rounded border-input accent-brand-600"
            />
            درس منشور (مرئي للمعلمين في الدورة المنشورة)
          </label>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
            <Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={onClose}>
              إلغاء
            </Button>
            <Button type="submit" size="sm" disabled={isPending}>
              {isPending ? "جارٍ الحفظ…" : lesson ? "حفظ الدرس" : "إضافة الدرس"}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

/* --------------------------- Main builder -------------------------- */

export function ContentBuilder({
  courseId,
  courseStatus,
  sections,
}: ContentBuilderProps) {
  const [editingSectionId, setEditingSectionId] = React.useState<
    string | null
  >(null);
  const [addingSection, setAddingSection] = React.useState(false);
  const [dialog, setDialog] = React.useState<{
    sectionId: string;
    sectionTitle: string;
    lesson?: LessonRow;
  } | null>(null);

  return (
    <SectionCard
      title="محتوى الدورة"
      description="أقسام الدورة ودروسها بالترتيب — الترتيب يُحفظ فورًا في قاعدة البيانات، والدروس غير المنشورة تبقى مخفية عن المعلمين."
      action={
        <StatusBadge
          tone={courseStatus === "PUBLISHED" ? "success" : "neutral"}
        >
          {courseStatus === "PUBLISHED"
            ? "دورة منشورة"
            : "الدورة غير منشورة بعد"}
        </StatusBadge>
      }
    >
      {sections.length === 0 && !addingSection ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={FileText}
          title="لا توجد أقسام بعد"
          description="ابدأ بإضافة أول قسم ثم أضف دروسه — لا يمكن نشر الدورة قبل وجود قسم ودرس واحد على الأقل."
          action={
            <Button size="sm" onClick={() => setAddingSection(true)}>
              <Plus className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              إضافة أول قسم
            </Button>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {sections.map((section, index) => (
            <div key={section.id} className="rounded-xl border">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b px-4 py-3">
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold">
                    <span className="flex size-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-brand-100 text-xs font-extrabold text-brand-700">
                      {index + 1}
                    </span>
                    {section.titleAr}
                  </p>
                  {section.description && (
                    <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {section.description}
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <span className="me-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-muted-foreground">
                    {section.lessons.length} درس
                  </span>
                  <SectionMove
                    courseId={courseId}
                    sectionId={section.id}
                    direction="up"
                    disabled={index === 0}
                  />
                  <SectionMove
                    courseId={courseId}
                    sectionId={section.id}
                    direction="down"
                    disabled={index === sections.length - 1}
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-label="تحرير القسم"
                    onClick={() =>
                      setEditingSectionId(
                        editingSectionId === section.id ? null : section.id
                      )
                    }
                    className="inline-flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-accent"
                  >
                    <Pencil className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                  </button>
                  <form
                    action={deleteSectionAction}
                    onSubmit={(event) => {
                      if (
                        !window.confirm(
                          `حذف القسم «${section.titleAr}» وكل دروسه وملفاته؟`
                        )
                      ) {
                        event.preventDefault();
                      }
                    }}
                  >
                    <input type="hidden" name="courseId" value={courseId} />
                    <input type="hidden" name="sectionId" value={section.id} />
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      aria-label="حذف القسم"
                      className="inline-flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border text-destructive transition-colors hover:bg-destructive/10"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    </button>
                  </form>
                </div>
              </div>

              {editingSectionId === section.id ? (
                <div className="border-b bg-muted/20 px-4 py-3">
                  <SectionForm
                    courseId={courseId}
                    section={section}
                    onDone={() => setEditingSectionId(null)}
                  />
                </div>
              ) : null}

              <div className="divide-y">
                {section.lessons.map((lesson, lessonIndex) => {
                  const Icon = LESSON_ICONS[lesson.type] ?? FileText;
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={lesson.id}
                      className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 px-4 py-2.5"
                    >
                      <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2.5">
                        <span
                          className="flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-muted text-muted-foreground"
                          aria-hidden="true"
                        >
                          <Icon className="size-4" />
                        </span>
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                          <p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold">
                            {lesson.titleAr}
                          </p>
                          <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
                            {LESSON_TYPE_LABELS[lesson.type as keyof typeof LESSON_TYPE_LABELS] ?? lesson.type}
                            {lesson.attachmentName
                              ? ` — ${lesson.attachmentName}`
                              : ""}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <StatusBadge
                          tone={lesson.isPublished ? "success" : "warning"}
                          withDot={false}
                        >
                          {lesson.isPublished ? "منشور" : "مسودة"}
                        </StatusBadge>
                        <LessonMove
                          courseId={courseId}
                          lessonId={lesson.id}
                          direction="up"
                          disabled={lessonIndex === 0}
                        />
                        <LessonMove
                          courseId={courseId}
                          lessonId={lesson.id}
                          direction="down"
                          disabled={lessonIndex === section.lessons.length - 1}
                        />
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          aria-label="تحرير الدرس"
                          onClick={() =>
                            setDialog({
                              sectionId: section.id,
                              sectionTitle: section.titleAr,
                              lesson,
                            })
                          }
                          className="inline-flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-accent"
                        >
                          <Pencil className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                        </button>
                        <form
                          action={deleteLessonAction}
                          onSubmit={(event) => {
                            if (
                              !window.confirm(
                                `حذف الدرس «${lesson.titleAr}» وملفاته؟`
                              )
                            ) {
                              event.preventDefault();
                            }
                          }}
                        >
                          <input type="hidden" name="courseId" value={courseId} />
                          <input type="hidden" name="lessonId" value={lesson.id} />
                          <button
                            type="submit"
                            aria-label="حذف الدرس"
                            className="inline-flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border text-destructive transition-colors hover:bg-destructive/10"
                          >
                            <Trash2 className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                          </button>
                        </form>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}

                <div className="px-4 py-2.5">
                  <Button
                    type="button"
                    size="sm"
                    variant="outline"
                    onClick={() =>
                      setDialog({
                        sectionId: section.id,
                        sectionTitle: section.titleAr,
                      })
                    }
                  >
                    <Plus className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    إضافة درس إلى هذا القسم
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}

          {addingSection ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed bg-muted/20 p-4">
              <SectionForm
                courseId={courseId}
                onDone={() => setAddingSection(false)}
              />
            </div>
          ) : (
            sections.length > 0 && (
              <Button
                type="button"
                size="sm"
                variant="outline"
                onClick={() => setAddingSection(true)}
              >
                <Plus className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                إضافة قسم جديد
              </Button>
            )
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {dialog && (
        <LessonDialog
          courseId={courseId}
          sectionId={dialog.sectionId}
          sectionTitle={dialog.sectionTitle}
          lesson={dialog.lesson}
          onClose={() => setDialog(null)}
        />
      )}
    </SectionCard>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\training\course-form.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { useFormStatus } from "react-dom";
import { Save, School } from "lucide-react";

import {
  createCourseAction,
  updateCourseAction,
  type TrainingFormState,
} from "@/app/actions/training";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { SectionCard } from "@/components/platform/section-card";
import {
  COURSE_LEVELS,
  COURSE_LEVEL_LABELS,
  TRAINING_CATEGORIES,
  TRAINING_CATEGORY_LABELS,
} from "@/config/training";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Course create/edit form. Scope (platform-wide vs school) is chosen   */
/* by SUPER_ADMIN only; office managers are fixed to their own school.  */
/* The service re-validates every relation server-side.                 */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

export interface CourseFormInitial {
  titleAr: string;
  titleEn: string | null;
  descriptionAr: string;
  descriptionEn: string | null;
  category: string;
  programId: string | null;
  level: string | null;
  estimatedHours: number | null;
  instructorId: string | null;
  schoolId: string | null;
}

interface CourseFormProps {
  mode: "create" | "edit";
  courseId?: string;
  initial?: CourseFormInitial;
  programOptions: { id: string; nameAr: string; platformWide: boolean }[];
  schools: { id: string; nameAr: string }[];
  instructors: { id: string; name: string; schoolId: string | null }[];
  showScope: boolean;
  defaultScope: "platform" | "school";
  fixedSchoolId?: string;
}

function SubmitButton({ mode }: { mode: "create" | "edit" }) {
  const { pending } = useFormStatus();
  return (
    <Button type="submit" disabled={pending} size="sm">
      <School className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
      {pending
        ? "جارٍ الحفظ…"
        : mode === "create"
          ? "إنشاء الدورة والانتقال لبناء المحتوى"
          : "حفظ التعديلات"}
    </Button>
  );
}

export function CourseForm({
  mode,
  courseId,
  initial,
  programOptions,
  schools,
  instructors,
  showScope,
  defaultScope,
  fixedSchoolId,
}: CourseFormProps) {
  const action = mode === "create" ? createCourseAction : updateCourseAction;
  const [state, formAction] = React.useActionState<
    TrainingFormState | null,
    FormData
  >(action, null);

  const [scope, setScope] = React.useState<"platform" | "school">(
    initial?.schoolId === null ? "platform" : defaultScope
  );
  const [schoolId, setSchoolId] = React.useState<string>(
    initial?.schoolId ?? fixedSchoolId ?? ""
  );
  const [instructorId, setInstructorId] = React.useState<string>(
    initial?.instructorId ?? "none"
  );

  const instructorOptions = React.useMemo(() => {
    if (!showScope) return instructors; // office manager: pre-scoped
    if (scope === "platform") return []; // platform courses: no school staff
    return instructors.filter((instructor) => instructor.schoolId === schoolId);
  }, [instructors, scope, schoolId, showScope]);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    setInstructorId("none");
  }, [schoolId, scope]);

  // Edit mode locks the creation scope (platform vs school) — derive it
  // from the initial record; create mode uses the (super-admin) choice.
  const effectiveScope = showScope
    ? scope
    : initial
      ? initial.schoolId === null
        ? "platform"
        : "school"
      : "school";

  return (
    <SectionCard
      title={mode === "create" ? "دورة تدريبية جديدة" : "بيانات الدورة"}
      description="الحقول العربية إلزامية — الإنجليزية والبرنامج والمستوى والمدة والمدرّب اختيارية. تُحفظ الدورة كمسودة ويمكنك بناء محتواها بعد الإنشاء."
    >
      <form action={formAction} className="space-y-4" noValidate>
        {state?.message && (
          <Alert variant={state.ok ? "default" : "destructive"} role={state.ok ? "status" : "alert"}>
            <AlertDescription>{state.message}</AlertDescription>
          </Alert>
        )}

        {mode === "edit" && courseId && (
          <input type="hidden" name="courseId" value={courseId} />
        )}
        {/* Scope never changes after creation — always submitted. */}
        <input type="hidden" name="scope" value={effectiveScope} />
        <input
          type="hidden"
          name="schoolId"
          value={effectiveScope === "platform" ? "" : schoolId}
        />

        <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="course-title-ar">العنوان بالعربية *</Label>
            <Input
              id="course-title-ar"
              name="titleAr"
              required
              defaultValue={initial?.titleAr}
              placeholder="مثال: استراتيجيات التعلم النشط"
            />
            {state?.fieldErrors?.titleAr && (
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
                {state.fieldErrors.titleAr}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="course-title-en">العنوان بالإنجليزية</Label>
            <Input
              id="course-title-en"
              name="titleEn"
              dir="ltr"
              defaultValue={initial?.titleEn ?? ""}
              placeholder="Optional English title"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="course-desc-ar">الوصف بالعربية *</Label>
            <Textarea
              id="course-desc-ar"
              name="descriptionAr"
              required
              rows={4}
              defaultValue={initial?.descriptionAr}
              placeholder="ماذا سيتعلم المعلم من هذه الدورة؟"
            />
            {state?.fieldErrors?.descriptionAr && (
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
                {state.fieldErrors.descriptionAr}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="course-desc-en">الوصف بالإنجليزية</Label>
            <Textarea
              id="course-desc-en"
              name="descriptionEn"
              dir="ltr"
              rows={4}
              defaultValue={initial?.descriptionEn ?? ""}
              placeholder="Optional English description"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="course-category">التصنيف *</Label>
            <Select name="category" defaultValue={initial?.category ?? undefined}>
              <SelectTrigger id="course-category" className="w-full">
                <SelectValue placeholder="اختر التصنيف" />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                {TRAINING_CATEGORIES.map((key) => (
                  <SelectItem key={key} value={key}>
                    {TRAINING_CATEGORY_LABELS[key]}
                  </SelectItem>
                ))}
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
            {state?.fieldErrors?.category && (
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
                {state.fieldErrors.category}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="course-program">البرنامج التدريبي</Label>
            <Select
              name="programId"
              defaultValue={initial?.programId ?? "none"}
            >
              <SelectTrigger id="course-program" className="w-full">
                <SelectValue placeholder="بدون برنامج" />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="none">بدون برنامج</SelectItem>
                {programOptions.map((program) => (
                  <SelectItem key={program.id} value={program.id}>
                    {program.nameAr}
                    {program.platformWide ? " — مشترك" : ""}
                  </SelectItem>
                ))}
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="course-level">مستوى الصعوبة</Label>
            <Select name="level" defaultValue={initial?.level ?? "none"}>
              <SelectTrigger id="course-level" className="w-full">
                <SelectValue placeholder="غير محدد" />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="none">غير محدد</SelectItem>
                {COURSE_LEVELS.map((key) => (
                  <SelectItem key={key} value={key}>
                    {COURSE_LEVEL_LABELS[key]}
                  </SelectItem>
                ))}
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="course-hours">المدة التقديرية (ساعات)</Label>
            <Input
              id="course-hours"
              name="estimatedHours"
              type="number"
              min={1}
              max={500}
              defaultValue={initial?.estimatedHours ?? ""}
              placeholder="مثال: 6"
            />
            {state?.fieldErrors?.estimatedHours && (
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
                {state.fieldErrors.estimatedHours}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {showScope && mode === "create" ? (
          <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="course-scope">نطاق الدورة *</Label>
              <Select
                value={scope}
                onValueChange={(value) => setScope(value as "platform" | "school")}
              >
                <SelectTrigger id="course-scope" className="w-full">
                  <SelectValue />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="school">خاصة بمدرسة محددة</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="platform">
                    مشتركة على مستوى المنصة
                  </SelectItem>
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>

            {scope === "school" && (
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label htmlFor="course-school">المدرسة المالكة *</Label>
                <Select
                  value={schoolId || undefined}
                  onValueChange={(value) => setSchoolId(value)}
                >
                  <SelectTrigger id="course-school" className="w-full">
                    <SelectValue placeholder="اختر المدرسة" />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent>
                    {schools.map((school) => (
                      <SelectItem key={school.id} value={school.id}>
                        {school.nameAr}
                      </SelectItem>
                    ))}
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {effectiveScope === "school" && schoolId ? (
          <div className="space-y-1.5 md:max-w-sm">
            <Label htmlFor="course-instructor">المدرّب المسؤول</Label>
            <Select
              name="instructorId"
              value={instructorId}
              onValueChange={setInstructorId}
            >
              <SelectTrigger id="course-instructor" className="w-full">
                <SelectValue placeholder="بدون مدرّب" />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="none">بدون مدرّب</SelectItem>
                {instructorOptions.map((instructor) => (
                  <SelectItem key={instructor.id} value={instructor.id}>
                    {instructor.name}
                  </SelectItem>
                ))}
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
            {state?.fieldErrors?.instructorId && (
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
                {state.fieldErrors.instructorId}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            المدرّب متاح للدورات الخاصة بمدرسة محددة — يُختار من معلمي
            المدرسة أو مشرفيها التربويين.
          </p>
        )}

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <SubmitButton mode={mode} />
          {state?.ok && state.message && (
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-success">
              {state.message}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </form>
    </SectionCard>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\training\cover-upload.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { ImageUp, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";

import {
  deleteAttachmentAction,
  uploadCourseCoverAction,
  type TrainingFormState,
} from "@/app/actions/training";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Course/program cover management — real upload through the server     */
/* action + storage adapter, replace deletes the previous file.         */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

interface CoverUploadProps {
  ownerType: "course" | "program";
  ownerId: string;
  currentCover: { id: string; name: string } | null;
}

export function CoverUpload({
  ownerType,
  ownerId,
  currentCover,
}: CoverUploadProps) {
  const [uploadState, uploadAction] = React.useActionState<
    TrainingFormState | null,
    FormData
  >(uploadCourseCoverAction, null);
  const [deleteState, deleteAction] = React.useActionState<
    TrainingFormState | null,
    FormData
  >(deleteAttachmentAction, null);

  const message = uploadState?.ok
    ? uploadState
    : deleteState?.ok
      ? deleteState
      : (!uploadState?.ok && uploadState?.message ? uploadState : (!deleteState?.ok && deleteState?.message ? deleteState : null));

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {currentCover ? (
        // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
        <img
          src={`/api/files/${currentCover.id}`}
          alt="صورة غلاف المحتوى"
          className="h-44 w-full max-w-md rounded-lg border object-cover"
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex h-44 w-full max-w-md items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-dashed bg-muted/40 text-muted-foreground">
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold">لا توجد صورة غلاف بعد</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {message?.message && (
        <Alert variant={message.ok ? "default" : "destructive"} role={message.ok ? "status" : "alert"}>
          <AlertDescription>{message.message}</AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      )}

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
        <form action={uploadAction} className="space-y-1.5">
          <input type="hidden" name="ownerType" value={ownerType} />
          <input type="hidden" name="ownerId" value={ownerId} />
          <Label htmlFor="cover-file" className="sr-only">
            صورة الغلاف
          </Label>
          <Input
            id="cover-file"
            name="file"
            type="file"
            accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp"
            className="w-72"
            required
          />
          <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
            PNG أو JPG أو WebP — بحد أقصى 5 ميغابايت
          </p>
          <Button type="submit" size="sm" variant="outline">
            <ImageUp className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            {uploadState?.ok ? "استبدال الغلاف" : "رفع الغلاف"}
          </Button>
        </form>

        {currentCover && (
          <form action={deleteAction}>
            <input type="hidden" name="attachmentId" value={currentCover.id} />
            {ownerType === "course" && (
              <input type="hidden" name="courseId" value={ownerId} />
            )}
            <Button
              type="submit"
              size="sm"
              variant="ghost"
              className="text-destructive hover:text-destructive"
            >
              <Trash2 className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              إزالة الغلاف
            </Button>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\training\enroll-button.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { useFormStatus } from "react-dom";
import { CheckCircle2, UserPlus } from "lucide-react";

import {
  enrollAction,
  type TrainingFormState,
} from "@/app/actions/training";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

/* Teacher enrollment — server-validated (role + school + published). */

function EnrollSubmit() {
  const { pending } = useFormStatus();
  return (
    <Button type="submit" size="sm" disabled={pending}>
      <UserPlus className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
      {pending ? "جارٍ التسجيل…" : "سجّل في الدورة"}
    </Button>
  );
}

export function EnrollButton({ courseId }: { courseId: string }) {
  const [state, formAction] = React.useActionState<
    TrainingFormState | null,
    FormData
  >(enrollAction, null);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      {state?.message && (
        <Alert variant={state.ok ? "default" : "destructive"} role={state.ok ? "status" : "alert"}>
          <CheckCircle2 className="size-4 text-success" aria-hidden="true" />
          <AlertDescription>{state.message}</AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      )}
      <form action={formAction}>
        <input type="hidden" name="courseId" value={courseId} />
        <EnrollSubmit />
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\training\program-manager.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { useFormStatus } from "react-dom";
import { GraduationCap, Pencil, Plus } from "lucide-react";

import {
  createProgramAction,
  setProgramStatusAction,
  updateProgramAction,
  type TrainingFormState,
} from "@/app/actions/training";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/platform/empty-state";
import { SectionCard } from "@/components/platform/section-card";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/platform/status-badge";
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from "@/components/ui/table";
import {
  contentStatusLabel,
  TRAINING_CATEGORIES,
  TRAINING_CATEGORY_LABELS,
} from "@/config/training";
import type { ManageProgramRow } from "@/lib/training/service";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Program management: create (draft), edit metadata, publish/archive.  */
/* Scope is fixed at creation — office managers never see the platform  */
/* option and the service re-forces it server-side.                     */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

export type ProgramRow = Omit<ManageProgramRow, "updatedAt"> & {
  updatedAtLabel: string;
};

interface ProgramManagerProps {
  programs: ProgramRow[];
  schools: { id: string; nameAr: string }[];
  isSuperAdmin: boolean;
}

function ProgramFields({
  idPrefix,
  initial,
  state,
}: {
  idPrefix: string;
  initial?: ProgramRow;
  state: TrainingFormState | null;
}) {
  return (
    <>
      <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor={`${idPrefix}-name-ar`}>اسم البرنامج بالعربية *</Label>
          <Input
            id={`${idPrefix}-name-ar`}
            name="nameAr"
            required
            defaultValue={initial?.nameAr}
            placeholder="مثال: برنامج التطوير المهني الشامل"
          />
          {state?.fieldErrors?.nameAr && (
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
              {state.fieldErrors.nameAr}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor={`${idPrefix}-name-en`}>الاسم بالإنجليزية</Label>
          <Input
            id={`${idPrefix}-name-en`}
            name="nameEn"
            dir="ltr"
            defaultValue={initial?.nameEn ?? ""}
            placeholder="Optional English name"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor={`${idPrefix}-desc-ar`}>الوصف بالعربية *</Label>
          <Textarea
            id={`${idPrefix}-desc-ar`}
            name="descriptionAr"
            required
            rows={3}
            defaultValue={initial?.descriptionAr}
            placeholder="ماذا يقدم هذا البرنامج للمعلمين؟"
          />
          {state?.fieldErrors?.descriptionAr && (
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
              {state.fieldErrors.descriptionAr}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor={`${idPrefix}-desc-en`}>الوصف بالإنجليزية</Label>
          <Textarea
            id={`${idPrefix}-desc-en`}
            name="descriptionEn"
            dir="ltr"
            rows={3}
            defaultValue={initial?.descriptionEn ?? ""}
            placeholder="Optional English description"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-1.5 md:max-w-sm">
        <Label htmlFor={`${idPrefix}-category`}>التصنيف *</Label>
        <Select
          name="category"
          defaultValue={initial?.category ?? undefined}
        >
          <SelectTrigger id={`${idPrefix}-category`} className="w-full">
            <SelectValue placeholder="اختر التصنيف" />
          </SelectTrigger>
          <SelectContent>
            {TRAINING_CATEGORIES.map((key) => (
              <SelectItem key={key} value={key}>
                {TRAINING_CATEGORY_LABELS[key]}
              </SelectItem>
            ))}
          </SelectContent>
        </Select>
        {state?.fieldErrors?.category && (
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
            {state.fieldErrors.category}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

function CreateSubmitButton() {
  const { pending } = useFormStatus();
  return (
    <Button type="submit" size="sm" disabled={pending}>
      <Plus className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
      {pending ? "جارٍ الإنشاء…" : "إنشاء البرنامج (مسودة)"}
    </Button>
  );
}

function CreateProgramForm({
  isSuperAdmin,
  schools,
}: {
  isSuperAdmin: boolean;
  schools: { id: string; nameAr: string }[];
}) {
  const [state, formAction] = React.useActionState<
    TrainingFormState | null,
    FormData
  >(createProgramAction, null);
  const [scope, setScope] = React.useState<"school" | "platform">("school");
  const [schoolId, setSchoolId] = React.useState<string>("");

  return (
    <SectionCard
      title="إنشاء برنامج تدريبي جديد"
      description="يُنشأ البرنامج كمسودة — انشره بعد مراجعته ليظهر كخيار عند بناء الدورات."
    >
      <form action={formAction} className="space-y-4" noValidate>
        {state?.message && (
          <Alert
            variant={state.ok ? "default" : "destructive"}
            role={state.ok ? "status" : "alert"}
          >
            <AlertDescription>{state.message}</AlertDescription>
          </Alert>
        )}

        <ProgramFields idPrefix="program-new" state={state} />

        {isSuperAdmin ? (
          <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="program-new-scope">نطاق البرنامج *</Label>
              <Select
                value={scope}
                onValueChange={(value) =>
                  setScope(value as "platform" | "school")
                }
              >
                <SelectTrigger id="program-new-scope" className="w-full">
                  <SelectValue />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="school">خاصة بمدرسة محددة</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="platform">
                    مشترك على مستوى المنصة
                  </SelectItem>
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
            {scope === "school" && (
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label htmlFor="program-new-school">المدرسة المالكة *</Label>
                <Select
                  name="schoolId"
                  value={schoolId || undefined}
                  onValueChange={setSchoolId}
                >
                  <SelectTrigger id="program-new-school" className="w-full">
                    <SelectValue placeholder="اختر المدرسة" />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent>
                    {schools.map((school) => (
                      <SelectItem key={school.id} value={school.id}>
                        {school.nameAr}
                      </SelectItem>
                    ))}
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : null}

        <CreateSubmitButton />
      </form>
    </SectionCard>
  );
}

function EditProgramDialog({
  program,
  onClose,
}: {
  program: ProgramRow;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const [state, formAction, isPending] = React.useActionState<
    TrainingFormState | null,
    FormData
  >(updateProgramAction, null);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (state?.ok) onClose();
  }, [state, onClose]);

  const scope = program.schoolId === null ? "platform" : "school";

  return (
    <Dialog open onOpenChange={(open) => !open && onClose()}>
      <DialogContent className="max-h-[85vh] overflow-y-auto sm:max-w-xl">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>تعديل البرنامج</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>
            النطاق ({program.schoolName ?? "منصة مشتركة"}) ثابت بعد الإنشاء.
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>

        {state?.message && !state.ok && (
          <Alert variant="destructive" role="alert">
            <AlertDescription>{state.message}</AlertDescription>
          </Alert>
        )}

        <form action={formAction} className="space-y-4" noValidate>
          <input type="hidden" name="programId" value={program.id} />
          <input type="hidden" name="scope" value={scope} />
          <input type="hidden" name="schoolId" value={program.schoolId ?? ""} />

          <ProgramFields idPrefix={`program-${program.id}`} initial={program} state={state} />

          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
            <Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={onClose}>
              إلغاء
            </Button>
            <Button type="submit" size="sm" disabled={isPending}>
              {isPending ? "جارٍ الحفظ…" : "حفظ التعديلات"}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

export function ProgramManager({
  programs,
  schools,
  isSuperAdmin,
}: ProgramManagerProps) {
  const [editing, setEditing] = React.useState<ProgramRow | null>(null);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <CreateProgramForm isSuperAdmin={isSuperAdmin} schools={schools} />

      <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border bg-card">
        {programs.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState
            icon={GraduationCap}
            title="لا توجد برامج تدريبية بعد"
            description="أنشئ أول برنامج من النموذج أعلاه — ثم اربط به دورات من صفحة إدارة الدورات."
            className="rounded-none border-0 bg-transparent"
          />
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <Table>
              <TableHeader>
                <TableRow>
                  <TableHead>البرنامج</TableHead>
                  <TableHead>النطاق</TableHead>
                  <TableHead>التصنيف</TableHead>
                  <TableHead>الحالة</TableHead>
                  <TableHead>الدورات</TableHead>
                  <TableHead>آخر تحديث</TableHead>
                  <TableHead aria-label="إجراءات" />
                </TableRow>
              </TableHeader>
              <TableBody>
                {programs.map((program) => (
                  <TableRow key={program.id}>
                    <TableCell>
                      <p className="text-sm font-bold">{program.nameAr}</p>
                      {program.nameEn && (
                        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground" dir="ltr">
                          {program.nameEn}
                        </p>
                      )}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-sm">
                      {program.schoolName ?? (
                        <span className="font-semibold text-brand-700">
                          منصة مشتركة
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-sm">
                      {TRAINING_CATEGORY_LABELS[
                        program.category as keyof typeof TRAINING_CATEGORY_LABELS
                      ] ?? program.category}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell>
                      <StatusBadge
                        tone={
                          program.status === "PUBLISHED"
                            ? "success"
                            : program.status === "ARCHIVED"
                              ? "neutral"
                              : "warning"
                        }
                      >
                        {contentStatusLabel(program.status)}
                      </StatusBadge>
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-sm tabular-nums">
                      {program.coursesCount}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {program.updatedAtLabel}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell>
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setEditing(program)}
                          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-accent"
                        >
                          <Pencil className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                          تعديل
                        </button>
                        {program.status !== "PUBLISHED" && (
                          <form action={setProgramStatusAction}>
                            <input
                              type="hidden"
                              name="programId"
                              value={program.id}
                            />
                            <input type="hidden" name="status" value="PUBLISHED" />
                            <button
                              type="submit"
                              className="rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-success transition-colors hover:bg-success-soft"
                            >
                              نشر
                            </button>
                          </form>
                        )}
                        {program.status !== "DRAFT" && (
                          <form action={setProgramStatusAction}>
                            <input
                              type="hidden"
                              name="programId"
                              value={program.id}
                            />
                            <input type="hidden" name="status" value="DRAFT" />
                            <button
                              type="submit"
                              className="rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-accent"
                            >
                              مسودة
                            </button>
                          </form>
                        )}
                        {program.status !== "ARCHIVED" && (
                          <form action={setProgramStatusAction}>
                            <input
                              type="hidden"
                              name="programId"
                              value={program.id}
                            />
                            <input type="hidden" name="status" value="ARCHIVED" />
                            <button
                              type="submit"
                              className="rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-warning transition-colors hover:bg-warning-soft"
                            >
                              أرشفة
                            </button>
                          </form>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </TableCell>
                  </TableRow>
                ))}
              </TableBody>
            </Table>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {editing && (
        <EditProgramDialog program={editing} onClose={() => setEditing(null)} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\training\publish-controls.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { Archive, Eye, Undo2 } from "lucide-react";

import {
  setCourseStatusAction,
  type TrainingFormState,
} from "@/app/actions/training";
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
  AlertDialogTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Publish / unpublish / archive controls. The service validates the    */
/* course content before publishing and rejects unauthorized roles —    */
/* these buttons only carry the intent.                                 */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

interface PublishControlsProps {
  courseId: string;
  status: string;
}

export function PublishControls({ courseId, status }: PublishControlsProps) {
  const [state, formAction] = React.useActionState<
    TrainingFormState | null,
    FormData
  >(setCourseStatusAction, null);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {state?.message && (
        <Alert variant={state.ok ? "default" : "destructive"} role={state.ok ? "status" : "alert"}>
          <AlertDescription>{state.message}</AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      )}

      <form action={formAction} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <input type="hidden" name="courseId" value={courseId} />

        {status !== "PUBLISHED" && (
          <AlertDialog>
            <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
              <Button type="submit" name="status" value="PUBLISHED" size="sm">
                <Eye className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                نشر الدورة
              </Button>
            </AlertDialogTrigger>
            <AlertDialogContent>
              <AlertDialogHeader>
                <AlertDialogTitle>نشر الدورة</AlertDialogTitle>
                <AlertDialogDescription>
                  ستُنشر الدورة لجمهورها المصرح له (معلمو مدرستك أو جميع
                  المدارس إن كانت مشتركة). يتحقق النظام من اكتمال المحتوى
                  قبل النشر.
                </AlertDialogDescription>
              </AlertDialogHeader>
              <AlertDialogFooter>
                <AlertDialogCancel>إلغاء</AlertDialogCancel>
                <AlertDialogAction>تأكيد النشر</AlertDialogAction>
              </AlertDialogFooter>
            </AlertDialogContent>
          </AlertDialog>
        )}

        {status === "PUBLISHED" && (
          <Button
            type="submit"
            name="status"
            value="DRAFT"
            size="sm"
            variant="outline"
          >
            <Undo2 className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            إرجاع إلى مسودة
          </Button>
        )}

        {status !== "ARCHIVED" && (
          <AlertDialog>
            <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
              <Button
                type="submit"
                name="status"
                value="ARCHIVED"
                size="sm"
                variant="outline"
                className="text-warning hover:text-warning"
              >
                <Archive className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                أرشفة
              </Button>
            </AlertDialogTrigger>
            <AlertDialogContent>
              <AlertDialogHeader>
                <AlertDialogTitle>أرشفة الدورة</AlertDialogTitle>
                <AlertDialogDescription>
                  لن تظهر الدورة كتدريب متاح لدى المعلمين، وتبقى بياناتها
                  وسجلات التسجيل محفوظة. يمكنك إعادة نشرها لاحقًا.
                </AlertDialogDescription>
              </AlertDialogHeader>
              <AlertDialogFooter>
                <AlertDialogCancel>إلغاء</AlertDialogCancel>
                <AlertDialogAction>تأكيد الأرشفة</AlertDialogAction>
              </AlertDialogFooter>
            </AlertDialogContent>
          </AlertDialog>
        )}
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\ui\alert-dialog.tsx =====
"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import * as AlertDialogPrimitive from "@radix-ui/react-alert-dialog"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { buttonVariants } from "@/components/ui/button"

function AlertDialog({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Root>) {
  return <AlertDialogPrimitive.Root data-slot="alert-dialog" {...props} />
}

function AlertDialogTrigger({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Trigger>) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPrimitive.Trigger data-slot="alert-dialog-trigger" {...props} />
  )
}

function AlertDialogPortal({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Portal>) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPrimitive.Portal data-slot="alert-dialog-portal" {...props} />
  )
}

function AlertDialogOverlay({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Overlay>) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPrimitive.Overlay
      data-slot="alert-dialog-overlay"
      className={cn(
        "data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=open]:fade-in-0 fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/50",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertDialogContent({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Content>) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPortal>
      <AlertDialogOverlay />
      <AlertDialogPrimitive.Content
        data-slot="alert-dialog-content"
        className={cn(
          "bg-background data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=open]:fade-in-0 data-[state=closed]:zoom-out-95 data-[state=open]:zoom-in-95 fixed top-[50%] left-[50%] z-50 grid w-full max-w-[calc(100%-2rem)] translate-x-[-50%] translate-y-[-50%] gap-4 rounded-lg border p-6 shadow-lg duration-200 sm:max-w-lg",
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      />
    </AlertDialogPortal>
  )
}

function AlertDialogHeader({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-dialog-header"
      className={cn("flex flex-col gap-2 text-center sm:text-left", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertDialogFooter({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-dialog-footer"
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-col-reverse gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertDialogTitle({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Title>) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPrimitive.Title
      data-slot="alert-dialog-title"
      className={cn("text-lg font-semibold", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertDialogDescription({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Description>) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPrimitive.Description
      data-slot="alert-dialog-description"
      className={cn("text-muted-foreground text-sm", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertDialogAction({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Action>) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPrimitive.Action
      className={cn(buttonVariants(), className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertDialogCancel({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Cancel>) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPrimitive.Cancel
      className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant: "outline" }), className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogPortal,
  AlertDialogOverlay,
  AlertDialogTrigger,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogTitle,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\ui\alert.tsx =====
import * as React from "react"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const alertVariants = cva(
  "relative w-full rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 text-sm grid has-[>svg]:grid-cols-[calc(var(--spacing)*4)_1fr] grid-cols-[0_1fr] has-[>svg]:gap-x-3 gap-y-0.5 items-start [&>svg]:size-4 [&>svg]:translate-y-0.5 [&>svg]:text-current",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-card text-card-foreground",
        destructive:
          "text-destructive bg-card [&>svg]:text-current *:data-[slot=alert-description]:text-destructive/90",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
    },
  }
)

function Alert({
  className,
  variant,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div"> & VariantProps<typeof alertVariants>) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert"
      role="alert"
      className={cn(alertVariants({ variant }), className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertTitle({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-title"
      className={cn(
        "col-start-2 line-clamp-1 min-h-4 font-medium tracking-tight",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertDescription({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-description"
      className={cn(
        "text-muted-foreground col-start-2 grid justify-items-start gap-1 text-sm [&_p]:leading-relaxed",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Alert, AlertTitle, AlertDescription }

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\ui\button.tsx =====
import * as React from "react"
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-all disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4 shrink-0 [&_svg]:shrink-0 outline-none focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:ring-[3px] aria-invalid:ring-destructive/20 dark:aria-invalid:ring-destructive/40 aria-invalid:border-destructive",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default:
          "bg-primary text-primary-foreground shadow-xs hover:bg-primary/90",
        destructive:
          "bg-destructive text-white shadow-xs hover:bg-destructive/90 focus-visible:ring-destructive/20 dark:focus-visible:ring-destructive/40 dark:bg-destructive/60",
        outline:
          "border bg-background shadow-xs hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground dark:bg-input/30 dark:border-input dark:hover:bg-input/50",
        secondary:
          "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground shadow-xs hover:bg-secondary/80",
        ghost:
          "hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground dark:hover:bg-accent/50",
        link: "text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
      },
      size: {
        default: "h-9 px-4 py-2 has-[>svg]:px-3",
        sm: "h-8 rounded-md gap-1.5 px-3 has-[>svg]:px-2.5",
        lg: "h-10 rounded-md px-6 has-[>svg]:px-4",
        icon: "size-9",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },
  }
)

function Button({
  className,
  variant,
  size,
  asChild = false,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"button"> &
  VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants> & {
    asChild?: boolean
  }) {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "button"

  return (
    <Comp
      data-slot="button"
      className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, className }))}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Button, buttonVariants }

import * as React from "react"
import * as DialogPrimitive from "@radix-ui/react-dialog"
import { XIcon } from "lucide-react"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

function Dialog({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Root>) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Root data-slot="dialog" {...props} />
}

function DialogTrigger({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Trigger>) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Trigger data-slot="dialog-trigger" {...props} />
}

function DialogPortal({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Portal>) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Portal data-slot="dialog-portal" {...props} />
}

function DialogClose({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Close>) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Close data-slot="dialog-close" {...props} />
}

function DialogOverlay({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Overlay>) {
  return (
    <DialogPrimitive.Overlay
      data-slot="dialog-overlay"
      className={cn(
        "data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=open]:fade-in-0 fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/50",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function DialogContent({
  className,
  children,
  showCloseButton = true,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Content> & {
  showCloseButton?: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <DialogPortal data-slot="dialog-portal">
      <DialogOverlay />

        data-slot="dialog-content"

          className
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        {children}
        {showCloseButton && (
          <DialogPrimitive.Close
            data-slot="dialog-close"

          >
            <XIcon />
            <span className="sr-only">Close</span>
          </DialogPrimitive.Close>
        )}
      </DialogPrimitive.Content>
    </DialogPortal>
  )
}

function DialogHeader({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="dialog-header"
      className={cn("flex flex-col gap-2 text-center sm:text-left", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

      data-slot="dialog-footer"

      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Title>) {

    <DialogPrimitive.Title
      data-slot="dialog-title"
      className={cn("text-lg leading-none font-semibold", className)}
      {...props}

  )
}

  className,

}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Description>) {

function TableFooter({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"tfoot">) {
  return (
    <tfoot
      data-slot="table-footer"
      className={cn(
        "bg-muted/50 border-t font-medium [&>tr]:last:border-b-0",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function TableRow({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"tr">) {
  return (
    <tr
      data-slot="table-row"
      className={cn(
        "hover:bg-muted/50 data-[state=selected]:bg-muted border-b transition-colors",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function TableHead({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"th">) {
  return (
    <th
      data-slot="table-head"
      className={cn(
        "text-foreground h-10 px-2 text-left align-middle font-medium whitespace-nowrap [&:has([role=checkbox])]:pr-0 [&>[role=checkbox]]:translate-y-[2px]",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function TableCell({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"td">) {
  return (
    <td
      data-slot="table-cell"
      className={cn(
        "p-2 align-middle whitespace-nowrap [&:has([role=checkbox])]:pr-0 [&>[role=checkbox]]:translate-y-[2px]",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function TableCaption({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"caption">) {
  return (
    <caption
      data-slot="table-caption"
      className={cn("text-muted-foreground mt-4 text-sm", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export {
  Table,
  TableHeader,
  TableBody,
  TableFooter,
  TableHead,
  TableRow,
  TableCell,
  TableCaption,
}
