===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\ui\toast.tsx =====
"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import * as ToastPrimitives from "@radix-ui/react-toast"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"
import { X } from "lucide-react"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const ToastProvider = ToastPrimitives.Provider

const ToastViewport = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof ToastPrimitives.Viewport>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof ToastPrimitives.Viewport>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <ToastPrimitives.Viewport
    ref={ref}
    className={cn(
      "fixed top-0 z-[100] flex max-h-screen w-full flex-col-reverse p-4 sm:bottom-0 sm:right-0 sm:top-auto sm:flex-col md:max-w-[420px]",
      className
    )}
    {...props}
  />
))
ToastViewport.displayName = ToastPrimitives.Viewport.displayName

const toastVariants = cva(
  "group pointer-events-auto relative flex w-full items-center justify-between space-x-2 overflow-hidden rounded-md border p-4 pr-6 shadow-lg transition-all data-[swipe=cancel]:translate-x-0 data-[swipe=end]:translate-x-[var(--radix-toast-swipe-end-x)] data-[swipe=move]:translate-x-[var(--radix-toast-swipe-move-x)] data-[swipe=move]:transition-none data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[swipe=end]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-80 data-[state=closed]:slide-out-to-right-full data-[state=open]:slide-in-from-top-full data-[state=open]:sm:slide-in-from-bottom-full",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "border bg-background text-foreground",
        destructive:
          "destructive group border-destructive bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
    },
  }
)

const Toast = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof ToastPrimitives.Root>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof ToastPrimitives.Root> &
  VariantProps<typeof toastVariants>
>(({ className, variant, ...props }, ref) => {
  return (
    <ToastPrimitives.Root
      ref={ref}
      className={cn(toastVariants({ variant }), className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
})
Toast.displayName = ToastPrimitives.Root.displayName

const ToastAction = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof ToastPrimitives.Action>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof ToastPrimitives.Action>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <ToastPrimitives.Action
    ref={ref}
    className={cn(
      "inline-flex h-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md border bg-transparent px-3 text-sm font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-secondary focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-ring disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 group-[.destructive]:border-muted/40 group-[.destructive]:hover:border-destructive/30 group-[.destructive]:hover:bg-destructive group-[.destructive]:hover:text-destructive-foreground group-[.destructive]:focus:ring-destructive",
      className
    )}
    {...props}
  />
))
ToastAction.displayName = ToastPrimitives.Action.displayName

const ToastClose = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof ToastPrimitives.Close>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof ToastPrimitives.Close>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <ToastPrimitives.Close
    ref={ref}
    className={cn(
      "absolute right-1 top-1 rounded-md p-1 text-foreground/50 opacity-0 transition-opacity hover:text-foreground focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 group-hover:opacity-100 group-[.destructive]:text-red-300 group-[.destructive]:hover:text-red-50 group-[.destructive]:focus:ring-red-400 group-[.destructive]:focus:ring-offset-red-600",
      className
    )}
    toast-close=""
    {...props}
  >
    <X className="h-4 w-4" />
  </ToastPrimitives.Close>
))
ToastClose.displayName = ToastPrimitives.Close.displayName

const ToastTitle = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof ToastPrimitives.Title>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof ToastPrimitives.Title>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <ToastPrimitives.Title
    ref={ref}
    className={cn("text-sm font-semibold [&+div]:text-xs", className)}
    {...props}
  />
))
ToastTitle.displayName = ToastPrimitives.Title.displayName

const ToastDescription = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof ToastPrimitives.Description>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof ToastPrimitives.Description>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <ToastPrimitives.Description
    ref={ref}
    className={cn("text-sm opacity-90", className)}
    {...props}
  />
))
ToastDescription.displayName = ToastPrimitives.Description.displayName

type ToastProps = React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof Toast>

type ToastActionElement = React.ReactElement<typeof ToastAction>

export {
  type ToastProps,
  type ToastActionElement,
  ToastProvider,
  ToastViewport,
  Toast,
  ToastTitle,
  ToastDescription,
  ToastClose,
  ToastAction,
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\ui\toaster.tsx =====
"use client"

import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast"
import {
  Toast,
  ToastClose,
  ToastDescription,
  ToastProvider,
  ToastTitle,
  ToastViewport,
} from "@/components/ui/toast"

export function Toaster() {
  const { toasts } = useToast()

  return (
    <ToastProvider>
      {toasts.map(function ({ id, title, description, action, ...props }) {
        return (
          <Toast key={id} {...props}>
            <div className="grid gap-1">
              {title && <ToastTitle>{title}</ToastTitle>}
              {description && (
                <ToastDescription>{description}</ToastDescription>
              )}
            </div>
            {action}
            <ToastClose />
          </Toast>
        )
      })}
      <ToastViewport />
    </ToastProvider>
  )
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\users\directory-filters.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { Filter, RotateCcw, Search } from "lucide-react";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import { SectionCard } from "@/components/platform/section-card";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Directory filter bar. A real GET-style navigation: the current       */
/* filters become URL search params, so every view is shareable and    */
/* the SERVER re-queries with the new filters (never client filtering  */
/* of sensitive data).                                                 */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

interface DirectoryFiltersProps {
  roleOptions: { value: string; label: string }[];
  schools: { id: string; nameAr: string }[];
  branches: { id: string; nameAr: string }[];
  showSchoolFilter: boolean;
}

export function DirectoryFilters({
  roleOptions,
  schools,
  branches,
  showSchoolFilter,
}: DirectoryFiltersProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();

  const current = {
    q: searchParams.get("q") ?? "",
    role: searchParams.get("role") ?? "ALL",
    schoolId: searchParams.get("schoolId") ?? "ALL",
    branchId: searchParams.get("branchId") ?? "ALL",
    status: searchParams.get("status") ?? "ALL",
  };

  const [q, setQ] = React.useState(current.q);

  function applyParam(key: string, value: string) {
    const sp = new URLSearchParams(searchParams.toString());
    if (value === "ALL" || value === "") sp.delete(key);
    else sp.set(key, value);
    sp.delete("page"); // any filter change resets pagination
    router.push(`/users${sp.toString() ? `?${sp.toString()}` : ""}`);
  }

  function submitSearch(event: React.FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    applyParam("q", q.trim());
  }

  function resetFilters() {
    setQ("");
    router.push("/users");
  }

  const selectClass = "w-full bg-background";

  return (
    <SectionCard
      title="تصفية النتائج"
      description="البحث والفلاتر تُنفَّذ على الخادم ضمن نطاق صلاحيتك — النتائج دائمًا من قاعدة البيانات."
    >
      <form onSubmit={submitSearch} className="space-y-4" noValidate>
        <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5">
          <div className="space-y-1.5 xl:col-span-2">
            <Label htmlFor="users-q">بحث بالاسم أو البريد</Label>
            <div className="relative">
              <Search
                className="pointer-events-none absolute start-3 top-1/2 size-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground"
                aria-hidden="true"
              />
              <Input
                id="users-q"
                value={q}
                onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="اكتب اسمًا أو بريدًا…"
                className="ps-10"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="users-role">الدور</Label>
            <Select
              value={current.role}
              onValueChange={(value) => applyParam("role", value)}
            >
              <SelectTrigger id="users-role" className={selectClass}>
                <SelectValue placeholder="جميع الأدوار" />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="ALL">جميع الأدوار</SelectItem>
                {roleOptions.map((role) => (
                  <SelectItem key={role.value} value={role.value}>
                    {role.label}
                  </SelectItem>
                ))}
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
          </div>

          {showSchoolFilter && (
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="users-school">المدرسة</Label>
              <Select
                value={current.schoolId}
                onValueChange={(value) => applyParam("schoolId", value)}
              >
                <SelectTrigger id="users-school" className={selectClass}>
                  <SelectValue placeholder="جميع المدارس" />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="ALL">جميع المدارس</SelectItem>
                  {schools.map((school) => (
                    <SelectItem key={school.id} value={school.id}>
                      {school.nameAr}
                    </SelectItem>
                  ))}
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="users-branch">الفرع</Label>
            {branches.length > 0 ? (
              <Select
                value={current.branchId}
                onValueChange={(value) => applyParam("branchId", value)}
              >
                <SelectTrigger id="users-branch" className={selectClass}>
                  <SelectValue placeholder="جميع الفروع" />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="ALL">جميع الفروع</SelectItem>
                  {branches.map((branch) => (
                    <SelectItem key={branch.id} value={branch.id}>
                      {branch.nameAr}
                    </SelectItem>
                  ))}
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            ) : (
              <Input
                id="users-branch"
                value="اختر مدرسة أولًا لعرض فروعها"
                disabled
                aria-describedby="users-branch-hint"
              />
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="users-status">حالة الحساب</Label>
            <Select
              value={current.status}
              onValueChange={(value) => applyParam("status", value)}
            >
              <SelectTrigger id="users-status" className={selectClass}>
                <SelectValue placeholder="جميع الحالات" />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="ALL">جميع الحالات</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="ACTIVE">نشط</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="INACTIVE">موقوف</SelectItem>
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <Button type="submit" size="sm">
            <Filter className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            تطبيق البحث
          </Button>
          <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={resetFilters}>
            <RotateCcw className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            إعادة التعيين
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </SectionCard>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\users\profile-name-form.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { useFormStatus } from "react-dom";
import { Save } from "lucide-react";

import {
  updateOwnProfileAction,
  type UserFormState,
} from "@/app/actions/users";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Self-service display-name edit. This is the ONLY self-mutation: the  */
/* server schema contains no other field, so role / school / branch /   */
/* supervisor / account state cannot be changed by the user.            */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

function SaveButton() {
  const { pending } = useFormStatus();
  return (
    <Button type="submit" size="sm" disabled={pending}>
      <Save className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
      {pending ? "جارٍ الحفظ…" : "حفظ الاسم"}
    </Button>
  );
}

export function ProfileNameForm({ currentName }: { currentName: string }) {
  const [state, formAction] = React.useActionState<UserFormState | null, FormData>(
    updateOwnProfileAction,
    null
  );

  return (
    <form action={formAction} className="space-y-4" noValidate>
      {state?.message && (
        <Alert
          variant={state.ok ? "default" : "destructive"}
          role={state.ok ? "status" : "alert"}
        >
          <AlertDescription>{state.message}</AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      )}
      <div className="max-w-sm space-y-1.5">
        <Label htmlFor="profile-name">الاسم المعروض</Label>
        <Input
          id="profile-name"
          name="name"
          defaultValue={currentName}
          required
          minLength={2}
          maxLength={120}
        />
        {state?.fieldErrors?.name && (
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
            {state.fieldErrors.name}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
      <SaveButton />
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        لا يمكنك تعديل دورك أو مدرستك أو فرعك أو حالة حسابك — هذه البيانات تُدار من
        الإدارة المصرّح لها فقط.
      </p>
    </form>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\users\staff-create-form.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { useFormStatus } from "react-dom";
import { CheckCircle2, MailWarning, UserPlus } from "lucide-react";

import {
  createStaffAccountAction,
  type UserFormState,
} from "@/app/actions/users";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import { SectionCard } from "@/components/platform/section-card";
import { roleLabel } from "@/lib/auth/roles";
import { STAFF_CREATABLE_ROLES } from "@/lib/validation/users";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Staff account creation (school-scoped). The role select offers ONLY  */
/* the whitelist (supervisor / deputy / teacher) — SUPER_ADMIN is not   */
/* an option anywhere in the UI, and the service re-forces this         */
/* server-side. New accounts are NOT pre-verified: a verification email */
/* is sent through the existing Phase 3 flow and the honest emailSent   */
/* state is displayed.                                                  */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

interface StaffCreateFormProps {
  actorRole: string;
  /** Platform schools (SUPER_ADMIN only); empty for office managers. */
  schools: { id: string; nameAr: string; isActive: boolean }[];
  /** Fixed school for office managers. */
  schoolId?: string;
  /** Branch options of the target school (office manager's school). */
  branches: { id: string; nameAr: string }[];
}

function CreateSubmitButton() {
  const { pending } = useFormStatus();
  return (
    <Button type="submit" disabled={pending} size="sm">
      <UserPlus className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
      {pending ? "جارٍ الإنشاء…" : "إنشاء الحساب وإرسال رابط التأكيد"}
    </Button>
  );
}

export function StaffCreateForm({
  actorRole,
  schools,
  schoolId,
  branches,
}: StaffCreateFormProps) {
  const isSuperAdmin = actorRole === "SUPER_ADMIN";
  const [state, formAction] = React.useActionState<UserFormState | null, FormData>(
    createStaffAccountAction,
    null
  );

  return (
    <SectionCard
      title="إنشاء حساب جديد"
      description="للمشرفين التربويين والوكلاء التعليميين والمعلمين — يُنشأ الحساب غير موثّق ويُرسل له رابط تأكيد البريد عبر مسار التحقق المعتمد."
    >
      <form action={formAction} className="space-y-4" noValidate>
        {state?.message && (
          <Alert
            variant={state.ok ? "default" : "destructive"}
            role={state.ok ? "status" : "alert"}
          >
            {state.ok && state.emailSent === true && (
              <CheckCircle2 className="size-4 text-success" aria-hidden="true" />
            )}
            {state.ok && state.emailSent === false && (
              <MailWarning className="size-4 text-warning" aria-hidden="true" />
            )}
            <AlertDescription>{state.message}</AlertDescription>
          </Alert>
        )}

        <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="staff-name">الاسم</Label>
            <Input id="staff-name" name="name" required placeholder="اسم المستخدم" />
            {state?.fieldErrors?.name && (
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
                {state.fieldErrors.name}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="staff-email">البريد الإلكتروني</Label>
            <Input
              id="staff-email"
              name="email"
              type="email"
              dir="ltr"
              required
              placeholder="staff@school.edu"
            />
            {state?.fieldErrors?.email && (
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
                {state.fieldErrors.email}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="staff-password">كلمة المرور الأولية</Label>
            <Input
              id="staff-password"
              name="password"
              type="password"
              dir="ltr"
              required
              placeholder="8 أحرف على الأقل + رقم"
            />
            {state?.fieldErrors?.password && (
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
                {state.fieldErrors.password}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="staff-role">الدور</Label>
            <Select name="role" required>
              <SelectTrigger id="staff-role" className="w-full">
                <SelectValue placeholder="اختر الدور" />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                {STAFF_CREATABLE_ROLES.map((role) => (
                  <SelectItem key={role} value={role}>
                    {roleLabel(role)}
                  </SelectItem>
                ))}
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
            {state?.fieldErrors?.role && (
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
                {state.fieldErrors.role}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          {isSuperAdmin ? (
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="staff-school">المدرسة</Label>
              <Select name="schoolId" required>
                <SelectTrigger id="staff-school" className="w-full">
                  <SelectValue placeholder="اختر المدرسة" />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  {schools.map((school) => (
                    <SelectItem key={school.id} value={school.id}>
                      {school.nameAr}
                      {!school.isActive ? " (موقوفة)" : ""}
                    </SelectItem>
                  ))}
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
              {state?.fieldErrors?.schoolId && (
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
                  {state.fieldErrors.schoolId}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <input type="hidden" name="schoolId" value={schoolId ?? ""} />
          )}

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="staff-branch">الفرع (اختياري)</Label>
            {branches.length > 0 ? (
              <Select name="branchId">
                <SelectTrigger id="staff-branch" className="w-full">
                  <SelectValue placeholder="بدون فرع" />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="none">بدون فرع</SelectItem>
                  {branches.map((branch) => (
                    <SelectItem key={branch.id} value={branch.id}>
                      {branch.nameAr}
                    </SelectItem>
                  ))}
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            ) : (
              <Input
                id="staff-branch"
                value={isSuperAdmin ? "تُعرض فروع المدرسة بعد اختيارها" : "لا توجد فروع بعد"}
                disabled
              />
            )}
            {state?.fieldErrors?.branchId && (
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
                {state.fieldErrors.branchId}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          <CreateSubmitButton />
        </div>
      </form>
    </SectionCard>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\users\user-edit-forms.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { useFormStatus } from "react-dom";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Save, Trash2, UserCheck } from "lucide-react";

import {
  assignTeacherSupervisorAction,
  removeTeacherSupervisorAction,
  setDeputyScopeAction,
  setSupervisorBranchesAction,
  updateUserProfileByAdminAction,
  type UserFormState,
} from "@/app/actions/users";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { RadioGroup, RadioGroupItem } from "@/components/ui/radio-group";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import { branchStageLabel } from "@/lib/validation/schools";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Management forms rendered on the user-details page. Each form is     */
/* presentation only — the service layer re-validates scope, roles and  */
/* relationships on every submit. Forms receive serializable data.      */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

export interface BranchOption {
  id: string;
  nameAr: string;
  stage?: string;
}

function SubmitButton({ label, pendingLabel }: { label: string; pendingLabel: string }) {
  const { pending } = useFormStatus();
  return (
    <Button type="submit" size="sm" disabled={pending}>
      <Save className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
      {pending ? pendingLabel : label}
    </Button>
  );
}

function FormFeedback({ state }: { state: UserFormState | null }) {
  if (!state?.message) return null;
  return (
    <Alert
      variant={state.ok ? "default" : "destructive"}
      role={state.ok ? "status" : "alert"}
    >
      <AlertDescription>{state.message}</AlertDescription>
    </Alert>
  );
}

/* ------------------------- Name + branch form ---------------------- */

export function ProfileEditForm({
  userId,
  name,
  branchId,
  branches,
  targetSchoolName,
}: {
  userId: string;
  name: string;
  branchId: string | null;
  branches: BranchOption[];
  targetSchoolName: string | null;
}) {
  const [state, formAction] = React.useActionState<UserFormState | null, FormData>(
    updateUserProfileByAdminAction,
    null
  );

  return (
    <form action={formAction} className="space-y-4" noValidate>
      <FormFeedback state={state} />
      <input type="hidden" name="userId" value={userId} />

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="edit-name">الاسم</Label>
          <Input id="edit-name" name="name" defaultValue={name} required />
          {state?.fieldErrors?.name && (
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-destructive">
              {state.fieldErrors.name}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="edit-branch">الفرع {targetSchoolName && `— فروع ${targetSchoolName}`}</Label>
          {branches.length > 0 ? (
            <Select name="branchId" defaultValue={branchId ?? "none"}>
              <SelectTrigger id="edit-branch" className="w-full">
                <SelectValue placeholder="بدون فرع" />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="none">بدون فرع</SelectItem>
                {branches.map((branch) => (
                  <SelectItem key={branch.id} value={branch.id}>
                    {branch.nameAr}
                  </SelectItem>
                ))}
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
          ) : (
            <Input
              id="edit-branch"
              disabled
              value={targetSchoolName ? "لا توجد فروع في هذه المدرسة" : "حساب غير تابع لمدرسة"}
            />
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
      <SubmitButton label="حفظ البيانات" pendingLabel="جارٍ الحفظ…" />
    </form>
  );
}

/* --------------------- Supervisor branch scope --------------------- */

export function SupervisorBranchesForm({
  supervisorId,
  assignedBranchIds,
  branches,
}: {
  supervisorId: string;
  assignedBranchIds: string[];
  branches: BranchOption[];
}) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [selected, setSelected] = React.useState<string[]>(assignedBranchIds);
  const [state, setState] = React.useState<UserFormState | null>(null);
  const [pending, setPending] = React.useState(false);

  async function handleSave() {
    setPending(true);
    const result = await setSupervisorBranchesAction(supervisorId, selected);
    setState(result);
    setPending(false);
    router.refresh();
  }

  function toggleBranch(branchId: string) {
    setSelected((prev) =>
      prev.includes(branchId)
        ? prev.filter((id) => id !== branchId)
        : [...prev, branchId]
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <FormFeedback state={state} />
      {branches.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="py-4 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          لا توجد فروع في هذه المدرسة بعد — أنشئ فروعًا أولًا من صفحة المدارس.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2" role="list">
          {branches.map((branch) => (
            <li
              key={branch.id}
              className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-background/60 p-3"
            >
              <Checkbox
                id={`branch-${branch.id}`}
                checked={selected.includes(branch.id)}
                onCheckedChange={() => toggleBranch(branch.id)}
                aria-label={`إسناد فرع ${branch.nameAr}`}
              />
              <Label
                htmlFor={`branch-${branch.id}`}
                className="min-w-0 flex-1 cursor-pointer leading-tight"
              >
                <span className="block truncate text-sm font-bold">{branch.nameAr}</span>
                {branch.stage && (
                  <span className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    {branchStageLabel(branch.stage)}
                  </span>
                )}
              </Label>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      <Button type="button" size="sm" onClick={handleSave} disabled={pending || branches.length === 0}>
        <Save className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
        {pending ? "جارٍ الحفظ…" : "حفظ نطاق الفروع"}
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

/* --------------------- Teacher supervisor form --------------------- */

export function TeacherSupervisorForm({
  teacherId,
  currentSupervisorId,
  supervisors,
}: {
  teacherId: string;
  currentSupervisorId: string | null;
  supervisors: { id: string; name: string }[];
}) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [state, formAction] = React.useActionState<UserFormState | null, FormData>(
    assignTeacherSupervisorAction,
    null
  );
  const [removeState, setRemoveState] = React.useState<UserFormState | null>(null);
  const [removing, setRemoving] = React.useState(false);

  async function handleRemove() {
    setRemoving(true);
    const result = await removeTeacherSupervisorAction(teacherId);
    setRemoveState(result);
    setRemoving(false);
    router.refresh();
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <FormFeedback state={state} />
      {removeState?.message && (
        <Alert variant={removeState.ok ? "default" : "destructive"} role={removeState.ok ? "status" : "alert"}>
          <AlertDescription>{removeState.message}</AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      )}

      {supervisors.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="py-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          لا يوجد مشرفون تربويون نشطون في هذه المدرسة — أنشئ حساب مشرف أولًا ثم أسند له فروعًا.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <form action={formAction} className="space-y-3" noValidate>
          <input type="hidden" name="teacherId" value={teacherId} />
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="teacher-supervisor">المشرف التربوي الحالي</Label>
            <Select name="supervisorId" defaultValue={currentSupervisorId ?? "none"} required>
              <SelectTrigger id="teacher-supervisor" className="w-full">
                <SelectValue placeholder="اختر المشرف" />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="none">بدون مشرف</SelectItem>
                {supervisors.map((supervisor) => (
                  <SelectItem key={supervisor.id} value={supervisor.id}>
                    {supervisor.name}
                  </SelectItem>
                ))}
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <Button type="submit" size="sm">
              <UserCheck className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              حفظ الإسناد
            </Button>
            {currentSupervisorId && (
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                onClick={handleRemove}
                disabled={removing}
              >
                <Trash2 className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                {removing ? "جارٍ الإلغاء…" : "إلغاء الإسناد"}
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        </form>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* -------------------------- Deputy scope --------------------------- */

export function DeputyScopeForm({
  deputyId,
  grants,
  branches,
}: {
  deputyId: string;
  /** branchId === null means a school-wide grant. */
  grants: { id: string; branchId: string | null }[];
  branches: BranchOption[];
}) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const schoolWide = grants.some((grant) => grant.branchId === null);
  const grantedBranchIds = grants
    .map((grant) => grant.branchId)
    .filter((id): id is string => id !== null);

  const [mode, setMode] = React.useState<"school" | "branches">(
    schoolWide ? "school" : "branches"
  );
  const [selected, setSelected] = React.useState<string[]>(grantedBranchIds);
  const [state, setState] = React.useState<UserFormState | null>(null);
  const [pending, setPending] = React.useState(false);

  async function handleSave() {
    setPending(true);
    const result = await setDeputyScopeAction(
      deputyId,
      mode,
      mode === "school" ? [] : selected
    );
    setState(result);
    setPending(false);
    router.refresh();
  }

  function toggleBranch(branchId: string) {
    setSelected((prev) =>
      prev.includes(branchId)
        ? prev.filter((id) => id !== branchId)
        : [...prev, branchId]
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <FormFeedback state={state} />

      <RadioGroup
        value={mode}
        onValueChange={(value) => setMode(value as "school" | "branches")}
        className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2"
      >
        <Label
          htmlFor="deputy-mode-school"
          className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-background/60 p-3 leading-tight"
        >
          <RadioGroupItem id="deputy-mode-school" value="school" className="mt-0.5" />
          <span>
            <span className="block text-sm font-bold">اطلاع على المدرسة كاملة</span>
            <span className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              كل الفروع الحالية والمستقبلية — قراءة فقط
            </span>
          </span>
        </Label>
        <Label
          htmlFor="deputy-mode-branches"
          className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-background/60 p-3 leading-tight"
        >
          <RadioGroupItem id="deputy-mode-branches" value="branches" className="mt-0.5" />
          <span>
            <span className="block text-sm font-bold">فروع محددة</span>
            <span className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              اختر الفروع المصرّح بها أدناه
            </span>
          </span>
        </Label>
      </RadioGroup>

      {mode === "branches" && (
        <ul className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2" role="list">
          {branches.map((branch) => (
            <li
              key={branch.id}
              className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-background/60 p-3"
            >
              <Checkbox
                id={`deputy-branch-${branch.id}`}
                checked={selected.includes(branch.id)}
                onCheckedChange={() => toggleBranch(branch.id)}
                aria-label={`منح اطلاع فرع ${branch.nameAr}`}
              />
              <Label
                htmlFor={`deputy-branch-${branch.id}`}
                className="min-w-0 flex-1 cursor-pointer leading-tight"
              >
                <span className="block truncate text-sm font-bold">{branch.nameAr}</span>
                {branch.stage && (
                  <span className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    {branchStageLabel(branch.stage)}
                  </span>
                )}
              </Label>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}

      <Button
        type="button"
        size="sm"
        onClick={handleSave}
        disabled={pending || (mode === "branches" && branches.length === 0)}
      >
        <Save className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
        {pending ? "جارٍ الحفظ…" : "حفظ نطاق الاطلاع"}
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\components\users\user-status-toggle.tsx =====
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { ShieldOff } from "lucide-react";

import { setUserActiveAction } from "@/app/actions/users";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Account activation switch (management views). Presentation only —   */
/* the server re-validates scope, self-toggle and SUPER_ADMIN rows.    */
/* Deactivation revokes live sessions immediately (tokenVersion).      */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

interface UserStatusToggleProps {
  userId: string;
  userName: string;
  isActive: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  disabledReason?: string;
}

export function UserStatusToggle({
  userId,
  userName,
  isActive,
  disabled,
  disabledReason,
}: UserStatusToggleProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [pending, setPending] = React.useState(false);

  async function handleToggle(checked: boolean) {
    setPending(true);
    const result = await setUserActiveAction(userId, checked);
    setPending(false);
    toast({
      title: result.ok ? "تم تحديث الحالة" : "تعذر تحديث الحالة",
      description: result.message,
      variant: result.ok ? "default" : "destructive",
    });
    router.refresh();
  }

  return (
    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
      <Switch
        checked={isActive}
        disabled={disabled || pending}
        onCheckedChange={handleToggle}
        aria-label={`حالة حساب ${userName}`}
      />
      {disabled && (
        <ShieldOff
          className="size-3.5 text-muted-foreground/60"
          aria-hidden="true"
        />
      )}
      {disabled && disabledReason && (
        <span className="sr-only">{disabledReason}</span>
      )}
    </span>
  );
}

===== D:\DevCore\Teachers-platform\src\app\users\page.tsx =====
import Link from "next/link";
import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, UserRound } from "lucide-react";

import { PageHeader } from "@/components/layout/page-header";
import { DirectoryFilters } from "@/components/users/directory-filters";
import { StaffCreateForm } from "@/components/users/staff-create-form";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/platform/status-badge";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/platform/empty-state";
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from "@/components/ui/table";
import { requireAdminContext } from "@/lib/auth/dev-admin";
import { roleLabel } from "@/lib/auth/roles";
import { listUsersForDirectory } from "@/lib/users/directory";
import { listSchools } from "@/lib/schools/service";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { DIRECTORY_ROLE_FILTER, USERS_PAGE_SIZE } from "@/lib/validation/users";

export const metadata = {
  title: "دليل المستخدمين",
};

/**
 * Phase 4 — user directory. SUPER_ADMIN browses the whole platform
 * (with an optional school filter); DEVELOPMENT_OFFICE_MANAGER is
 * structurally scoped to their own school by the service layer. All
 * rows and counts come from persisted data.
 */
export default async function UsersPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>>;
}) {
  const ctx = await requireAdminContext();

  const params = await searchParams;
  const flat = Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(params).map(([key, value]) => [
      key,
      Array.isArray(value) ? (value[0] ?? "") : (value ?? ""),
    ])
  );

  const result = await listUsersForDirectory(ctx, flat);

  // Filter dropdown data — office managers only ever see their school.
  const schools =
    ctx.role === "SUPER_ADMIN" ? await listSchools() : [];
  const effectiveSchoolId =
    ctx.role === "DEVELOPMENT_OFFICE_MANAGER"
      ? ctx.schoolId
      : (flat.schoolId as string | undefined) || undefined;
  const branchOptions = effectiveSchoolId
    ? await db.branch.findMany({
        where: { schoolId: effectiveSchoolId },
        select: { id: true, nameAr: true },
        orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" },
      })
    : [];

  // Staff-creation dropdown data (compact: platform schools).
  const schoolsForCreate =
    ctx.role === "SUPER_ADMIN"
      ? (await listSchools()).map((school) => ({
          id: school.id,
          nameAr: school.nameAr,
          isActive: school.isActive,
        }))
      : [];

  const roleOptions = DIRECTORY_ROLE_FILTER.map((role) => ({
    value: role,
    label: roleLabel(role),
  }));

  function pageHref(page: number): string {
    const sp = new URLSearchParams(
      Object.entries(flat).filter(([, v]) => v !== "")
    );
    sp.set("page", String(page));
    return `/users?${sp.toString()}`;
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHeader
        title="دليل المستخدمين"
        description={
          ctx.role === "SUPER_ADMIN"
            ? "جميع حسابات المنصة عبر المدارس: الأدوار والحالات والإسنادات — إدارة مديري مكاتب التطوير من صفحة إدارة الحسابات."
            : "حسابات مدرستك: المعلمون والمشرفون والوكلاء — الأدوار والحالات والإسنادات."
        }
        breadcrumbs={[{ label: "دليل المستخدمين" }]}
      />

      <div className="space-y-6">
        <StaffCreateForm
          actorRole={ctx.role}
          schools={schoolsForCreate}
          schoolId={ctx.role === "DEVELOPMENT_OFFICE_MANAGER" ? ctx.schoolId : undefined}
          branches={branchOptions}
        />

        <DirectoryFilters
          roleOptions={roleOptions}
          schools={schools.map((school) => ({ id: school.id, nameAr: school.nameAr }))}
          branches={branchOptions}
          showSchoolFilter={ctx.role === "SUPER_ADMIN"}
        />

        {result.ok ? (
          <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border bg-card">
            {result.data.rows.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState
                icon={UserRound}
                title="لا توجد حسابات مطابقة"
                description="جرّب تعديل كلمات البحث أو الفلاتر — أو أنشئ حسابًا جديدًا من النموذج أعلاه."
                className="rounded-none border-0 bg-transparent"
              />
            ) : (
              <>
                <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                  <Table>
                    <TableHeader>
                      <TableRow>
                        <TableHead>المستخدم</TableHead>
                        <TableHead>الدور</TableHead>
                        <TableHead>المدرسة</TableHead>
                        <TableHead>الفرع</TableHead>
                        <TableHead>الحالة</TableHead>
                        <TableHead>آخر دخول</TableHead>
                        <TableHead aria-label="تفاصيل" />
                      </TableRow>
                    </TableHeader>
                    <TableBody>
                      {result.data.rows.map((user) => (
                        <TableRow key={user.id}>
                          <TableCell>
                            <p className="text-sm font-bold">{user.name}</p>
                            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground" dir="ltr">
                              {user.email}
                            </p>
                          </TableCell>
                          <TableCell className="text-sm">{roleLabel(user.role)}</TableCell>
                          <TableCell className="text-sm">
                            {user.schoolName ?? (
                              <span className="text-muted-foreground">— منصة</span>
                            )}
                          </TableCell>
                          <TableCell className="text-sm">
                            {user.branchName ?? (
                              <span className="text-muted-foreground">— بدون فرع</span>
                            )}
                          </TableCell>
                          <TableCell>
                            <div className="flex flex-col items-start gap-1">
                              <StatusBadge tone={user.isActive ? "success" : "error"}>
                                {user.isActive ? "نشط" : "موقوف"}
                              </StatusBadge>
                              <StatusBadge
                                tone={user.isVerified ? "info" : "warning"}
                                withDot={false}
                              >
                                {user.isVerified ? "بريد موثّق" : "بانتظار التأكيد"}
                              </StatusBadge>
                            </div>
                          </TableCell>
                          <TableCell className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                            {user.lastLoginLabel ?? "لم يسجّل بعد"}
                          </TableCell>
                          <TableCell>
                            <Link
                              href={`/users/${user.id}`}
                              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-brand-600 transition-colors hover:bg-brand-50 hover:text-brand-700"
                            >
                              التفاصيل
                              <ChevronLeft className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                            </Link>
                          </TableCell>
                        </TableRow>
                      ))}
                    </TableBody>
                  </Table>
                </div>

                {/* Pagination — real server-computed pages, params preserved */}
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t px-4 py-3">
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    الإجمالي: {result.data.total} حساب — صفحة {result.data.page} من{" "}
                    {result.data.pageCount} ({USERS_PAGE_SIZE} لكل صفحة)
                  </p>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    {result.data.page > 1 ? (
                      <Link
                        href={pageHref(result.data.page - 1)}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold transition-colors hover:bg-accent"
                        aria-label="الصفحة السابقة"
                      >
                        <ChevronRight className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                        السابقة
                      </Link>
                    ) : (
                      <span
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-muted-foreground/50"
                        aria-disabled="true"
                      >
                        <ChevronRight className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                        السابقة
                      </span>
                    )}
                    {result.data.page < result.data.pageCount ? (
                      <Link
                        href={pageHref(result.data.page + 1)}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold transition-colors hover:bg-accent"
                        aria-label="الصفحة التالية"
                      >
                        التالية
                        <ChevronLeft className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                      </Link>
                    ) : (
                      <span
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-muted-foreground/50"
                        aria-disabled="true"
                      >
                        التالية
                        <ChevronLeft className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div
            role="alert"
            className="rounded-xl border border-destructive/40 bg-destructive/5 p-6 text-center text-sm font-semibold text-destructive"
          >
            {result.message}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
